# M3 App (`@tysiac/app`) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build `apps/app`, the Vite + React 19 PWA for Tysiąc: Home, Lobby and a never-scrolling Game screen with every phase, optimistic card play, event-paced animations, three themes, coach, sounds, and Playwright WebKit e2e that plays full games against bots.

**Architecture:** All networking sits behind one `RoomConnection` interface (emits `ServerMessage`, accepts `ClientMessage`) with two implementations: `SocketConnection` (WebSocket `/ws/:code`, backoff, `hello`) and `LocalConnection` (an in-browser `LocalRoom` that runs `@tysiac/engine` + bots with the same lobby semantics as the Worker). A pure client reducer (`store/client.ts`) turns server messages into a *latest* view and a *shown* view that lags behind while an event queue plays (card flights, ~1 s trick hold, sweep, round-result pause); a thin zustand store pumps that queue on timers. Screens are lazy chunks; the Game screen is one CSS grid in `100dvh` whose sizes come only from CSS `clamp()`/`min()`.

**Tech Stack:** Vite 8, React 19.3, TypeScript 5.9 (strict), zustand 5, wouter 3 (router), motion 14 (`motion/react`, `LazyMotion` + `m`), @fontsource (Fraunces, Young Serif, Inter), vite-plugin-pwa 2 + @vite-pwa/assets-generator 2, Vitest 5 + jsdom + @testing-library/react 16, Playwright 1.64 (WebKit). pnpm 9 workspace, Node 24.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-tysiac-rebuild-design.md` (§1 goals, §7 App is authoritative, §9 coach UI, §10 app tests). Binding wire contract: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-protocol-contract.md`. Engine API: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-09-m1-engine.md` (Task 1 `src/types.ts`; `createGame`, `reduce`, `viewFor`, `eventsFor`, `botAction`, `explainIllegal`, `advise`, `explainEvent`). Visual references: `.superpowers/brainstorm/40901-1791579542/content/visual-direction-v2.html` (themes A/B/C) and `phases-v3.html` (lobby, bidding, hand-out).

## Global Constraints

- **Preconditions (checked in Task 1 Step 1):** `packages/engine` (M1, `@tysiac/engine`) and `packages/protocol` (M2, `@tysiac/protocol`, exports exactly the contract names) exist on the branch. M3 execution starts only after both exist; if either is missing, stop and report BLOCKED — do **not** create or edit those packages.
- **Protocol import rule:** app code imports protocol types only from `apps/app/src/net/protocol.ts`, which type-re-exports `@tysiac/protocol` and defines `PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1` locally (a unit test asserts it equals the package's value). This keeps `zod` out of the client bundle.
- **Engine is read-only:** never modify `packages/engine`. Only use its public exports: types (`Action`, `Advice`, `Card`, `CompletedTrick`, `GameEvent`, `GameState`, `Phase`, `PlayerId`, `PlayerView`, `RoundOutcome`, `RoundSummary`, `RoundView`, `Seat`, `Suit`, `TrickCard`), `RULES`, `SUIT_SYMBOL`, `cardKey`, `cardLabel`, `cardEquals`, `hasMarriage`, `marriagesInHand`, `parseCard`, `parseCards`, `createGame`, `reduce`, `viewFor`, `eventsFor`, `turnOf`, `botAction`, `makeRandom`, `explainIllegal`, `advise`, `explainEvent`.
- Package `apps/app`, name `@tysiac/app`, `"type": "module"`, build output `apps/app/dist` (the M2 Worker serves it with SPA fallback). TypeScript `strict: true`; `tsc --noEmit` passes.
- Exact dependency ranges (checked with `npm view` on 2026-10-09): `react`/`react-dom` `^19.3.0`, `@types/react`/`@types/react-dom` `^19.3.0`, `vite` `^8.3.4`, `@vitejs/plugin-react` `^6.1.2`, `typescript` `^5.9.3` (same major as the engine's `^5.4.0`; TS 7 is not used), `zustand` `^5.0.15`, `wouter` `^3.13.0`, `motion` `^14.1.0`, `vite-plugin-pwa` `^2.0.0`, `workbox-window` `^7.4.1`, `@vite-pwa/assets-generator` `^2.0.0`, `@fontsource/fraunces` `^5.3.0`, `@fontsource/young-serif` `^5.3.0`, `@fontsource/inter` `^5.3.0`, `vitest` `^5.0.3`, `jsdom` `^30.1.2`, `@testing-library/react` `^16.3.3`, `@testing-library/jest-dom` `^7.0.1`, `@testing-library/user-event` `^14.6.7`, `@playwright/test` `^1.64.0`.
- Routes: `/` Home, `/r/:code` room (Lobby or Game), `/learn` placeholder, `/history` placeholder. Lobby, Game and the room route are `React.lazy` chunks. **Initial JS ≤ 120 KB gzip**, enforced by `scripts/check-budget.mjs` in `pnpm build`.
- Game screen: one CSS grid filling `100dvh` (fallback `var(--vvh)` from `visualViewport`), `body` not fixed, no nested scroll, never scrolls; safe-area insets on all sides; landscape (`orientation: landscape` and `max-height: 520px`) = two columns; card size from CSS `clamp()`/`min(vw, dvh)` only — **no JS screen-size hooks**, no `useIsMobile`.
- **Never play a card on tap.** Tap raises/lowers; the button named "Play X♦" commits. Illegal cards are dimmed (`aria-disabled="true"`); tapping one shows `explainIllegal`'s reason.
- Text inputs exist only on Home, the `/r/:code` name gate and Settings opened from Home (never inside the Game screen); every input uses `font-size: 16px` or larger (stops iOS zoom).
- Motion: import from `motion/react`; use `m.*` components only (root `<LazyMotion strict>`); features = `domMax` loaded by dynamic import (layoutId and drag need `domMax`; `domAnimation` lacks layout animations). `<MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">` at the root. No infinite decorative animations; no `backdrop-filter`.
- Themes: CSS variables on `<html data-theme>` with ids `brass` (default, "Brass & Baize"), `wycinanki` ("Wycinanki"), `midnight` ("Midnight"); colors/fonts copied from `visual-direction-v2.html`. Fonts self-hosted via @fontsource (latin + latin-ext subsets only).
- Storage keys: localStorage `tysiac.identity` (`{playerId, secret}`), `tysiac.prefs` (zustand persist); sessionStorage `tysiac.local` (`'1'` or `'fast'`). Every storage access is wrapped so blocked storage falls back to memory.
- Local mode: `?local=1` (sticky for the tab via sessionStorage) or `VITE_LOCAL=1` selects `LocalConnection`; `?local=1&fast=1` also scales bot delays ×0.05 and playback ×0.1 (used by e2e).
- Bots: names "Bot Basia", "Bot Zenek", "Bot Halina", avatar 🤖; move delay 600–900 ms, +1100 ms when the triggering events include `trickWon`, +1500 ms when they include `roundScored` or `threw`. Away → "Let a bot play for NAME" allowed after 30 s (`AWAY_BOT_AFTER_MS = 30_000`).
- UI copy: short friendly English; suits always as symbols ♥♦♣♠ in UI text.
- Fixed accessible names used by tests (do not rename): buttons "Pass", "Bid N", "Play", "Throw in", "Confirm hand-out", "Play X♦" (e.g. "Play A♦"), "Give to NAME", "Take back X♦", "Continue", "Rematch", "Practice vs bots", "3 players", "4 players", "Join", "Resume", "Menu", "Score sheet", "Settings", "Rules", "Close", "Why?", "Add bot", "Start game", "Share link", "Make first dealer", "Let a bot play for NAME"; group "Your hand"; region "Table"; dialogs titled "Round N", "Game over", "Score sheet", "Settings", "Rules", "Why?", "Menu"; input label "Your name"; avatar buttons "Avatar 🦊" etc.; lobby seat buttons "Seat N: NAME" / "Seat N: empty".
- Commands (run from `/Users/filip/Code/tysiac-game`): `pnpm --filter @tysiac/app test` (vitest run), `pnpm --filter @tysiac/app typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @tysiac/app build`, `pnpm --filter @tysiac/app e2e`.
- Commit messages end with the line `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

## Review Focus

1. **Reconnect in the middle of an animation** (a `state` with `events: []` arrives while the queue is playing and a card is pending) — the client must drop the queue and the pending card and show the server's view at once, with no ghost card on the table. Test: Task 7 "a resync state drops the queue and the pending card".
2. **Double-tapping Play** on a slow connection — exactly one `act` is sent and the card cannot be played twice. Test: Task 14 "double-clicking Play sends one act".
3. **Sitting out as the 4-player dealer** (empty hand, `maxBid` 0) — the Game must render, show "You deal and sit out this round." and offer no bid buttons. Test: Task 13 "sitting-out dealer sees a sit-out note and no buttons".
4. **Odd names** (whitespace only, 40 characters, emoji, surrounding spaces) — names are trimmed, inner whitespace collapsed, capped at 16 characters by code point, and blank names cannot be submitted. Test: Task 3 "cleanName trims, collapses and caps by code point".
5. **Opening a link to a full room or a game already in progress** — the room route shows "Can't join" with the reason and a Home button and does not keep reconnecting. Test: Task 16 "fatal join errors stop reconnecting and explain".

```ts
// The ONLY place app code imports protocol types from. Types are re-exported (erased at build time)
// and the version constant is duplicated so the zod schemas in @tysiac/protocol never reach the bundle.
export type {
  ClientMessage,
  CreateRoomRequest,
  CreateRoomResponse,
  ErrorCode,
  HistoryResponse,
  LobbyOp,
  RoomExistsResponse,
  RoomInfo,
  RoomSeat,
  RoomStatus,
  ServerMessage,
} from '@tysiac/protocol';

export const PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1 as const;
```

### Task 2: Themes, base styles, fonts and the viewport height variable

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/src/theme/themes.css`, `apps/app/src/theme/base.css`, `apps/app/src/theme/fonts.ts`, `apps/app/src/theme/theme.ts`, `apps/app/src/theme/viewport.ts`
- Test: `apps/app/src/theme/theme.test.ts`, `apps/app/src/theme/viewport.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/app/src/main.tsx`, `apps/app/index.html`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces:
  - `type ThemeId = 'brass' | 'wycinanki' | 'midnight'`; `THEMES: { id: ThemeId; name: string; themeColor: string; swatch: [string, string, string] }[]`; `isThemeId(x: unknown): x is ThemeId`; `applyTheme(id: ThemeId, doc?: Document): void` (sets `<html data-theme>` and `<meta name="theme-color">`).
  - `THEME_TOKENS: readonly string[]` (every CSS custom property each theme must define).
  - `installViewportVar(win?: Window): () => void` — keeps `--vvh` = visual viewport height in px on `<html>`; after an input loses focus, scrolls back to 0 when the page is not scrollable. Returns an uninstall function.
  - CSS utility classes used by every later task: `.screen`, `.btn`, `.btn--pri`, `.btn--sec`, `.iconbtn`, `.panel`, `.lbl`, `.display`, `.row`, `.chip`, `.chip--muted`, `.avatar`, `.badge`, `.linkbtn`, `.visually-hidden`, `.field`, `.switch`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`apps/app/src/theme/theme.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { THEMES, THEME_TOKENS, applyTheme, isThemeId } from './theme';

const css = readFileSync(fileURLToPath(new URL('./themes.css', import.meta.url)), 'utf8');

function block(id: string): string {
  const start = css.indexOf(`[data-theme='${id}']`);
  expect(start, `theme ${id} block`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
  return css.slice(start, css.indexOf('}', start));
}

describe('themes', () => {
  it('lists the three themes with brass first', () => {
    expect(THEMES.map(t => t.id)).toEqual(['brass', 'wycinanki', 'midnight']);
    expect(THEMES.map(t => t.name)).toEqual(['Brass & Baize', 'Wycinanki', 'Midnight']);
  });

  it('every theme defines every token', () => {
    for (const t of THEMES) {
      const b = block(t.id);
      for (const token of THEME_TOKENS) expect(b, `${t.id} ${token}`).toContain(`${token}:`);
    }
  });

  it('uses the reference colours', () => {
    expect(block('brass')).toContain('#d4af6a');
    expect(block('brass')).toContain('#f8f4ea');
    expect(block('wycinanki')).toContain('#c8312b');
    expect(block('wycinanki')).toContain('#1f2740');
    expect(block('midnight')).toContain('#ff7a45');
    expect(block('midnight')).toContain('#0e1016');
  });

  it('applyTheme sets data-theme and theme-color', () => {
    applyTheme('midnight');
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe('midnight');
    expect(document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]')?.getAttribute('content')).toBe('#0e1016');
    applyTheme('brass');
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe('brass');
  });

  it('isThemeId guards unknown values', () => {
    expect(isThemeId('wycinanki')).toBe(true);
    expect(isThemeId('neon')).toBe(false);
    expect(isThemeId(null)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`apps/app/src/theme/viewport.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { installViewportVar } from './viewport';

describe('installViewportVar', () => {
  it('sets --vvh from visualViewport and follows resizes', () => {
    const listeners: Record<string, () => void> = {};
    const vv = { height: 612.4, addEventListener: (t: string, f: () => void) => { listeners[t] = f; }, removeEventListener: vi.fn() };
    Object.defineProperty(window, 'visualViewport', { value: vv, configurable: true });
    const off = installViewportVar(window);
    expect(document.documentElement.style.getPropertyValue('--vvh')).toBe('612px');
    vv.height = 500;
    listeners.resize();
    expect(document.documentElement.style.getPropertyValue('--vvh')).toBe('500px');
    off();
    expect(vv.removeEventListener).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('falls back to innerHeight without visualViewport', () => {
    Object.defineProperty(window, 'visualViewport', { value: undefined, configurable: true });
    const off = installViewportVar(window);
    expect(document.documentElement.style.getPropertyValue('--vvh')).toBe(`${window.innerHeight}px`);
    off();
  });

  it('scrolls back to the top after an input blurs on a non-scrollable page', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const scrollTo = vi.spyOn(window, 'scrollTo').mockImplementation(() => {});
    Object.defineProperty(window, 'scrollY', { value: 40, configurable: true });
    const off = installViewportVar(window);
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    document.body.append(input);
    input.focus();
    input.blur();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(100);
    expect(scrollTo).toHaveBeenCalledWith(0, 0);
    off();
    input.remove();
    vi.useRealTimers();
    Object.defineProperty(window, 'scrollY', { value: 0, configurable: true });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/theme/theme.ts`**

```ts
export type ThemeId = 'brass' | 'wycinanki' | 'midnight';

export const THEMES: { id: ThemeId; name: string; themeColor: string; swatch: [string, string, string] }[] = [
  { id: 'brass', name: 'Brass & Baize', themeColor: '#0d3528', swatch: ['#0d3528', '#f8f4ea', '#d4af6a'] },
  { id: 'wycinanki', name: 'Wycinanki', themeColor: '#f3ead8', swatch: ['#f3ead8', '#1f2740', '#c8312b'] },
  { id: 'midnight', name: 'Midnight', themeColor: '#0e1016', swatch: ['#0e1016', '#ffffff', '#ff7a45'] },
];

/** Every custom property a theme block in themes.css must define. */
export const THEME_TOKENS = [
  '--bg', '--bg-solid', '--ink', '--ink-dim', '--accent', '--accent-ink', '--accent-soft',
  '--panel', '--panel-border', '--panel-shadow', '--btn-sec-bg', '--btn-sec-border', '--icon-bg', '--icon-border',
  '--avatar-bg', '--avatar-ink', '--avatar-ring', '--turn-ring', '--card-bg', '--card-ink', '--card-red',
  '--card-border', '--card-shadow', '--back-bg', '--back-border', '--slot-border', '--slot-bg', '--chip-muted-bg',
  '--font-display', '--font-card', '--sheet-bg', '--table-felt', '--table-rim', '--danger',
] as const;

export function isThemeId(x: unknown): x is ThemeId {
  return THEMES.some(t => t.id === x);
}

export function applyTheme(id: ThemeId, doc: Document = document): void {
  doc.documentElement.dataset.theme = id;
  let meta = doc.querySelector<HTMLMetaElement>('meta[name="theme-color"]');
  if (!meta) {
    meta = doc.createElement('meta');
    meta.name = 'theme-color';
    doc.head.append(meta);
  }
  meta.content = THEMES.find(t => t.id === id)!.themeColor;
}
```

```css
*,
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `src/theme/viewport.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * Keeps `--vvh` (visual viewport height, px) on <html> as a fallback for browsers without `dvh`,
 * and undoes the scroll offset iOS sometimes leaves after the keyboard closes.
 */
export function installViewportVar(win: Window = window): () => void {
  const root = win.document.documentElement;
  const vv = win.visualViewport ?? null;
  const update = () => {
    const h = vv ? vv.height : win.innerHeight;
    root.style.setProperty('--vvh', `${Math.round(h)}px`);
  };
  const onFocusOut = (e: FocusEvent) => {
    const t = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
    if (!t || !/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(t.tagName)) return;
    win.setTimeout(() => {
      const doc = win.document.documentElement;
      if (doc.scrollHeight <= win.innerHeight + 1 && win.scrollY !== 0) win.scrollTo(0, 0);
    }, 60);
  };
  update();
  vv?.addEventListener('resize', update);
  win.addEventListener('resize', update);
  win.document.addEventListener('focusout', onFocusOut);
  return () => {
    vv?.removeEventListener('resize', update);
    win.removeEventListener('resize', update);
    win.document.removeEventListener('focusout', onFocusOut);
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/store/storage.ts`**

```ts
export interface KV {
  getItem(key: string): string | null;
  setItem(key: string, value: string): void;
  removeItem(key: string): void;
}

function memoryKV(): KV {
  const m = new Map<string, string>();
  return {
    getItem: k => m.get(k) ?? null,
    setItem: (k, v) => void m.set(k, v),
    removeItem: k => void m.delete(k),
  };
}

const memLocal = memoryKV();
const memSession = memoryKV();

function probe(get: () => Storage, fallback: KV): KV {
  try {
    const s = get();
    s.setItem('__probe', '1');
    s.removeItem('__probe');
    return s;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

/** localStorage, or an in-memory stand-in when storage is blocked (private mode, disabled cookies). */
export function safeLocalStorage(): KV {
  return probe(() => window.localStorage, memLocal);
}

export function safeSessionStorage(): KV {
  return probe(() => window.sessionStorage, memSession);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/net/identity.ts`**

```ts
import { safeLocalStorage, type KV } from '../store/storage';

export interface Identity {
  playerId: string;
  secret: string;
}

const KEY = 'tysiac.identity';
const UUID = /^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/;

/** This device's player id and secret (created on first use, kept in localStorage). */
export function getIdentity(store: KV = safeLocalStorage()): Identity {
  try {
    const raw = JSON.parse(store.getItem(KEY) ?? 'null') as Partial<Identity> | null;
    if (raw && typeof raw.playerId === 'string' && UUID.test(raw.playerId) && typeof raw.secret === 'string' && UUID.test(raw.secret)) {
      return { playerId: raw.playerId, secret: raw.secret };
    }
  } catch {
    // fall through and create a new identity
  }
  const id: Identity = { playerId: crypto.randomUUID(), secret: crypto.randomUUID() };
  store.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(id));
  return id;
}
```

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { createJSONStorage, persist } from 'zustand/middleware';
import { applyTheme, isThemeId, type ThemeId } from '../theme/theme';
import { safeLocalStorage } from './storage';

export type Speed = 'normal' | 'fast';
export interface LastRoom {
  code: string;
  practice: boolean;
  at: number;
}
export interface Prefs {
  name: string;
  avatar: string;
  theme: ThemeId;
  sound: boolean;
  /** null = automatic (on in practice and for the first 3 games). */
  coach: boolean | null;
  speed: Speed;
  gamesStarted: number;
  countedRoom: string | null;
  lastRoom: LastRoom | null;
}
interface PrefsActions {
  setProfile(name: string, avatar: string): void;
  setTheme(id: ThemeId): void;
  setSound(on: boolean): void;
  setCoach(on: boolean): void;
  setSpeed(speed: Speed): void;
  setLastRoom(room: LastRoom | null): void;
  noteGameStarted(code: string): void;
}

export const AVATARS = ['🦊', '🐻', '🦉', '🐺', '🐸', '🐼', '🦁', '🐯', '🐨', '🐙', '🦄', '🐝'] as const;
export const NAME_MAX = 16;

/** Trims, collapses whitespace and caps at NAME_MAX code points (emoji-safe). */
export function cleanName(raw: string): string {
  return [...raw.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()].slice(0, NAME_MAX).join('').trim();
}

export function coachEnabled(p: Pick<Prefs, 'coach' | 'gamesStarted'>, practice: boolean): boolean {
  return p.coach ?? (practice || p.gamesStarted < 3);
}

export const usePrefs = create<Prefs & PrefsActions>()(
  persist(
    set => ({
      name: '',
      avatar: AVATARS[0],
      theme: 'brass',
      sound: true,
      coach: null,
      speed: 'normal',
      gamesStarted: 0,
      countedRoom: null,
      lastRoom: null,
      setProfile: (name, avatar) => set({ name: cleanName(name), avatar }),
      setTheme: id => {
        applyTheme(id);
        set({ theme: id });
      },
      setSound: on => set({ sound: on }),
      setCoach: on => set({ coach: on }),
      setSpeed: speed => set({ speed }),
      setLastRoom: room => set({ lastRoom: room }),
      noteGameStarted: code =>
        set(s => (s.countedRoom === code ? s : { gamesStarted: s.gamesStarted + 1, countedRoom: code })),
    }),
    {
      name: 'tysiac.prefs',
      version: 1,
      storage: createJSONStorage(() => safeLocalStorage()),
      partialize: s => ({
        name: s.name, avatar: s.avatar, theme: s.theme, sound: s.sound, coach: s.coach, speed: s.speed,
        gamesStarted: s.gamesStarted, countedRoom: s.countedRoom, lastRoom: s.lastRoom,
      }),
      onRehydrateStorage: () => state => {
        if (state && isThemeId(state.theme)) applyTheme(state.theme);
      },
    },
  ),
);
```

### Task 4: `RoomConnection` contract and the in-browser `LocalRoom`

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/src/net/connection.ts`, `apps/app/src/net/localRoom.ts`
- Test: `apps/app/src/net/localRoom.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: protocol types + `PROTOCOL_VERSION` (Task 1); engine `createGame`, `reduce`, `viewFor`, `eventsFor`, `turnOf`, `botAction`, `makeRandom`.
- Produces:
  - `connection.ts`: `type HelloMessage = Extract<ClientMessage, { t: 'hello' }>`; `type ConnStatus = 'connecting' | 'open' | 'reconnecting' | 'closed'`; `interface RoomConnection { readonly code: string; readonly status: ConnStatus; send(msg: ClientMessage): boolean; onMessage(fn: (m: ServerMessage) => void): () => void; onStatus(fn: (s: ConnStatus) => void): () => void; close(): void }`; `interface RoomService { readonly kind: 'local' | 'remote'; createRoom(req: CreateRoomRequest): Promise<CreateRoomResponse>; roomExists(code: string): Promise<RoomExistsResponse>; connect(code: string, hello: () => HelloMessage): RoomConnection }`; `class Emitter<T> { on(fn): () => void; emit(v: T): void }`; `CODE_ALPHABET`; `normalizeCode(raw: string): string | null`; `randomCode(): string`; `AWAY_BOT_AFTER_MS = 30_000`; `FATAL_ERRORS: ReadonlySet<string>` (`roomFull`, `badSecret`, `gameInProgress`, `versionMismatch`, `notInRoom`).
  - `localRoom.ts`: `interface Clock { now(): number; setTimeout(fn: () => void, ms: number): () => void }`; `realClock`; `BOT_NAMES`; `BOT_AVATAR = '🤖'`; `interface LocalRoomOptions { code; maxPlayers: 3 | 4; host: { playerId; name; avatar }; seed: string; clock?: Clock; botDelayScale?: number }`; `type Deliver = (m: ServerMessage) => void`; `interface LocalSocket { send(m: ClientMessage): void; close(): void }`; `class LocalRoom { readonly code; open(deliver: Deliver, onClosed?: () => void): LocalSocket; exists(): RoomExistsResponse; snapshot(): RoomInfo }`. Delivery from `LocalRoom` is synchronous; `LocalConnection` (Task 6) adds the async hop.
  - Behaviour mirrors the protocol contract: first message must be `hello` (else `error badMessage` + close); `welcome` then `state` (events `[]`) if a game exists; `room` broadcast on every lobby/seat/connection change; `act` → `state` to every connected human (personalised view + `eventsFor`) then `ack`, or `reject {id, code, message}`; host-only `addBot`/`removeBot`/`setFirstDealer`/`start`/`rematch` (`error notHost`); `swapSeats` by anyone; `leave` frees a lobby seat; `botFor` after 30 s away; bots move on the clock.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** — `apps/app/src/net/localRoom.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { botAction, type PlayerView } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { PROTOCOL_VERSION, type ClientMessage, type RoomInfo, type ServerMessage } from './protocol';
import { LocalRoom, type Clock } from './localRoom';
import { AWAY_BOT_AFTER_MS, normalizeCode } from './connection';

class FakeClock implements Clock {
  t = 1_000_000;
  private seq = 0;
  private timers: { at: number; id: number; fn: () => void }[] = [];
  now() {
    return this.t;
  }
  setTimeout(fn: () => void, ms: number) {
    const id = ++this.seq;
    this.timers.push({ at: this.t + ms, id, fn });
    return () => {
      this.timers = this.timers.filter(x => x.id !== id);
    };
  }
  advance(ms: number) {
    const end = this.t + ms;
    for (;;) {
      this.timers.sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at || a.id - b.id);
      const next = this.timers[0];
      if (!next || next.at > end) break;
      this.timers.shift();
      this.t = next.at;
      next.fn();
    }
    this.t = end;
  }
}

interface TestClient {
  id: string;
  msgs: ServerMessage[];
  closed: boolean;
  send(m: ClientMessage): void;
  close(): void;
  last<T extends ServerMessage['t']>(t: T): Extract<ServerMessage, { t: T }> | undefined;
  view(): PlayerView | null;
  room(): RoomInfo | null;
}

function client(room: LocalRoom, id: string, opts: { secret?: string; name?: string; coach?: boolean; skipHello?: boolean } = {}): TestClient {
  const msgs: ServerMessage[] = [];
  const c = { id, msgs, closed: false } as TestClient;
  const sock = room.open(m => msgs.push(m), () => {
    c.closed = true;
  });
  c.send = m => sock.send(m);
  c.close = () => sock.close();
  c.last = t => [...msgs].reverse().find(m => m.t === t) as never;
  c.view = () => c.last('state')?.view ?? null;
  c.room = () => {
    const m = [...msgs].reverse().find(x => x.t === 'room' || x.t === 'welcome');
    return m && (m.t === 'room' || m.t === 'welcome') ? m.room : null;
  };
  if (!opts.skipHello) {
    sock.send({
      t: 'hello', v: PROTOCOL_VERSION, playerId: id, secret: opts.secret ?? `s-${id}`,
      name: opts.name ?? id.toUpperCase(), avatar: '🦊', coach: opts.coach ?? false,
    });
  }
  return c;
}

function setup(maxPlayers: 3 | 4 = 3) {
  const clock = new FakeClock();
  const room = new LocalRoom({ code: 'ABCDEF', maxPlayers, host: { playerId: 'h', name: 'Host', avatar: '🦊' }, seed: 'seed-1', clock });
  return { clock, room };
}

const lobby = (c: TestClient, op: Extract<ClientMessage, { t: 'lobby' }>['op']) => c.send({ t: 'lobby', op });

/** Humans act with the bot heuristic when it's their turn; otherwise time passes so bots move. */
function drive(clock: FakeClock, clients: TestClient[], until: () => boolean, maxSteps = 30_000) {
  for (let i = 0; i < maxSteps && !until(); i++) {
    const c = clients.find(x => {
      const v = x.view();
      return !x.closed && v && v.turn === x.id && !v.paused && v.legal.some(a => a.type !== 'pause' && a.type !== 'resume');
    });
    if (c) c.send({ t: 'act', id: `a${i}`, action: botAction(c.view()!, () => 0.5) });
    else clock.advance(1000);
  }
  if (!until()) throw new Error('drive: condition not reached');
}

describe('LocalRoom lobby', () => {
  it('welcomes the host into seat 0 and broadcasts the room', () => {
    const { room } = setup();
    const h = client(room, 'h');
    const w = h.last('welcome')!;
    expect(w.you).toBe('h');
    expect(w.room.seats[0]).toMatchObject({ id: 'h', name: 'H', connected: true, isBot: false });
    expect(w.room.seats.slice(1)).toEqual([null, null]);
    expect(room.exists()).toEqual({ exists: true, status: 'lobby', maxPlayers: 3, seatsTaken: 1 });
  });

  it('seats joiners in order and rejects a full room', () => {
    const { room } = setup();
    client(room, 'h');
    const a = client(room, 'a');
    client(room, 'b');
    expect(a.room()!.seats.map(s => s?.id)).toEqual(['h', 'a', 'b']);
    const late = client(room, 'late');
    expect(late.last('error')).toMatchObject({ code: 'roomFull' });
    expect(late.closed).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects a wrong secret for a known player', () => {
    const { room } = setup();
    client(room, 'h');
    const thief = client(room, 'h', { secret: 'nope' });
    expect(thief.last('error')).toMatchObject({ code: 'badSecret' });
    expect(thief.closed).toBe(true);
  });

  it('requires hello first', () => {
    const { room } = setup();
    const c = client(room, 'x', { skipHello: true });
    c.send({ t: 'ping' });
    expect(c.last('error')).toMatchObject({ code: 'badMessage' });
    expect(c.closed).toBe(true);
  });

  it('only the host manages bots, first dealer and start; anyone swaps seats', () => {
    const { room } = setup();
    const h = client(room, 'h');
    const a = client(room, 'a');
    lobby(a, { op: 'addBot' });
    expect(a.last('error')).toMatchObject({ code: 'notHost' });
    lobby(h, { op: 'addBot' });
    expect(h.room()!.seats[2]).toMatchObject({ name: 'Bot Basia', avatar: '🤖', isBot: true, connected: true });
    lobby(a, { op: 'swapSeats', a: 0, b: 2 });
    expect(h.room()!.seats.map(s => s?.name)).toEqual(['Bot Basia', 'A', 'H']);
    lobby(a, { op: 'setFirstDealer', seat: 1 });
    expect(a.last('error')).toMatchObject({ code: 'notHost' });
    lobby(h, { op: 'setFirstDealer', seat: 1 });
    expect(h.room()!.firstDealer).toBe(1);
    const botId = h.room()!.seats[0]!.id;
    lobby(h, { op: 'removeBot', playerId: botId });
    expect(h.room()!.seats[0]).toBeNull();
    lobby(h, { op: 'start' });
    expect(h.last('error')).toMatchObject({ code: 'badMessage' });
    expect(h.room()!.status).toBe('lobby');
  });

  it('leave frees a lobby seat', () => {
    const { room } = setup();
    const h = client(room, 'h');
    const a = client(room, 'a');
    a.send({ t: 'leave' });
    expect(h.room()!.seats[1]).toBeNull();
  });

  it('answers ping with pong', () => {
    const { room } = setup();
    const h = client(room, 'h');
    h.send({ t: 'ping' });
    expect(h.msgs.at(-1)).toEqual({ t: 'pong' });
  });
});

describe('LocalRoom game', () => {
  function started() {
    const s = setup();
    const h = client(s.room, 'h', { coach: true });
    lobby(h, { op: 'addBot' });
    lobby(h, { op: 'addBot' });
    lobby(h, { op: 'start' });
    return { ...s, h };
  }

  it('start deals and sends each human a personal view', () => {
    const { h } = started();
    expect(h.room()!.status).toBe('playing');
    const st = h.last('state')!;
    expect(st.events).toEqual([]);
    expect(st.view.me).toBe('h');
    expect(st.view.round!.hand).toHaveLength(7);
    expect(st.view.seats.map(s => s.name)).toEqual(['H', 'Bot Basia', 'Bot Zenek']);
    expect(h.room()!.seats[0]!.coach).toBe(true);
  });

  it('plays a whole game with bots and finishes the room', () => {
    const { clock, h } = started();
    drive(clock, [h], () => h.room()!.status === 'finished');
    expect(h.msgs.some(m => m.t === 'reject')).toBe(false);
    const seqs = h.msgs.flatMap(m => (m.t === 'state' ? [m.seq] : []));
    for (let i = 1; i < seqs.length; i++) expect(seqs[i]).toBeGreaterThan(seqs[i - 1]);
    expect(h.view()!.phase).toBe('gameEnd');
    expect(h.view()!.winner).not.toBeNull();
  }, 60_000);

  it('acks accepted actions and rejects illegal ones with the rule message', () => {
    const { clock, h } = started();
    drive(clock, [], () => h.view()!.turn === 'h');
    h.send({ t: 'act', id: 'r1', action: { type: 'resume' } });
    expect(h.last('reject')).toMatchObject({ id: 'r1', code: 'illegalAction' });
    const legal = h.view()!.legal.find(a => a.type !== 'pause')!;
    h.send({ t: 'act', id: 'ok1', action: legal });
    expect(h.msgs.at(-1)).toEqual({ t: 'ack', id: 'ok1' });
  });

  it('pause stops bots until anyone resumes', () => {
    const { clock, h } = started();
    drive(clock, [h], () => h.view()!.turn !== 'h' && h.view()!.phase === 'tricks');
    h.send({ t: 'act', id: 'p', action: { type: 'pause' } });
    const seq = h.view()!.seq;
    clock.advance(20_000);
    expect(h.view()!.seq).toBe(seq);
    h.send({ t: 'act', id: 'r', action: { type: 'resume' } });
    clock.advance(5_000);
    expect(h.view()!.seq).toBeGreaterThan(seq + 1);
  });

  it('rematch is host-only and rotates the first dealer', () => {
    const { clock, h } = started();
    drive(clock, [h], () => h.room()!.status === 'finished');
    h.send({ t: 'rematch' });
    expect(h.room()!.status).toBe('playing');
    expect(h.room()!.firstDealer).toBe(1);
    expect(h.last('state')!.view.rounds).toEqual([]);
  }, 60_000);

  it('rejects strangers once the game is running', () => {
    const { room } = started();
    const x = client(room, 'x');
    expect(x.last('error')).toMatchObject({ code: 'gameInProgress' });
  });
});

describe('LocalRoom away handling', () => {
  it('marks a dropped player away, allows botFor after 30 s, and returns control on reconnect', () => {
    const { clock, room } = setup();
    const h = client(room, 'h');
    let a = client(room, 'a');
    lobby(h, { op: 'addBot' });
    lobby(h, { op: 'start' });
    a.close();
    const seatA = () => h.room()!.seats.find(s => s?.id === 'a')!;
    expect(seatA()).toMatchObject({ connected: false, awaySince: clock.now() });
    h.send({ t: 'botFor', playerId: 'a' });
    expect(h.last('error')).toMatchObject({ code: 'badMessage' });
    clock.advance(AWAY_BOT_AFTER_MS);
    h.send({ t: 'botFor', playerId: 'a' });
    expect(seatA().botFor).toBe(true);
    drive(clock, [h], () => h.view()!.phase === 'tricks' && h.view()!.round!.trick.cards.some(t => t.p === 'a'));
    a = client(room, 'a');
    expect(seatA()).toMatchObject({ connected: true, awaySince: null, botFor: false });
    expect(a.last('state')!.events).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('normalizeCode', () => {
  it('accepts 6 characters from the room alphabet, any case', () => {
    expect(normalizeCode(' k7qx2m ')).toBe('K7QX2M');
    expect(normalizeCode('K7QX2')).toBeNull();
    expect(normalizeCode('K7QX2O')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/net/connection.ts`**

```ts
import type {
  ClientMessage, CreateRoomRequest, CreateRoomResponse, RoomExistsResponse, ServerMessage,
} from './protocol';

export type HelloMessage = Extract<ClientMessage, { t: 'hello' }>;
export type ConnStatus = 'connecting' | 'open' | 'reconnecting' | 'closed';

/** One live link to a room. SocketConnection talks to the Worker; LocalConnection to an in-browser LocalRoom. */
export interface RoomConnection {
  readonly code: string;
  readonly status: ConnStatus;
  /** Returns false (and drops the message) when the link is not open. */
  send(msg: ClientMessage): boolean;
  onMessage(fn: (msg: ServerMessage) => void): () => void;
  onStatus(fn: (status: ConnStatus) => void): () => void;
  close(): void;
}

export interface RoomService {
  readonly kind: 'local' | 'remote';
  createRoom(req: CreateRoomRequest): Promise<CreateRoomResponse>;
  roomExists(code: string): Promise<RoomExistsResponse>;
  connect(code: string, hello: () => HelloMessage): RoomConnection;
}

export class Emitter<T> {
  private readonly fns = new Set<(value: T) => void>();
  on(fn: (value: T) => void): () => void {
    this.fns.add(fn);
    return () => {
      this.fns.delete(fn);
    };
  }
  emit(value: T): void {
    for (const fn of [...this.fns]) fn(value);
  }
}

export const CODE_ALPHABET = 'ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
const CODE_RE = new RegExp(`^[${CODE_ALPHABET}]{6}$`);

export function normalizeCode(raw: string): string | null {
  const code = raw.trim().toUpperCase();
  return CODE_RE.test(code) ? code : null;
}

export function randomCode(): string {
  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(6));
  return [...bytes].map(b => CODE_ALPHABET[b % CODE_ALPHABET.length]).join('');
}

/** A disconnected player's seat may be handed to a bot after this long. */
export const AWAY_BOT_AFTER_MS = 30_000;

/** Errors after which reconnecting cannot help. */
export const FATAL_ERRORS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['roomFull', 'badSecret', 'gameInProgress', 'versionMismatch', 'notInRoom']);
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/net/localRoom.ts`**

```ts
import {
  botAction, createGame, eventsFor, makeRandom, reduce, turnOf, viewFor,
  type Action, type GameEvent, type GameState, type PlayerId,
} from '@tysiac/engine';
import {
  PROTOCOL_VERSION,
  type ClientMessage, type ErrorCode, type LobbyOp, type RoomExistsResponse, type RoomInfo, type RoomSeat, type ServerMessage,
} from './protocol';
import { AWAY_BOT_AFTER_MS, type HelloMessage } from './connection';

export interface Clock {
  now(): number;
  setTimeout(fn: () => void, ms: number): () => void;
}

export const realClock: Clock = {
  now: () => Date.now(),
  setTimeout: (fn, ms) => {
    const t = setTimeout(fn, ms);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  },
};

export const BOT_NAMES = ['Bot Basia', 'Bot Zenek', 'Bot Halina'] as const;
export const BOT_AVATAR = '🤖';

export interface LocalRoomOptions {
  code: string;
  maxPlayers: 3 | 4;
  host: { playerId: PlayerId; name: string; avatar: string };
  seed: string;
  clock?: Clock;
  /** Multiplies bot thinking time (e2e uses 0.05). */
  botDelayScale?: number;
}

export type Deliver = (msg: ServerMessage) => void;
export interface LocalSocket {
  send(msg: ClientMessage): void;
  close(): void;
}

interface Sock {
  pid: PlayerId | null;
  open: boolean;
  deliver: Deliver;
  onClosed: () => void;
}

const human = (id: PlayerId, name: string, avatar: string, coach: boolean): RoomSeat => ({
  id, name, avatar, isBot: false, connected: true, awaySince: null, botFor: false, coach,
});

/** An in-browser room with the same lobby semantics as the Worker's Room Durable Object. */
export class LocalRoom {
  readonly code: string;
  private readonly info: RoomInfo;
  private readonly secrets = new Map<PlayerId, string>();
  private readonly socks = new Map<PlayerId, Sock>();
  private state: GameState | null = null;
  private readonly seed: string;
  private games = 0;
  private botCount = 0;
  private readonly clock: Clock;
  private readonly botScale: number;
  private readonly rng: () => number;
  private cancelBot: (() => void) | null = null;

  constructor(o: LocalRoomOptions) {
    this.code = o.code;
    this.seed = o.seed;
    this.clock = o.clock ?? realClock;
    this.botScale = o.botDelayScale ?? 1;
    this.rng = makeRandom(`${o.seed}:bots`);
    const seats: (RoomSeat | null)[] = Array.from({ length: o.maxPlayers }, () => null);
    seats[0] = { ...human(o.host.playerId, o.host.name, o.host.avatar, false), connected: false };
    this.info = { code: o.code, status: 'lobby', maxPlayers: o.maxPlayers, hostId: o.host.playerId, seats, firstDealer: 0 };
  }

  exists(): RoomExistsResponse {
    return { exists: true, status: this.info.status, maxPlayers: this.info.maxPlayers, seatsTaken: this.info.seats.filter(Boolean).length };
  }

  snapshot(): RoomInfo {
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.info)) as RoomInfo;
  }

  open(deliver: Deliver, onClosed: () => void = () => {}): LocalSocket {
    const sock: Sock = { pid: null, open: true, deliver, onClosed };
    return { send: msg => this.receive(sock, msg), close: () => this.drop(sock) };
  }

  // ---------- connection ----------

  private receive(sock: Sock, m: ClientMessage): void {
    if (!sock.open) return;
    if (m.t === 'hello') return this.hello(sock, m);
    if (!sock.pid) return this.fatal(sock, 'badMessage', 'Say hello first');
    switch (m.t) {
      case 'act':
        return this.act(sock, m.id, m.action);
      case 'lobby':
        return this.lobby(sock, m.op);
      case 'botFor':
        return this.botFor(sock, m.playerId);
      case 'setCoach': {
        const seat = this.seatOf(sock.pid);
        if (seat) seat.coach = m.coach;
        return this.broadcastRoom();
      }
      case 'rematch':
        return this.rematch(sock);
      case 'leave':
        return this.leave(sock);
      case 'ping':
        return sock.deliver({ t: 'pong' });
    }
  }

  private hello(sock: Sock, m: HelloMessage): void {
    if (m.v !== PROTOCOL_VERSION) return this.fatal(sock, 'versionMismatch', 'Please reload the app');
    const known = this.secrets.get(m.playerId);
    if (known !== undefined && known !== m.secret) return this.fatal(sock, 'badSecret', 'That seat belongs to another device');
    let seat = this.seatOf(m.playerId);
    if (!seat) {
      if (this.info.status !== 'lobby') return this.fatal(sock, 'gameInProgress', 'This game has already started');
      const i = this.info.seats.indexOf(null);
      if (i < 0) return this.fatal(sock, 'roomFull', 'This room is full');
      seat = human(m.playerId, m.name, m.avatar, m.coach);
      this.info.seats[i] = seat;
    }
    this.secrets.set(m.playerId, m.secret);
    const prev = this.socks.get(m.playerId);
    if (prev && prev !== sock) {
      prev.open = false;
      prev.onClosed();
    }
    sock.pid = m.playerId;
    this.socks.set(m.playerId, sock);
    seat.connected = true;
    seat.awaySince = null;
    seat.coach = m.coach;
    if (this.info.status === 'lobby') {
      seat.name = m.name;
      seat.avatar = m.avatar;
    }
    const hadBot = seat.botFor;
    seat.botFor = false;
    this.setEngineBotFor(m.playerId, false);
    sock.deliver({ t: 'welcome', you: m.playerId, room: this.snapshot(), serverTime: this.clock.now() });
    if (this.state) sock.deliver({ t: 'state', seq: this.state.seq, view: viewFor(this.state, m.playerId), events: [] });
    this.broadcastRoom();
    if (hadBot) this.scheduleBot([]);
  }

  private drop(sock: Sock): void {
    if (!sock.open) return;
    sock.open = false;
    const pid = sock.pid;
    if (!pid || this.socks.get(pid) !== sock) return;
    this.socks.delete(pid);
    const seat = this.seatOf(pid);
    if (seat) {
      seat.connected = false;
      if (this.info.status !== 'lobby') seat.awaySince = this.clock.now();
    }
    this.broadcastRoom();
  }

  private fatal(sock: Sock, code: ErrorCode, message: string): void {
    sock.deliver({ t: 'error', code, message });
    if (sock.pid && this.socks.get(sock.pid) === sock) this.drop(sock);
    sock.open = false;
    sock.onClosed();
  }

  private err(sock: Sock, code: ErrorCode, message: string): void {
    sock.deliver({ t: 'error', code, message });
  }

  // ---------- lobby ----------

  private lobby(sock: Sock, op: LobbyOp): void {
    if (this.info.status !== 'lobby') return this.err(sock, 'gameInProgress', 'The game has started');
    const isHost = sock.pid === this.info.hostId;
    const seats = this.info.seats;
    const valid = (i: unknown): i is number => Number.isInteger(i) && (i as number) >= 0 && (i as number) < seats.length;
    switch (op.op) {
      case 'addBot': {
        if (!isHost) return this.err(sock, 'notHost', 'Only the host can add bots');
        const i = seats.indexOf(null);
        if (i < 0) return this.err(sock, 'roomFull', 'No empty seat');
        const used = new Set(seats.map(s => s?.name));
        const name = BOT_NAMES.find(n => !used.has(n)) ?? `Bot ${i + 1}`;
        seats[i] = { id: `bot-${++this.botCount}`, name, avatar: BOT_AVATAR, isBot: true, connected: true, awaySince: null, botFor: false, coach: false };
        break;
      }
      case 'removeBot': {
        if (!isHost) return this.err(sock, 'notHost', 'Only the host can remove bots');
        const i = seats.findIndex(s => s?.id === op.playerId && s.isBot);
        if (i < 0) return this.err(sock, 'badMessage', 'No such bot');
        seats[i] = null;
        break;
      }
      case 'swapSeats': {
        if (!valid(op.a) || !valid(op.b)) return this.err(sock, 'badMessage', 'Bad seat');
        [seats[op.a], seats[op.b]] = [seats[op.b], seats[op.a]];
        break;
      }
      case 'setFirstDealer': {
        if (!isHost) return this.err(sock, 'notHost', 'Only the host picks the first dealer');
        if (!valid(op.seat)) return this.err(sock, 'badMessage', 'Bad seat');
        this.info.firstDealer = op.seat;
        break;
      }
      case 'start': {
        if (!isHost) return this.err(sock, 'notHost', 'Only the host can start');
        if (seats.some(s => s === null)) return this.err(sock, 'badMessage', 'Fill every seat first');
        return this.startGame();
      }
    }
    this.broadcastRoom();
  }

  private leave(sock: Sock): void {
    const pid = sock.pid!;
    if (this.info.status !== 'lobby') return this.err(sock, 'gameInProgress', 'You can only leave from the lobby');
    const i = this.info.seats.findIndex(s => s?.id === pid);
    if (i >= 0) this.info.seats[i] = null;
    this.secrets.delete(pid);
    this.socks.delete(pid);
    sock.open = false;
    if (pid === this.info.hostId) {
      const next = this.info.seats.find(s => s && !s.isBot);
      if (next) this.info.hostId = next.id;
    }
    this.broadcastRoom();
  }

  private rematch(sock: Sock): void {
    if (sock.pid !== this.info.hostId) return this.err(sock, 'notHost', 'Only the host can start a rematch');
    if (this.info.status !== 'finished') return this.err(sock, 'badMessage', 'The game is not over');
    this.info.firstDealer = (this.info.firstDealer + 1) % this.info.maxPlayers;
    this.startGame();
  }

  private botFor(sock: Sock, target: PlayerId): void {
    const seat = this.seatOf(target);
    if (!seat || seat.isBot || this.info.status !== 'playing') return this.err(sock, 'badMessage', 'Nobody to replace');
    if (seat.connected || seat.awaySince === null || this.clock.now() - seat.awaySince < AWAY_BOT_AFTER_MS) {
      return this.err(sock, 'badMessage', 'Wait 30 s before letting a bot play');
    }
    seat.botFor = true;
    this.setEngineBotFor(target, true);
    this.broadcastRoom();
    this.scheduleBot([]);
  }

  // ---------- game ----------

  private startGame(): void {
    const seats = this.info.seats as RoomSeat[];
    this.state = createGame({
      seed: `${this.seed}:${this.games++}`,
      seats: seats.map(s => ({ id: s.id, name: s.name, avatar: s.avatar, isBot: s.isBot })),
      maxPlayers: this.info.maxPlayers,
      firstDealer: this.info.firstDealer,
    });
    for (const s of seats) if (s.botFor) this.setEngineBotFor(s.id, true);
    this.info.status = 'playing';
    this.broadcastRoom();
    this.broadcastState([]);
    this.scheduleBot([]);
  }

  private act(sock: Sock, id: string, action: Action): void {
    if (!this.state || this.info.status !== 'playing') {
      return sock.deliver({ t: 'reject', id, code: 'notStarted', message: 'The game has not started' });
    }
    const r = reduce(this.state, sock.pid!, action, this.clock.now());
    if (!r.ok) return sock.deliver({ t: 'reject', id, code: r.error.code, message: r.error.message });
    this.commit(r.state, r.events);
    sock.deliver({ t: 'ack', id });
  }

  private commit(state: GameState, events: GameEvent[]): void {
    this.state = state;
    this.broadcastState(events);
    if (state.phase === 'gameEnd' && this.info.status === 'playing') {
      this.info.status = 'finished';
      this.broadcastRoom();
    }
    this.scheduleBot(events);
  }

  private scheduleBot(events: GameEvent[]): void {
    this.cancelBot?.();
    this.cancelBot = null;
    const s = this.state;
    if (!s || s.phase === 'gameEnd' || s.paused) return;
    const turn = turnOf(s);
    if (!turn || !this.botControlled(turn)) return;
    let delay = 600 + Math.floor(this.rng() * 300);
    if (events.some(e => e.type === 'trickWon')) delay += 1100;
    if (events.some(e => e.type === 'roundScored' || e.type === 'threw')) delay += 1500;
    this.cancelBot = this.clock.setTimeout(() => {
      this.cancelBot = null;
      this.botMove(turn);
    }, delay * this.botScale);
  }

  private botMove(pid: PlayerId): void {
    const s = this.state;
    if (!s || s.paused || turnOf(s) !== pid || !this.botControlled(pid)) return;
    const r = reduce(s, pid, botAction(viewFor(s, pid), this.rng), this.clock.now());
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`LocalRoom: bot ${pid} chose an illegal move: ${r.error.message}`);
    this.commit(r.state, r.events);
  }

  private botControlled(pid: PlayerId): boolean {
    const seat = this.seatOf(pid);
    return !!seat && (seat.isBot || seat.botFor);
  }

  private setEngineBotFor(pid: PlayerId, on: boolean): void {
    const seat = this.state?.seats.find(x => x.id === pid);
    if (!seat) return;
    if (on) seat.botFor = 'bot';
    else delete seat.botFor;
  }

  // ---------- broadcast ----------

  private seatOf(pid: PlayerId): RoomSeat | undefined {
    return this.info.seats.find((s): s is RoomSeat => s?.id === pid);
  }

  private broadcastRoom(): void {
    const room = this.snapshot();
    for (const sock of this.socks.values()) if (sock.open) sock.deliver({ t: 'room', room });
  }

  private broadcastState(events: GameEvent[]): void {
    const s = this.state;
    if (!s) return;
    for (const [pid, sock] of this.socks) {
      if (sock.open) sock.deliver({ t: 'state', seq: s.seq, view: viewFor(s, pid), events: eventsFor(events, pid) });
    }
  }
}
```

### Task 5: `SocketConnection` (WebSocket with hello, ping and backoff)

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/src/net/socket.ts`
- Test: `apps/app/src/net/socket.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RoomConnection`, `ConnStatus`, `HelloMessage`, `Emitter` (Task 4); protocol types (Task 1).
- Produces: `interface SocketLike` (the subset of `WebSocket` used); `interface SocketOptions { url?: string; WebSocketImpl?: new (url: string) => SocketLike; pingMs?: number }`; `backoffDelay(attempt: number): number` (500 · 2^attempt, max 5000); `class SocketConnection implements RoomConnection` — `new SocketConnection(code, hello, opts?)` connects to `ws(s)://<host>/ws/<code>`, sends `hello()` as the first frame on every (re)open, pings every 20 s, reconnects after any unexpected close (0.5 s → 1 → 2 → 4 → 5 → 5 s; reset after a successful open), retries immediately on `online` / tab becoming visible, and stops for good on `close()`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** — `apps/app/src/net/socket.test.ts`

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { PROTOCOL_VERSION, type ServerMessage } from './protocol';
import { SocketConnection, backoffDelay, type SocketLike } from './socket';
import type { HelloMessage } from './connection';

class FakeWS implements SocketLike {
  static instances: FakeWS[] = [];
  readyState = 0;
  sent: string[] = [];
  onopen: (() => void) | null = null;
  onmessage: ((ev: { data: unknown }) => void) | null = null;
  onclose: (() => void) | null = null;
  onerror: (() => void) | null = null;
  constructor(public url: string) {
    FakeWS.instances.push(this);
  }
  send(data: string) {
    this.sent.push(data);
  }
  close() {
    if (this.readyState === 3) return;
    this.readyState = 3;
    this.onclose?.();
  }
  accept() {
    this.readyState = 1;
    this.onopen?.();
  }
  message(m: unknown) {
    this.onmessage?.({ data: typeof m === 'string' ? m : JSON.stringify(m) });
  }
  drop() {
    this.readyState = 3;
    this.onclose?.();
  }
}

const hello: HelloMessage = { t: 'hello', v: PROTOCOL_VERSION, playerId: 'p', secret: 's', name: 'Filip', avatar: '🦊', coach: false };
const make = (opts = {}) => new SocketConnection('ABCDEF', () => hello, { WebSocketImpl: FakeWS, ...opts });
const latest = () => FakeWS.instances[FakeWS.instances.length - 1];

describe('SocketConnection', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    FakeWS.instances = [];
  });
  afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

  it('connects to /ws/:code on this host and sends hello first', () => {
    const conn = make();
    expect(latest().url).toBe(`ws://${window.location.host}/ws/ABCDEF`);
    expect(conn.status).toBe('connecting');
    latest().accept();
    expect(conn.status).toBe('open');
    expect(JSON.parse(latest().sent[0])).toEqual(hello);
    conn.close();
  });

  it('emits parsed server messages and ignores garbage', () => {
    const conn = make();
    const got: ServerMessage[] = [];
    conn.onMessage(m => got.push(m));
    latest().accept();
    latest().message('not json{');
    latest().message({ t: 'pong' });
    expect(got).toEqual([{ t: 'pong' }]);
    conn.close();
  });

  it('send returns false unless open', () => {
    const conn = make();
    expect(conn.send({ t: 'ping' })).toBe(false);
    latest().accept();
    expect(conn.send({ t: 'ping' })).toBe(true);
    expect(JSON.parse(latest().sent[1])).toEqual({ t: 'ping' });
    conn.close();
  });

  it('reconnects with backoff 0.5 → 5 s and re-sends hello', () => {
    const conn = make();
    const statuses: string[] = [];
    conn.onStatus(s => statuses.push(s));
    latest().accept();
    latest().drop();
    expect(conn.status).toBe('reconnecting');
    for (const delay of [500, 1000, 2000, 4000, 5000, 5000]) {
      const n = FakeWS.instances.length;
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(delay - 1);
      expect(FakeWS.instances.length).toBe(n);
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
      expect(FakeWS.instances.length).toBe(n + 1);
      latest().drop();
    }
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(5000);
    latest().accept();
    expect(JSON.parse(latest().sent[0])).toEqual(hello);
    latest().drop();
    const n = FakeWS.instances.length;
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(500);
    expect(FakeWS.instances.length).toBe(n + 1);
    expect(statuses).toContain('open');
    conn.close();
  });

  it('pings every 20 s while open', () => {
    const conn = make();
    latest().accept();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(40_000);
    expect(latest().sent.slice(1).map(s => JSON.parse(s))).toEqual([{ t: 'ping' }, { t: 'ping' }]);
    conn.close();
  });

  it('close() stops reconnecting', () => {
    const conn = make();
    latest().accept();
    conn.close();
    expect(conn.status).toBe('closed');
    const n = FakeWS.instances.length;
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_000);
    expect(FakeWS.instances.length).toBe(n);
  });

  it('backoffDelay doubles up to 5 s', () => {
    expect([0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 9].map(backoffDelay)).toEqual([500, 1000, 2000, 4000, 5000, 5000]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/net/socket.ts`**

```ts
import type { ClientMessage, ServerMessage } from './protocol';
import { Emitter, type ConnStatus, type HelloMessage, type RoomConnection } from './connection';

/** The part of the browser WebSocket this class uses (lets tests pass a fake). */
export interface SocketLike {
  readyState: number;
  send(data: string): void;
  close(): void;
  onopen: ((ev?: unknown) => void) | null;
  onmessage: ((ev: { data: unknown }) => void) | null;
  onclose: ((ev?: unknown) => void) | null;
  onerror: ((ev?: unknown) => void) | null;
}

export interface SocketOptions {
  url?: string;
  WebSocketImpl?: new (url: string) => SocketLike;
  pingMs?: number;
}

const OPEN = 1;

export function backoffDelay(attempt: number): number {
  return Math.min(500 * 2 ** attempt, 5000);
}

export class SocketConnection implements RoomConnection {
  status: ConnStatus = 'connecting';
  private ws: SocketLike | null = null;
  private attempt = 0;
  private retry: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  private ping: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private closedByUser = false;
  private readonly msgs = new Emitter<ServerMessage>();
  private readonly statuses = new Emitter<ConnStatus>();

  constructor(
    readonly code: string,
    private readonly hello: () => HelloMessage,
    private readonly opts: SocketOptions = {},
  ) {
    window.addEventListener('online', this.wake);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', this.wake);
    this.connect();
  }

  send(msg: ClientMessage): boolean {
    if (this.status !== 'open' || !this.ws || this.ws.readyState !== OPEN) return false;
    this.ws.send(JSON.stringify(msg));
    return true;
  }

  onMessage(fn: (msg: ServerMessage) => void): () => void {
    return this.msgs.on(fn);
  }

  onStatus(fn: (status: ConnStatus) => void): () => void {
    return this.statuses.on(fn);
  }

  close(): void {
    if (this.closedByUser) return;
    this.closedByUser = true;
    window.removeEventListener('online', this.wake);
    document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', this.wake);
    if (this.retry) clearTimeout(this.retry);
    this.retry = null;
    this.stopPing();
    const ws = this.ws;
    this.ws = null;
    ws?.close();
    this.setStatus('closed');
  }

  private url(): string {
    if (this.opts.url) return this.opts.url;
    const scheme = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss' : 'ws';
    return `${scheme}://${window.location.host}/ws/${encodeURIComponent(this.code)}`;
  }

  private connect(): void {
    const Impl = this.opts.WebSocketImpl ?? (WebSocket as unknown as new (url: string) => SocketLike);
    const ws = new Impl(this.url());
    this.ws = ws;
    ws.onopen = () => {
      if (this.ws !== ws) return;
      this.attempt = 0;
      this.setStatus('open');
      ws.send(JSON.stringify(this.hello()));
      this.stopPing();
      this.ping = setInterval(() => this.send({ t: 'ping' }), this.opts.pingMs ?? 20_000);
    };
    ws.onmessage = ev => {
      if (this.ws !== ws) return;
      let msg: ServerMessage;
      try {
        msg = JSON.parse(String(ev.data)) as ServerMessage;
      } catch {
        return;
      }
      this.msgs.emit(msg);
    };
    ws.onerror = () => {};
    ws.onclose = () => {
      if (this.ws !== ws) return;
      this.ws = null;
      this.stopPing();
      if (this.closedByUser) return;
      this.setStatus('reconnecting');
      this.scheduleRetry();
    };
  }

  private scheduleRetry(): void {
    const delay = backoffDelay(this.attempt);
    this.attempt += 1;
    this.retry = setTimeout(() => {
      this.retry = null;
      this.connect();
    }, delay);
  }

  /** Back online / app visible again: retry now instead of waiting out the backoff. */
  private readonly wake = (): void => {
    if (this.closedByUser || document.visibilityState === 'hidden' || !this.retry) return;
    clearTimeout(this.retry);
    this.retry = null;
    this.attempt = 0;
    this.connect();
  };

  private stopPing(): void {
    if (this.ping) clearInterval(this.ping);
    this.ping = null;
  }

  private setStatus(s: ConnStatus): void {
    if (this.status === s) return;
    this.status = s;
    this.statuses.emit(s);
  }
}
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:
  - `localService.ts`: `interface LocalOptions { botDelayScale?: number; latencyMs?: number; clock?: Clock }`; `class LocalHub { constructor(opts?: LocalOptions); create(req: CreateRoomRequest): string; get(code: string): LocalRoom | undefined; readonly latencyMs: number }`; `class LocalConnection implements RoomConnection` (opens on the next macrotask, sends `hello()` itself, delivers every server message asynchronously); `localService(hub?: LocalHub): RoomService` (`kind: 'local'`).
  - `httpService.ts`: `httpService(base?: string): RoomService` (`kind: 'remote'`; `POST /api/rooms`, `GET /api/rooms/:code`, `connect` → `SocketConnection` from Task 5).

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { PROTOCOL_VERSION, type ServerMessage } from './protocol';
import { LocalHub, localService } from './localService';
import type { ConnStatus, HelloMessage } from './connection';

const hello = (id = 'p1'): HelloMessage => ({ t: 'hello', v: PROTOCOL_VERSION, playerId: id, secret: `s-${id}`, name: 'Filip', avatar: '🦊', coach: true });

function collect(conn: { onMessage(fn: (m: ServerMessage) => void): () => void; onStatus(fn: (s: ConnStatus) => void): () => void }) {
  const msgs: ServerMessage[] = [];
  const statuses: ConnStatus[] = [];
  conn.onMessage(m => msgs.push(m));
  conn.onStatus(s => statuses.push(s));
  return { msgs, statuses };
}

describe('localService', () => {
  afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

  it('creates a room and connects asynchronously', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const svc = localService(new LocalHub());
    const { code } = await svc.createRoom({ maxPlayers: 3, playerId: 'p1', name: 'Filip', avatar: '🦊' });
    expect(code).toMatch(/^[A-Z2-9]{6}$/);
    expect(await svc.roomExists(code)).toMatchObject({ exists: true, status: 'lobby', seatsTaken: 1 });
    const conn = svc.connect(code, () => hello());
    const got = collect(conn);
    expect(conn.status).toBe('connecting');
    expect(got.msgs).toEqual([]);
    await vi.runAllTimersAsync();
    expect(got.statuses).toEqual(['open']);
    expect(got.msgs[0]).toMatchObject({ t: 'welcome', you: 'p1' });
  });

  it('reports a missing room and closes', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const svc = localService(new LocalHub());
    expect(await svc.roomExists('ZZZZZZ')).toEqual({ exists: false });
    const conn = svc.connect('ZZZZZZ', () => hello());
    const got = collect(conn);
    await vi.runAllTimersAsync();
    expect(got.msgs).toEqual([{ t: 'error', code: 'notInRoom', message: 'That room does not exist' }]);
    expect(conn.status).toBe('closed');
  });

  it('refuses to send before open and stops delivering after close', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const svc = localService(new LocalHub());
    const { code } = await svc.createRoom({ maxPlayers: 3, playerId: 'p1', name: 'Filip', avatar: '🦊' });
    const conn = svc.connect(code, () => hello());
    expect(conn.send({ t: 'ping' })).toBe(false);
    const got = collect(conn);
    await vi.runAllTimersAsync();
    expect(conn.send({ t: 'ping' })).toBe(true);
    conn.close();
    await vi.runAllTimersAsync();
    expect(got.msgs.some(m => m.t === 'pong')).toBe(false);
    expect(conn.status).toBe('closed');
  });

  it('runs a practice game: two bots and start', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const svc = localService(new LocalHub({ botDelayScale: 0.01 }));
    const { code } = await svc.createRoom({ maxPlayers: 3, playerId: 'p1', name: 'Filip', avatar: '🦊' });
    const conn = svc.connect(code, () => hello());
    const got = collect(conn);
    await vi.runAllTimersAsync();
    conn.send({ t: 'lobby', op: { op: 'addBot' } });
    conn.send({ t: 'lobby', op: { op: 'addBot' } });
    conn.send({ t: 'lobby', op: { op: 'start' } });
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
    const state = got.msgs.find(m => m.t === 'state');
    expect(state && state.t === 'state' && state.view.round!.hand.length).toBe(7);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/net/localService.ts`**

```ts
import type { ClientMessage, CreateRoomRequest, ServerMessage } from './protocol';
import { Emitter, randomCode, type ConnStatus, type HelloMessage, type RoomConnection, type RoomService } from './connection';
import { LocalRoom, realClock, type Clock, type LocalSocket } from './localRoom';

export interface LocalOptions {
  botDelayScale?: number;
  latencyMs?: number;
  clock?: Clock;
}

/** All rooms of this tab (memory only — a reload forgets them). */
export class LocalHub {
  private readonly rooms = new Map<string, LocalRoom>();
  readonly latencyMs: number;
  constructor(private readonly opts: LocalOptions = {}) {
    this.latencyMs = opts.latencyMs ?? 0;
  }

  create(req: CreateRoomRequest): string {
    let code = randomCode();
    while (this.rooms.has(code)) code = randomCode();
    this.rooms.set(code, new LocalRoom({
      code,
      maxPlayers: req.maxPlayers,
      host: { playerId: req.playerId, name: req.name, avatar: req.avatar },
      seed: `${code}:${Date.now()}`,
      clock: this.opts.clock ?? realClock,
      botDelayScale: this.opts.botDelayScale,
    }));
    return code;
  }

  get(code: string): LocalRoom | undefined {
    return this.rooms.get(code);
  }
}

export class LocalConnection implements RoomConnection {
  status: ConnStatus = 'connecting';
  private sock: LocalSocket | null = null;
  private userClosed = false;
  private readonly msgs = new Emitter<ServerMessage>();
  private readonly statuses = new Emitter<ConnStatus>();

  constructor(readonly code: string, hub: LocalHub, hello: () => HelloMessage, private readonly latencyMs = 0) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.userClosed) return;
      const room = hub.get(code);
      if (!room) {
        this.msgs.emit({ t: 'error', code: 'notInRoom', message: 'That room does not exist' });
        this.setStatus('closed');
        return;
      }
      this.sock = room.open(
        msg => setTimeout(() => !this.userClosed && this.msgs.emit(msg), this.latencyMs),
        () => {
          this.sock = null;
          setTimeout(() => this.setStatus('closed'), this.latencyMs);
        },
      );
      this.setStatus('open');
      this.sock.send(hello());
    }, 0);
  }

  send(msg: ClientMessage): boolean {
    const sock = this.sock;
    if (this.status !== 'open' || !sock) return false;
    setTimeout(() => sock.send(msg), this.latencyMs);
    return true;
  }

  onMessage(fn: (msg: ServerMessage) => void): () => void {
    return this.msgs.on(fn);
  }

  onStatus(fn: (status: ConnStatus) => void): () => void {
    return this.statuses.on(fn);
  }

  close(): void {
    this.userClosed = true;
    this.sock?.close();
    this.sock = null;
    this.setStatus('closed');
  }

  private setStatus(s: ConnStatus): void {
    if (this.status === s) return;
    this.status = s;
    this.statuses.emit(s);
  }
}

export function localService(hub: LocalHub = new LocalHub()): RoomService {
  return {
    kind: 'local',
    createRoom: async req => ({ code: hub.create(req) }),
    roomExists: async code => hub.get(code)?.exists() ?? { exists: false },
    connect: (code, hello) => new LocalConnection(code, hub, hello, hub.latencyMs),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/net/service.ts`**

```ts
import type { RoomService } from './connection';
import { safeSessionStorage } from '../store/storage';

const FLAG = 'tysiac.local';

function readFlag(): string | null {
  const store = safeSessionStorage();
  const q = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  if (q.has('local')) store.setItem(FLAG, q.get('fast') === '1' ? 'fast' : '1');
  return store.getItem(FLAG);
}

/** `?local=1` (sticky for this tab) or VITE_LOCAL=1 → rooms run in the browser (no server). */
export function isLocalMode(): boolean {
  return readFlag() !== null || import.meta.env.VITE_LOCAL === '1';
}

/** `?local=1&fast=1`: bots and animations run ~10–20× faster (e2e). */
export function isFastLocal(): boolean {
  return readFlag() === 'fast';
}

let service: RoomService | null = null;
let override: RoomService | null = null;

export async function getService(): Promise<RoomService> {
  if (override) return override;
  if (service) return service;
  if (isLocalMode()) {
    const m = await import('./localService');
    service = m.localService(new m.LocalHub({ botDelayScale: isFastLocal() ? 0.05 : 1 }));
  } else {
    const m = await import('./httpService');
    service = m.httpService();
  }
  return service;
}

export function setServiceForTests(s: RoomService | null): void {
  override = s;
  service = null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `src/net/httpService.ts`**

```ts
import type { CreateRoomResponse, RoomExistsResponse } from './protocol';
import type { RoomService } from './connection';
import { SocketConnection } from './socket';

export function httpService(base = ''): RoomService {
  return {
    kind: 'remote',
    async createRoom(req) {
      const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/rooms`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(req),
      });
      const body = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as Partial<CreateRoomResponse> & { error?: string };
      if (!res.ok || typeof body.code !== 'string') throw new Error(body.error ?? `HTTP ${res.status}`);
      return { code: body.code };
    },
    async roomExists(code) {
      const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/rooms/${encodeURIComponent(code)}`);
      if (res.status === 404) return { exists: false };
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
      return (await res.json()) as RoomExistsResponse;
    },
    connect: (code, hello) => new SocketConnection(code, hello),
  };
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/src/store/client.ts`, `apps/app/src/store/notes.ts`, `apps/app/src/test/fixtures.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: engine `cardEquals`, `cardKey`, `explainEvent`, `SUIT_SYMBOL`, types; protocol `ServerMessage`, `RoomInfo`.
- Produces:
  - `notes.ts`: `who(view: PlayerView, p: PlayerId): string` (`'You'` for the viewer, else the seat name); `eventNote(e: GameEvent, view: PlayerView, coach: boolean): string | null` (coach on → `explainEvent` text; always notes for `marriage`, `wykladana`, `biddingWon`).
  - `client.ts`: `type SoundName = 'card' | 'shuffle' | 'sweep' | 'turn'`; constants `FLIGHT_MS = 280`, `HOLD_MS = 1000`, `SWEEP_MS = 380`, `DEAL_MS = 400`, `NOTE_MS = 700`; `interface ClientState { me; room; clockOffset; view; shown; queue; holding; sweepTo; pending: {id, card} | null; inflight: string | null; shake: string | null; toast: string | null; roundResult: RoundSummary | null; note: string | null; error: {code, message} | null }`; `initialClient`; `applyServer(s, msg, localNow): ClientState`; `beginPlay(s, id, card)`; `beginAct(s, id)`; `dismissResult(s)`; `isIdle(s: Pick<ClientState, 'queue' | 'shown' | 'view' | 'roundResult' | 'holding'>): boolean`; `type StepKind = 'flight' | 'hold' | 'sweep' | 'pause' | 'beat' | 'sync'`; `interface Step { state; wait: number; kind: StepKind; sound?: SoundName }`; `advance(s, coach: boolean): Step | null`; view helpers `withCardPlayed`, `withTrickWon`, `withMarriage`.
  - Display rules later tasks rely on: the table shows `shown.round.trick.cards` plus `pending` (as the viewer's card); the hand shows `shown.round.hand` minus `pending.card`; `isIdle` gates every action button.
  - `test/fixtures.ts`: `SEATS` (ids `me`, `b` "Bot Basia", `z` "Bot Zenek"), `SEATS4` (+ `h` "Bot Halina"), `step(state, p, action)`, `botStep(state, rng?)`, `findState(until, seats?, maxActions?)`, `viewOf(state, p?)`, `stateMsg(state, events?, p?)`, `roomInfo(over?, seats?)`, `welcomeMsg(room?)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test fixtures** — `apps/app/src/test/fixtures.ts`

```ts
import {
  botAction, createGame, eventsFor, makeRandom, reduce, turnOf, viewFor,
  type Action, type GameEvent, type GameState, type PlayerId, type PlayerView, type Seat,
} from '@tysiac/engine';
import type { RoomInfo, RoomSeat, ServerMessage } from '../net/protocol';

export const SEATS: Seat[] = [
  { id: 'me', name: 'Filip', avatar: '🦊', isBot: false },
  { id: 'b', name: 'Bot Basia', avatar: '🤖', isBot: true },
  { id: 'z', name: 'Bot Zenek', avatar: '🤖', isBot: true },
];
export const SEATS4: Seat[] = [...SEATS, { id: 'h', name: 'Bot Halina', avatar: '🤖', isBot: true }];

export interface Stepped {
  state: GameState;
  events: GameEvent[];
}

export function step(state: GameState, p: PlayerId, action: Action): Stepped {
  const r = reduce(state, p, action, 0);
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`fixture: ${p} ${JSON.stringify(action)} rejected: ${r.error.message}`);
  return { state: r.state, events: r.events };
}

export function botStep(state: GameState, rng: () => number = () => 0.5): Stepped {
  const p = turnOf(state);
  if (!p) throw new Error('fixture: nobody to act');
  return step(state, p, botAction(viewFor(state, p), rng));
}

/** Plays bot moves for every seat (including `me`) from fresh games fx-0…fx-299 until `until` holds. */
export function findState(until: (s: GameState) => boolean, seats: Seat[] = SEATS, maxActions = 300): GameState {
  for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) {
    let s = createGame({ seed: `fx-${i}`, seats, maxPlayers: seats.length as 3 | 4, firstDealer: 0 });
    const rng = makeRandom(`fx-${i}`);
    for (let n = 0; n < maxActions && s.phase !== 'gameEnd'; n++) {
      if (until(s)) return s;
      s = botStep(s, rng).state;
    }
    if (until(s)) return s;
  }
  throw new Error('findState: no seed reached the condition');
}

export const viewOf = (s: GameState, p: PlayerId = 'me'): PlayerView => viewFor(s, p);

export function stateMsg(s: GameState, events: GameEvent[] = [], p: PlayerId = 'me'): Extract<ServerMessage, { t: 'state' }> {
  return { t: 'state', seq: s.seq, view: viewFor(s, p), events: eventsFor(events, p) };
}

export function roomInfo(over: Partial<RoomInfo> = {}, seats: Seat[] = SEATS): RoomInfo {
  return {
    code: 'ABCDEF',
    status: 'playing',
    maxPlayers: seats.length as 3 | 4,
    hostId: 'me',
    firstDealer: 0,
    seats: seats.map((s): RoomSeat => ({ id: s.id, name: s.name, avatar: s.avatar, isBot: s.isBot, connected: true, awaySince: null, botFor: false, coach: false })),
    ...over,
  };
}

export const welcomeMsg = (room: RoomInfo = roomInfo()): ServerMessage => ({ t: 'welcome', you: 'me', room, serverTime: 0 });
```
