```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { cardEquals, cardKey, turnOf, type Action, type Card, type GameEvent, type GameState } from '@tysiac/engine';
import {
  FLIGHT_MS, HOLD_MS, advance, applyServer, beginAct, beginPlay, dismissResult, initialClient, isIdle,
  type ClientState, type Step,
} from './client';
import { eventNote } from './notes';
import { botStep, findState, roomInfo, stateMsg, step, viewOf } from '../test/fixtures';

const ready = (s: GameState): ClientState => {
  const v = viewOf(s);
  return { ...initialClient, me: 'me', room: roomInfo(), view: v, shown: v };
};
const firstCard = (s: GameState): Card => (viewOf(s).legal.find(a => a.type === 'card') as Extract<Action, { type: 'card' }>).card;
function drain(c: ClientState): { state: ClientState; steps: Step[] } {
  const steps: Step[] = [];
  for (let st = advance(c, false); st; st = advance(c, false)) {
    steps.push(st);
    c = st.state;
  }
  return { state: c, steps };
}

describe('applyServer', () => {
  it('welcome stores me, room and the clock offset', () => {
    const c = applyServer(initialClient, { t: 'welcome', you: 'me', room: roomInfo(), serverTime: 5_000 }, 3_000);
    expect(c.me).toBe('me');
    expect(c.room!.code).toBe('ABCDEF');
    expect(c.clockOffset).toBe(2_000);
  });

  it('a state without events is shown immediately', () => {
    const s = findState(x => x.phase === 'bidding');
    const c = applyServer({ ...initialClient, me: 'me' }, stateMsg(s), 0);
    expect(c.view).toBe(c.shown);
    expect(isIdle(c)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('a state with events is queued and shown lags behind', () => {
    const s0 = findState(x => x.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(x) === 'b' && x.round!.trick.cards.length === 1);
    const { state: s1, events } = botStep(s0);
    const c = applyServer(ready(s0), stateMsg(s1, events), 0);
    expect(c.queue.map(e => e.type)).toEqual(['cardPlayed']);
    expect(c.shown!.round!.trick.cards).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(isIdle(c)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('a resync state drops the queue and the pending card', () => {
    const s0 = findState(x => x.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(x) === 'me');
    let c = beginPlay(ready(s0), 'p1', firstCard(s0));
    c = { ...c, queue: [{ type: 'paused', p: 'b', audience: 'all' }] };
    const c2 = applyServer(c, stateMsg(s0), 0);
    expect(c2.queue).toEqual([]);
    expect(c2.pending).toBeNull();
    expect(c2.shown).toBe(c2.view);
  });

  it('reject of the pending card rolls it back with a shake and the reason', () => {
    const s0 = findState(x => x.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(x) === 'me');
    const card = firstCard(s0);
    const c = applyServer(beginPlay(ready(s0), 'p1', card), { t: 'reject', id: 'p1', code: 'illegalCard', message: 'You must follow ♦' }, 0);
    expect(c.pending).toBeNull();
    expect(c.shake).toBe(cardKey(card));
    expect(c.toast).toBe('You must follow ♦');
  });

  it('ack clears an in-flight action; reject of it shows the reason', () => {
    const s0 = findState(x => x.phase === 'bidding' && turnOf(x) === 'me');
    const c = beginAct(ready(s0), 'a1');
    expect(c.inflight).toBe('a1');
    expect(applyServer(c, { t: 'ack', id: 'a1' }, 0).inflight).toBeNull();
    const r = applyServer(c, { t: 'reject', id: 'a1', code: 'badBid', message: 'Too high' }, 0);
    expect(r.inflight).toBeNull();
    expect(r.toast).toBe('Too high');
  });

  it('error is stored', () => {
    expect(applyServer(initialClient, { t: 'error', code: 'roomFull', message: 'This room is full' }, 0).error).toEqual({ code: 'roomFull', message: 'This room is full' });
  });
});

describe('advance', () => {
  it('own pending card resolves without a second flight, then holds and sweeps the trick', () => {
    const s0 = findState(x => x.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(x) === 'me' && x.round!.trick.cards.length === 2 && x.round!.hands.me.length > 1);
    const card = firstCard(s0);
    const { state: s1, events } = step(s0, 'me', { type: 'card', card });
    let c = beginPlay(ready(s0), 'p1', card);
    c = applyServer(c, stateMsg(s1, events), 0);

    let st = advance(c, false)!;
    expect(st.kind).toBe('flight');
    expect(st.wait).toBe(0);
    expect(st.state.pending).toBeNull();
    expect(st.state.shown!.round!.trick.cards).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(st.state.shown!.round!.hand.some(x => cardEquals(x, card))).toBe(false);

    st = advance(st.state, false)!;
    expect(st.kind).toBe('hold');
    expect(st.wait).toBe(HOLD_MS);
    expect(st.state.shown!.round!.trick.cards).toHaveLength(3);

    st = advance(st.state, false)!;
    expect(st.kind).toBe('sweep');
    expect(st.sound).toBe('sweep');
    const winner = events.find(e => e.type === 'trickWon')!;
    expect(st.state.sweepTo).toBe(winner.type === 'trickWon' ? winner.p : null);
    expect(st.state.shown!.round!.trick.cards).toEqual([]);
    expect(st.state.shown!.round!.lastTrick!.cards).toHaveLength(3);

    st = advance(st.state, false)!;
    expect(st.kind).toBe('sync');
    expect(st.state.shown).toBe(st.state.view);
    expect(advance(st.state, false)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("an opponent's card flies in with a sound and lowers their card count", () => {
    const s0 = findState(x => x.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(x) === 'b' && x.round!.trick.cards.length === 1);
    const { state: s1, events } = botStep(s0);
    const c = applyServer(ready(s0), stateMsg(s1, events), 0);
    const st = advance(c, false)!;
    expect(st.kind).toBe('flight');
    expect(st.wait).toBe(FLIGHT_MS);
    expect(st.sound).toBe('card');
    expect(st.state.shown!.round!.trick.cards).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(st.state.shown!.round!.handCounts.b).toBe(c.shown!.round!.handCounts.b - 1);
  });

  it('pauses on the round result until dismissed, then deals the next round', () => {
    const s0 = findState(x => x.phase === 'tricks' && x.rounds.length === 0 && turnOf(x) === 'me' && x.round!.hands.me.length === 1 && x.round!.trick.cards.length === 2);
    const { state: s1, events } = step(s0, 'me', { type: 'card', card: firstCard(s0) });
    let c = applyServer(ready(s0), stateMsg(s1, events), 0);
    const first = drain(c);
    expect(first.steps.at(-1)!.kind).toBe('pause');
    expect(first.state.roundResult!.number).toBe(1);
    expect(advance(first.state, false)).toBeNull();
    c = dismissResult(first.state);
    const second = drain(c);
    expect(second.steps.some(x => x.sound === 'shuffle')).toBe(true);
    expect(second.state.shown).toBe(second.state.view);
    expect(second.state.shown!.round!.number).toBe(2);
    expect(isIdle(second.state)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('plays the turn sound when it becomes my turn', () => {
    const s0 = findState(x => x.phase === 'bidding' && turnOf(x) === 'b' && turnOf(botStep(x).state) === 'me');
    const { state: s1, events } = botStep(s0);
    const { steps } = drain(applyServer(ready(s0), stateMsg(s1, events), 0));
    expect(steps.at(-1)!.kind).toBe('sync');
    expect(steps.at(-1)!.sound).toBe('turn');
  });
});

describe('eventNote', () => {
  const s = findState(x => x.phase === 'tricks');
  const v = viewOf(s);
  it('announces marriages without the coach', () => {
    expect(eventNote({ type: 'marriage', p: 'b', suit: 'hearts', points: 100, audience: 'all' }, v, false)).toBe('Bot Basia declared the ♥ marriage: +100. ♥ are trumps.');
  });
  it('stays quiet for ordinary events without the coach', () => {
    expect(eventNote({ type: 'passed', p: 'b', audience: 'all' }, v, false)).toBeNull();
  });
  it('uses the engine explanation with the coach', () => {
    const e: GameEvent = { type: 'trickWon', p: 'me', cards: [], card: { suit: 'hearts', rank: 'A' }, points: 0, byTrump: true, audience: 'all' };
    expect(eventNote(e, v, true)).toContain('You won with A♥');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/store/notes.ts`**

```ts
import { SUIT_SYMBOL, explainEvent, type GameEvent, type PlayerId, type PlayerView } from '@tysiac/engine';

/** 'You' for the viewer, otherwise the seat's name. */
export function who(view: PlayerView, p: PlayerId): string {
  if (p === view.me) return 'You';
  return view.seats.find(s => s.id === p)?.name ?? 'Someone';
}

/** A one-line note shown in the action zone while an event plays. */
export function eventNote(e: GameEvent, view: PlayerView, coach: boolean): string | null {
  if (coach) {
    const text = explainEvent(e, view);
    if (text) return text;
  }
  switch (e.type) {
    case 'marriage': {
      const s = SUIT_SYMBOL[e.suit];
      return `${who(view, e.p)} declared the ${s} marriage: +${e.points}. ${s} are trumps.`;
    }
    case 'wykladana':
      return `${who(view, e.p)} won every trick — wykładana!`;
    case 'biddingWon':
      return `${who(view, e.p)} won the bid at ${e.amount}.`;
    default:
      return null;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/store/client.ts`**

```ts
import {
  cardEquals, cardKey,
  type Card, type GameEvent, type PlayerId, type PlayerView, type RoundSummary, type RoundView,
} from '@tysiac/engine';
import type { RoomInfo, ServerMessage } from '../net/protocol';
import { eventNote } from './notes';

export type SoundName = 'card' | 'shuffle' | 'sweep' | 'turn';

export const FLIGHT_MS = 280;
export const HOLD_MS = 1000;
export const SWEEP_MS = 380;
export const DEAL_MS = 400;
export const NOTE_MS = 700;

export interface ClientState {
  me: PlayerId | null;
  room: RoomInfo | null;
  /** serverTime − local time at welcome (to compare with awaySince). */
  clockOffset: number;
  /** Latest view from the server. */
  view: PlayerView | null;
  /** What the table shows; catches up with `view` as the event queue plays. */
  shown: PlayerView | null;
  queue: GameEvent[];
  /** The completed trick at the head of the queue is being held on the table. */
  holding: boolean;
  /** Winner of the last swept trick (exit animation target). */
  sweepTo: PlayerId | null;
  /** Card played optimistically, awaiting the server. */
  pending: { id: string; card: Card } | null;
  /** Non-card action awaiting ack/reject. */
  inflight: string | null;
  /** cardKey of a card that bounced back. */
  shake: string | null;
  toast: string | null;
  roundResult: RoundSummary | null;
  note: string | null;
  error: { code: string; message: string } | null;
}

export const initialClient: ClientState = {
  me: null, room: null, clockOffset: 0, view: null, shown: null, queue: [], holding: false, sweepTo: null,
  pending: null, inflight: null, shake: null, toast: null, roundResult: null, note: null, error: null,
};

export function applyServer(s: ClientState, msg: ServerMessage, localNow: number): ClientState {
  switch (msg.t) {
    case 'welcome':
      return { ...s, me: msg.you, room: msg.room, clockOffset: msg.serverTime - localNow, error: null };
    case 'room':
      return { ...s, room: msg.room };
    case 'state':
      if (msg.events.length === 0 || s.shown === null) {
        return { ...s, view: msg.view, shown: msg.view, queue: [], holding: false, pending: null, inflight: null };
      }
      return { ...s, view: msg.view, queue: [...s.queue, ...msg.events] };
    case 'ack':
      return s.inflight === msg.id ? { ...s, inflight: null } : s;
    case 'reject':
      if (s.pending && s.pending.id === msg.id) {
        return { ...s, pending: null, shake: cardKey(s.pending.card), toast: msg.message };
      }
      if (s.inflight === msg.id) return { ...s, inflight: null, toast: msg.message };
      return s;
    case 'error':
      return { ...s, error: { code: msg.code, message: msg.message } };
    case 'pong':
      return s;
  }
}

export function beginPlay(s: ClientState, id: string, card: Card): ClientState {
  return { ...s, pending: { id, card }, shake: null, toast: null };
}

export function beginAct(s: ClientState, id: string): ClientState {
  return { ...s, inflight: id, toast: null };
}

export function dismissResult(s: ClientState): ClientState {
  return { ...s, roundResult: null };
}

/** Nothing is animating or waiting for the player to dismiss: actions may be offered. */
export function isIdle(s: Pick<ClientState, 'queue' | 'shown' | 'view' | 'roundResult' | 'holding'>): boolean {
  return s.queue.length === 0 && s.shown === s.view && s.roundResult === null && !s.holding;
}

function mapRound(v: PlayerView, f: (r: RoundView) => RoundView): PlayerView {
  return v.round ? { ...v, round: f(v.round) } : v;
}

export function withCardPlayed(v: PlayerView, p: PlayerId, card: Card): PlayerView {
  return {
    ...mapRound(v, r => ({
      ...r,
      hand: p === v.me ? r.hand.filter(c => !cardEquals(c, card)) : r.hand,
      handCounts: { ...r.handCounts, [p]: Math.max(0, (r.handCounts[p] ?? 0) - 1) },
      trick: { ...r.trick, cards: [...r.trick.cards, { p, card }] },
    })),
    turn: null,
  };
}

export function withTrickWon(v: PlayerView, e: Extract<GameEvent, { type: 'trickWon' }>): PlayerView {
  return mapRound(v, r => ({
    ...r,
    trick: { leader: e.p, cards: [] },
    lastTrick: { cards: e.cards, winner: e.p, points: e.points },
    played: [...r.played, ...e.cards.map(t => t.card)],
    tricksTaken: { ...r.tricksTaken, [e.p]: (r.tricksTaken[e.p] ?? 0) + 1 },
    trickPoints: { ...r.trickPoints, [e.p]: (r.trickPoints[e.p] ?? 0) + e.points },
  }));
}

export function withMarriage(v: PlayerView, e: Extract<GameEvent, { type: 'marriage' }>): PlayerView {
  return mapRound(v, r => ({
    ...r,
    trump: e.suit,
    marriages: { ...r.marriages, [e.p]: [...(r.marriages[e.p] ?? []), e.suit] },
  }));
}

export type StepKind = 'flight' | 'hold' | 'sweep' | 'pause' | 'beat' | 'sync';
export interface Step {
  state: ClientState;
  /** How long to wait before the next step (before speed scaling). */
  wait: number;
  kind: StepKind;
  sound?: SoundName;
}

/** Plays the next queued event onto `shown`. Returns null when there is nothing to do (or a sheet blocks). */
export function advance(s: ClientState, coach: boolean): Step | null {
  if (s.roundResult) return null;
  const e = s.queue[0];
  if (!e) {
    if (s.view && s.shown !== s.view) {
      const sound: SoundName | undefined = s.view.turn === s.me && s.view.phase !== 'gameEnd' ? 'turn' : undefined;
      return { state: { ...s, shown: s.view, holding: false }, wait: 0, kind: 'sync', sound };
    }
    return null;
  }
  const shown = s.shown!;
  const rest = s.queue.slice(1);
  const note = () => eventNote(e, s.view ?? shown, coach) ?? s.note;
  switch (e.type) {
    case 'cardPlayed': {
      const mine = e.p === s.me && s.pending !== null && cardEquals(s.pending.card, e.card);
      return {
        state: { ...s, queue: rest, shown: withCardPlayed(shown, e.p, e.card), pending: mine ? null : s.pending },
        wait: mine ? 0 : FLIGHT_MS,
        kind: 'flight',
        sound: mine ? undefined : 'card',
      };
    }
    case 'trickWon':
      if (!s.holding) return { state: { ...s, holding: true, note: note() }, wait: HOLD_MS, kind: 'hold' };
      return {
        state: { ...s, queue: rest, holding: false, sweepTo: e.p, shown: withTrickWon(shown, e) },
        wait: SWEEP_MS,
        kind: 'sweep',
        sound: 'sweep',
      };
    case 'roundScored':
    case 'threw':
      return { state: { ...s, queue: rest, roundResult: e.summary, note: note() }, wait: 0, kind: 'pause' };
    case 'dealt':
      return { state: { ...s, queue: rest, note: null, sweepTo: null }, wait: DEAL_MS, kind: 'beat', sound: 'shuffle' };
    case 'marriage':
      return { state: { ...s, queue: rest, shown: withMarriage(shown, e), note: note() }, wait: NOTE_MS, kind: 'beat' };
    case 'biddingWon':
    case 'wykladana':
      return { state: { ...s, queue: rest, note: note() }, wait: NOTE_MS, kind: 'beat' };
    default:
      return { state: { ...s, queue: rest }, wait: 0, kind: 'beat' };
  }
}
```

### Task 8: Room store (zustand) with the playback pump and sound sink

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/src/store/room.ts`, `apps/app/src/test/fakes.ts`
- Test: `apps/app/src/store/room.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything in `store/client.ts` (Task 7); `RoomConnection`, `ConnStatus`, `Emitter`, `FATAL_ERRORS` (Task 4); `isFastLocal` (Task 6); `usePrefs` (Task 3).
- Produces:
  - `interface RoomStore extends ClientState { status: ConnStatus | 'idle'; conn: RoomConnection | null; attach(conn); detach(); send(msg): boolean; act(action: Action): void; playCard(card: Card): void; dismissResult(): void; clearToast(): void; clearShake(): void }`; `useRoom` (zustand hook).
  - `pump(): void` (plays queued steps on timers); `coachOn(s: Pick<ClientState,'room'|'me'>): boolean` (the viewer's seat `coach` flag); `stepWait(step, { speed, reduced, fast }): number` (fast-local ×0.1, speed fast ×0.5, reduced motion → flights/sweeps 0); `setSoundSink(fn: (s: SoundName) => void): void`; `newId(): string`.
  - Rules: `playCard` is ignored while a card is pending or an action is in flight (double-tap guard); a send that fails (offline) is rolled back at once with toast "You're offline — reconnecting…"; fatal `error` codes close the connection.
  - `test/fakes.ts`: `class FakeConnection implements RoomConnection { sent: ClientMessage[]; accept: boolean; push(msg); setStatus(s); acts() }`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the fake connection** — `apps/app/src/test/fakes.ts`

```ts
import { Emitter, type ConnStatus, type RoomConnection } from '../net/connection';
import type { ClientMessage, ServerMessage } from '../net/protocol';

export type ActMessage = Extract<ClientMessage, { t: 'act' }>;

export class FakeConnection implements RoomConnection {
  readonly code = 'ABCDEF';
  status: ConnStatus = 'open';
  sent: ClientMessage[] = [];
  /** Set false to simulate being offline (send returns false). */
  accept = true;
  private readonly msgs = new Emitter<ServerMessage>();
  private readonly statuses = new Emitter<ConnStatus>();

  send(msg: ClientMessage): boolean {
    if (!this.accept) return false;
    this.sent.push(msg);
    return true;
  }
  onMessage(fn: (msg: ServerMessage) => void): () => void {
    return this.msgs.on(fn);
  }
  onStatus(fn: (s: ConnStatus) => void): () => void {
    return this.statuses.on(fn);
  }
  close(): void {
    this.setStatus('closed');
  }
  push(msg: ServerMessage): void {
    this.msgs.emit(msg);
  }
  setStatus(s: ConnStatus): void {
    this.status = s;
    this.statuses.emit(s);
  }
  acts(): ActMessage[] {
    return this.sent.filter((m): m is ActMessage => m.t === 'act');
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing test** — `apps/app/src/store/room.test.ts`

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { cardKey, turnOf, type Action, type Card, type GameState } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { pump, setSoundSink, stepWait, useRoom } from './room';
import { FakeConnection } from '../test/fakes';
import { botStep, findState, stateMsg, viewOf, welcomeMsg } from '../test/fixtures';

function joinAt(s: GameState): FakeConnection {
  const conn = new FakeConnection();
  useRoom.getState().attach(conn);
  conn.push(welcomeMsg());
  conn.push(stateMsg(s));
  return conn;
}
const legalCard = (s: GameState): Card => (viewOf(s).legal.find(a => a.type === 'card') as Extract<Action, { type: 'card' }>).card;

describe('useRoom', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    useRoom.getState().detach();
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    useRoom.getState().detach();
    setSoundSink(() => {});
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('attach follows the connection status; detach closes and resets', () => {
    const conn = new FakeConnection();
    useRoom.getState().attach(conn);
    expect(useRoom.getState().status).toBe('open');
    conn.setStatus('reconnecting');
    expect(useRoom.getState().status).toBe('reconnecting');
    useRoom.getState().detach();
    expect(conn.status).toBe('closed');
    expect(useRoom.getState().status).toBe('idle');
    expect(useRoom.getState().view).toBeNull();
  });

  it('playCard is optimistic and sends exactly one act even when tapped twice', () => {
    const s0 = findState(s => s.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(s) === 'me');
    const conn = joinAt(s0);
    const card = legalCard(s0);
    useRoom.getState().playCard(card);
    useRoom.getState().playCard(card);
    expect(useRoom.getState().pending?.card).toEqual(card);
    expect(conn.acts()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(conn.acts()[0].action).toEqual({ type: 'card', card });
  });

  it('a reject rolls the card back with a shake', () => {
    const s0 = findState(s => s.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(s) === 'me');
    const conn = joinAt(s0);
    const card = legalCard(s0);
    useRoom.getState().playCard(card);
    conn.push({ t: 'reject', id: conn.acts()[0].id, code: 'illegalCard', message: 'You must follow ♦' });
    expect(useRoom.getState().pending).toBeNull();
    expect(useRoom.getState().shake).toBe(cardKey(card));
    expect(useRoom.getState().toast).toBe('You must follow ♦');
    useRoom.getState().clearShake();
    expect(useRoom.getState().shake).toBeNull();
  });

  it('rolls back at once when offline', () => {
    const s0 = findState(s => s.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(s) === 'me');
    const conn = joinAt(s0);
    conn.accept = false;
    useRoom.getState().playCard(legalCard(s0));
    expect(useRoom.getState().pending).toBeNull();
    expect(useRoom.getState().toast).toBe("You're offline — reconnecting…");
  });

  it('act marks the action in flight until ack', () => {
    const s0 = findState(s => s.phase === 'bidding' && turnOf(s) === 'me');
    const conn = joinAt(s0);
    useRoom.getState().act({ type: 'pass' });
    expect(useRoom.getState().inflight).toBe(conn.acts()[0].id);
    conn.push({ t: 'ack', id: conn.acts()[0].id });
    expect(useRoom.getState().inflight).toBeNull();
  });

  it('plays queued events on timers until the table catches up, with sounds', () => {
    const sink = vi.fn();
    setSoundSink(sink);
    const s0 = findState(s => s.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(s) === 'b' && s.round!.trick.cards.length === 1);
    const conn = joinAt(s0);
    const { state: s1, events } = botStep(s0);
    conn.push(stateMsg(s1, events));
    expect(useRoom.getState().shown).not.toBe(useRoom.getState().view);
    expect(sink).toHaveBeenCalledWith('card');
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(3000);
    expect(useRoom.getState().shown).toBe(useRoom.getState().view);
    pump();
    expect(useRoom.getState().shown).toBe(useRoom.getState().view);
  });

  it('fatal errors close the connection', () => {
    const conn = new FakeConnection();
    useRoom.getState().attach(conn);
    conn.push({ t: 'error', code: 'roomFull', message: 'This room is full' });
    expect(conn.status).toBe('closed');
    expect(useRoom.getState().error).toEqual({ code: 'roomFull', message: 'This room is full' });
  });

  it('stepWait scales with speed, fast mode and reduced motion', () => {
    const st = { state: useRoom.getState(), wait: 1000, kind: 'hold' as const };
    const fl = { ...st, kind: 'flight' as const };
    expect(stepWait(st, { speed: 'normal', reduced: false, fast: false })).toBe(1000);
    expect(stepWait(st, { speed: 'fast', reduced: false, fast: false })).toBe(500);
    expect(stepWait(st, { speed: 'normal', reduced: false, fast: true })).toBe(100);
    expect(stepWait(fl, { speed: 'normal', reduced: true, fast: false })).toBe(0);
    expect(stepWait(st, { speed: 'normal', reduced: true, fast: false })).toBe(1000);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/store/room.ts`**

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import type { Action, Card } from '@tysiac/engine';
import type { ClientMessage, ServerMessage } from '../net/protocol';
import { FATAL_ERRORS, type ConnStatus, type RoomConnection } from '../net/connection';
import { isFastLocal } from '../net/service';
import { usePrefs, type Speed } from './prefs';
import {
  advance, applyServer, beginAct, beginPlay, dismissResult, initialClient,
  type ClientState, type SoundName, type Step,
} from './client';

export interface RoomStore extends ClientState {
  status: ConnStatus | 'idle';
  conn: RoomConnection | null;
  attach(conn: RoomConnection): void;
  detach(): void;
  send(msg: ClientMessage): boolean;
  act(action: Action): void;
  playCard(card: Card): void;
  dismissResult(): void;
  clearToast(): void;
  clearShake(): void;
}

const OFFLINE = "You're offline — reconnecting…";
let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
let offs: (() => void)[] = [];
let sink: (s: SoundName) => void = () => {};
let counter = 0;
const prefix = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);

export const newId = (): string => `${prefix}${++counter}`;

export function setSoundSink(fn: (s: SoundName) => void): void {
  sink = fn;
}

export function stepWait(step: Step, o: { speed: Speed; reduced: boolean; fast: boolean }): number {
  if (o.reduced && (step.kind === 'flight' || step.kind === 'sweep')) return 0;
  const factor = o.fast ? 0.1 : o.speed === 'fast' ? 0.5 : 1;
  return Math.round(step.wait * factor);
}

export function coachOn(s: Pick<ClientState, 'room' | 'me'>): boolean {
  return !!s.room?.seats.find(x => x?.id === s.me)?.coach;
}

function reducedMotion(): boolean {
  try {
    return window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export const useRoom = create<RoomStore>()((set, get) => ({
  ...initialClient,
  status: 'idle',
  conn: null,
  attach(conn) {
    get().detach();
    offs = [conn.onMessage(receive), conn.onStatus(status => set({ status }))];
    set({ conn, status: conn.status });
  },
  detach() {
    for (const off of offs) off();
    offs = [];
    get().conn?.close();
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = null;
    set({ ...initialClient, conn: null, status: 'idle' });
  },
  send(msg) {
    return get().conn?.send(msg) ?? false;
  },
  act(action) {
    const id = newId();
    set(s => beginAct(s, id));
    if (!get().send({ t: 'act', id, action })) receive({ t: 'reject', id, code: 'offline', message: OFFLINE });
  },
  playCard(card) {
    const s = get();
    if (s.pending || s.inflight) return;
    const id = newId();
    set(st => beginPlay(st, id, card));
    sink('card');
    if (!get().send({ t: 'act', id, action: { type: 'card', card } })) receive({ t: 'reject', id, code: 'offline', message: OFFLINE });
  },
  dismissResult() {
    set(s => dismissResult(s));
    pump();
  },
  clearToast() {
    set({ toast: null });
  },
  clearShake() {
    set({ shake: null });
  },
}));

function receive(msg: ServerMessage): void {
  const conn = useRoom.getState().conn;
  useRoom.setState(s => applyServer(s, msg, Date.now()));
  if (msg.t === 'error' && FATAL_ERRORS.has(msg.code)) conn?.close();
  if (msg.t === 'state') {
    if (msg.events.length === 0 && timer) {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = null;
    }
    pump();
  }
}

/** Applies the next playback step now and schedules the one after it. */
export function pump(): void {
  if (timer) return;
  const s = useRoom.getState();
  const step = advance(s, coachOn(s));
  if (!step) return;
  useRoom.setState(step.state);
  if (step.sound) sink(step.sound);
  const wait = stepWait(step, { speed: usePrefs.getState().speed, reduced: reducedMotion(), fast: isFastLocal() });
  timer = setTimeout(() => {
    timer = null;
    pump();
  }, wait);
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { cardKey, parseCards, type Card } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { Hand } from './Hand';
import { marriageTag, sortHand } from './cards';

const cards = parseCards('9S KH 10D QH AS');
const labels = () => within(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Your hand' })).getAllByRole('button').map(b => b.getAttribute('aria-label'));

function renderHand(over: Partial<Parameters<typeof Hand>[0]> = {}) {
  const onTap = vi.fn<(c: Card) => void>();
  render(<Hand cards={cards} selected={null} isPlayable={() => true} recommended={null} declared={[]} shake={null} onTap={onTap} {...over} />);
  return onTap;
}

describe('Hand', () => {
  it('sorts ♠ ♥ ♣ ♦ and by strength, one labelled button per card', () => {
    renderHand();
    expect(labels()).toEqual(['A♠', '9♠', 'K♥', 'Q♥', '10♦']);
  });

  it('dims unplayable cards and raises the selected card', () => {
    renderHand({ selected: cardKey(cards[2]), isPlayable: c => c.suit !== 'spades' });
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'A♠' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '10♦' })).not.toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '10♦' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'K♥' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
  });

  it('tags the king of an undeclared marriage only', () => {
    renderHand();
    expect(screen.getByText('♥ 100')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(marriageTag(cards[1], cards, ['hearts'])).toBeNull();
    expect(marriageTag(cards[3], cards, [])).toBeNull();
  });

  it('only reports taps', async () => {
    const onTap = renderHand();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '10♦' }));
    expect(onTap).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(onTap).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ rank: '10', suit: 'diamonds' });
  });

  it('exposes the card count for the CSS layout', () => {
    renderHand();
    expect(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Your hand' }).style.getPropertyValue('--n')).toBe('5');
  });

  it('sortHand does not mutate its input', () => {
    const copy = [...cards];
    sortHand(cards);
    expect(cards).toEqual(copy);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/game/cards.ts`**

```ts
import { RULES, SUIT_SYMBOL, hasMarriage, type Card, type Suit } from '@tysiac/engine';

/** Alternating colours: ♠ ♥ ♣ ♦. */
const SUIT_ORDER: readonly Suit[] = ['spades', 'hearts', 'clubs', 'diamonds'];

export function sortHand(cards: readonly Card[]): Card[] {
  return [...cards].sort(
    (a, b) => SUIT_ORDER.indexOf(a.suit) - SUIT_ORDER.indexOf(b.suit) || RULES.rankStrength[b.rank] - RULES.rankStrength[a.rank],
  );
}

export function isRed(card: Card): boolean {
  return card.suit === 'hearts' || card.suit === 'diamonds';
}

/** "♥ 100" on the king of a K+Q pair that has not been declared yet. */
export function marriageTag(card: Card, hand: readonly Card[], declared: readonly Suit[]): string | null {
  if (card.rank !== 'K' || declared.includes(card.suit) || !hasMarriage(hand, card.suit)) return null;
  return `${SUIT_SYMBOL[card.suit]} ${RULES.marriageValues[card.suit]}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/game/Card.tsx`**

```tsx
import type { CSSProperties } from 'react';
import { m } from 'motion/react';
import { SUIT_SYMBOL, cardKey, cardLabel, type Card } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { isRed } from './cards';
import './card.css';

export type CardLook = 'normal' | 'dim' | 'raised' | 'recommended';

export function CardFace({ card }: { card: Card }) {
  const s = SUIT_SYMBOL[card.suit];
  return (
    <>
      <span className="card__idx" aria-hidden="true">
        <span className="card__rank">{card.rank}</span>
        <span className="card__suit">{s}</span>
      </span>
      <span className="card__pip" aria-hidden="true">
        {s}
      </span>
    </>
  );
}

export interface CardButtonProps {
  card: Card;
  look: CardLook;
  tag?: string | null;
  shake?: boolean;
  className?: string;
  style?: CSSProperties;
  onTap(): void;
}

export function CardButton({ card, look, tag, shake, className = '', style, onTap }: CardButtonProps) {
  return (
    <m.button
      type="button"
      layout
      layoutId={cardKey(card)}
      className={`card ${isRed(card) ? 'card--red' : ''} card--${look} ${className}`}
      aria-label={cardLabel(card)}
      aria-pressed={look === 'raised'}
      aria-disabled={look === 'dim' ? true : undefined}
      data-card={cardKey(card)}
      style={style}
      onClick={onTap}
      animate={shake ? { x: [0, -9, 9, -6, 6, 0] } : { x: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: shake ? 0.45 : 0.2 }}
    >
      <CardFace card={card} />
      {tag && <span className="card__tag">{tag}</span>}
    </m.button>
  );
}

export function MiniCard({ card }: { card: Card }) {
  return (
    <span className={`card card--mini ${isRed(card) ? 'card--red' : ''}`} role="img" aria-label={cardLabel(card)}>
      <CardFace card={card} />
    </span>
  );
}

export function CardBack({ size = 'table' }: { size?: 'table' | 'mini' }) {
  return <span className={`back ${size === 'mini' ? 'back--mini' : ''}`} aria-hidden="true" />;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `src/game/Hand.tsx`**

```tsx
import type { CSSProperties } from 'react';
import { cardKey, type Card, type Suit } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { CardButton, type CardLook } from './Card';
import { marriageTag, sortHand } from './cards';

export interface HandProps {
  cards: Card[];
  /** cardKey of the raised card. */
  selected: string | null;
  isPlayable(card: Card): boolean;
  /** cardKey the coach recommends (thin accent outline). */
  recommended: string | null;
  /** Marriages already declared by the viewer (no tag for those). */
  declared: readonly Suit[];
  shake: string | null;
  onTap(card: Card): void;
}

/** The viewer's cards. Tapping only reports the tap — it never plays a card. */
export function Hand({ cards, selected, isPlayable, recommended, declared, shake, onTap }: HandProps) {
  const sorted = sortHand(cards);
  return (
    <div className="hand" role="group" aria-label="Your hand" style={{ '--n': sorted.length } as CSSProperties}>
      {sorted.map((card, i) => {
        const key = cardKey(card);
        const look: CardLook = key === selected ? 'raised' : !isPlayable(card) ? 'dim' : key === recommended ? 'recommended' : 'normal';
        return (
          <CardButton
            key={key}
            card={card}
            look={look}
            tag={marriageTag(card, cards, declared)}
            shake={shake === key}
            className="hand__card"
            style={{ '--i': i } as CSSProperties}
            onTap={() => onTap(card)}
          />
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
```

  - `Table({ view, pending, sweepTo })` — region "Table": talon (backs while bidding; face-up in decision when visible), trick cards (`role="img"`, named by label) positioned by seat spot, entering from the player's side and exiting toward `sweepTo`; the viewer's cards share `layoutId` with the hand; "Last trick" minis during tricks.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/game/seating.ts`**

```ts
import type { PlayerId } from '@tysiac/engine';

export type Spot = 'me' | 'left' | 'top' | 'right';

/** Screen position of every seat, clockwise from the viewer at the bottom. */
export function spotsFor(seatIds: readonly PlayerId[], me: PlayerId): Record<PlayerId, Spot> {
  const order: Spot[] = seatIds.length === 4 ? ['me', 'left', 'top', 'right'] : ['me', 'left', 'right'];
  const start = Math.max(0, seatIds.indexOf(me));
  const out: Record<PlayerId, Spot> = {};
  for (let k = 0; k < seatIds.length; k++) out[seatIds[(start + k) % seatIds.length]] = order[k];
  return out;
}

export function opponentIds(seatIds: readonly PlayerId[], me: PlayerId): PlayerId[] {
  const start = Math.max(0, seatIds.indexOf(me));
  return Array.from({ length: seatIds.length - 1 }, (_, k) => seatIds[(start + k + 1) % seatIds.length]);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/game/TopBar.tsx`**

```tsx
import { SUIT_SYMBOL, type PlayerView } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { who } from '../store/notes';

export function TopBar({ view, onMenu, onScores }: { view: PlayerView; onMenu(): void; onScores(): void }) {
  const r = view.round;
  let sub = '';
  if (r && view.phase === 'bidding') {
    sub = `${who(view, r.dealer)} ${r.dealer === view.me ? 'deal' : 'deals'} · bidding`;
  } else if (r) {
    sub = `${who(view, r.bid.high)} bid ${r.bid.amount}${r.trump ? ` · trump ${SUIT_SYMBOL[r.trump]}` : ''}`;
  }
  return (
    <header className="topbar">
      <button type="button" className="iconbtn" aria-label="Menu" onClick={onMenu}>
        ☰
      </button>
      <div className="topbar__info">
        <b className="display">{r ? `Round ${r.number}` : 'Tysiąc'}</b>
        <span>{sub}</span>
      </div>
      <button type="button" className="iconbtn topbar__score" aria-label="Score sheet" onClick={onScores}>
        <span className="display">{view.scores[view.me]?.total ?? 0}</span> ≡
      </button>
    </header>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/game/Opponents.tsx`**

```tsx
import { cardLabel, type Card, type PlayerId, type PlayerView } from '@tysiac/engine';
import type { RoomInfo, RoomSeat } from '../net/protocol';
import { CardBack, MiniCard } from './Card';
import { opponentIds } from './seating';

export function chipFor(view: PlayerView, id: PlayerId): { text: string; muted: boolean } | null {
  const r = view.round;
  if (!r || r.sittingOut === id) return null;
  if (view.phase === 'bidding') {
    if (r.bid.passed.includes(id)) return { text: 'pass', muted: true };
    return r.bid.high === id ? { text: String(r.bid.amount), muted: false } : null;
  }
  return r.bid.high === id ? { text: String(r.bid.amount), muted: false } : null;
}

export interface OpponentsProps {
  view: PlayerView;
  room: RoomInfo | null;
  given?: Partial<Record<PlayerId, Card>>;
  targetable?: boolean;
  onTarget?(p: PlayerId): void;
  onUngive?(p: PlayerId): void;
}

export function Opponents({ view, room, given = {}, targetable = false, onTarget, onUngive }: OpponentsProps) {
  const ids = opponentIds(view.seats.map(s => s.id), view.me);
  return (
    <div className="opps" data-count={ids.length}>
      {ids.map(id => (
        <Opponent
          key={id}
          id={id}
          view={view}
          seat={room?.seats.find((s): s is RoomSeat => s?.id === id) ?? null}
          given={given[id] ?? null}
          targetable={targetable}
          onTarget={onTarget}
          onUngive={onUngive}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

interface OpponentProps {
  id: PlayerId;
  view: PlayerView;
  seat: RoomSeat | null;
  given: Card | null;
  targetable: boolean;
  onTarget?(p: PlayerId): void;
  onUngive?(p: PlayerId): void;
}

function Opponent({ id, view, seat, given, targetable, onTarget, onUngive }: OpponentProps) {
  const s = view.seats.find(x => x.id === id)!;
  const r = view.round;
  const sittingOut = r?.sittingOut === id;
  const count = r?.handCounts[id] ?? 0;
  const chip = chipFor(view, id);
  const away = seat !== null && !seat.isBot && !seat.connected;
  const cls = `opp ${view.turn === id && !view.paused ? 'opp--turn' : ''} ${targetable && !given && !sittingOut ? 'opp--target' : ''}`;
  const body = (
    <>
      <span className="avatar">
        {s.avatar}
        {r?.dealer === id && <span className="avatar__dealer" title="Dealer">D</span>}
      </span>
      <span className="opp__name">
        {s.name}
        {seat?.coach && <span aria-label="coach on"> 🎓</span>}
      </span>
      <span className="opp__meta">
        <span className="opp__score">{view.scores[id]?.total ?? 0}</span>
        {chip && <span className={`chip ${chip.muted ? 'chip--muted' : ''}`}>{chip.text}</span>}
      </span>
      {sittingOut ? (
        <span className="opp__out">sits out</span>
      ) : (
        <span className="backs" aria-label={`${count} cards`}>
          {Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => (
            <CardBack key={i} size="mini" />
          ))}
        </span>
      )}
      {away && <span className="badge">{seat.botFor ? '🤖 bot playing' : 'away'}</span>}
    </>
  );
  if (targetable && !given && !sittingOut) {
    return (
      <button type="button" className={cls} data-seat={id} aria-label={`Give to ${s.name}`} onClick={() => onTarget?.(id)}>
        {body}
      </button>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className={cls} data-seat={id}>
      {body}
      {given && (
        <span className="opp__given">
          gets <MiniCard card={given} />
          <button type="button" className="iconbtn opp__ungive" aria-label={`Take back ${cardLabel(given)}`} onClick={() => onUngive?.(id)}>
            ✕
          </button>
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `src/game/Table.tsx`**

```tsx
import { AnimatePresence, m } from 'motion/react';
import { cardKey, cardLabel, type Card, type CompletedTrick, type PlayerId, type PlayerView, type TrickCard } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { CardBack, CardFace, MiniCard } from './Card';
import { isRed } from './cards';
import { spotsFor, type Spot } from './seating';
import './table.css';

const FROM: Record<Spot, { x: string; y: string }> = {
  me: { x: '0vw', y: '40vh' },
  left: { x: '-45vw', y: '-25vh' },
  top: { x: '0vw', y: '-35vh' },
  right: { x: '45vw', y: '-25vh' },
};

const variants = {
  enter: (from: Spot) => ({ opacity: 0, scale: 0.7, x: FROM[from].x, y: FROM[from].y }),
  rest: { opacity: 1, scale: 1, x: '0vw', y: '0vh' },
  gone: (to: Spot) => ({ opacity: 0, scale: 0.5, x: FROM[to].x, y: FROM[to].y }),
};

export interface TableProps {
  view: PlayerView;
  /** The viewer's optimistically played card. */
  pending: Card | null;
  /** Winner of the trick being swept away. */
  sweepTo: PlayerId | null;
}

export function Table({ view, pending, sweepTo }: TableProps) {
  const r = view.round;
  const spots = spotsFor(view.seats.map(s => s.id), view.me);
  const trick: TrickCard[] = r ? [...r.trick.cards, ...(pending ? [{ p: view.me, card: pending }] : [])] : [];
  const showTalon = !!r && (view.phase === 'bidding' || view.phase === 'decision');
  return (
    <section className="table" aria-label="Table">
      {showTalon && <Talon cards={view.phase === 'decision' ? (r?.talon ?? null) : null} />}
      <AnimatePresence custom={(sweepTo && spots[sweepTo]) || 'top'}>
        {trick.map(t => {
          const spot = spots[t.p] ?? 'top';
          const mine = t.p === view.me;
          return (
            <m.div
              key={cardKey(t.card)}
              layoutId={mine ? cardKey(t.card) : undefined}
              custom={spot}
              variants={variants}
              initial={mine ? false : 'enter'}
              animate="rest"
              exit="gone"
              transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 380, damping: 32 }}
              className={`card tcard tcard--${spot} ${isRed(t.card) ? 'card--red' : ''}`}
              role="img"
              aria-label={cardLabel(t.card)}
            >
              <CardFace card={t.card} />
            </m.div>
          );
        })}
      </AnimatePresence>
      {r?.lastTrick && view.phase === 'tricks' && <LastTrick trick={r.lastTrick} />}
    </section>
  );
}

function Talon({ cards }: { cards: Card[] | null }) {
  return (
    <div className="talon" aria-label="Talon">
      {cards
        ? cards.map(c => (
            <span key={cardKey(c)} className={`card ${isRed(c) ? 'card--red' : ''}`} role="img" aria-label={cardLabel(c)}>
              <CardFace card={c} />
            </span>
          ))
        : [0, 1, 2].map(i => <CardBack key={i} />)}
      <span className="talon__lbl">talon</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function LastTrick({ trick }: { trick: CompletedTrick }) {
  return (
    <div className="lasttrick" aria-label="Last trick">
      <span className="lbl">Last</span>
      {trick.cards.map(t => (
        <MiniCard key={cardKey(t.card)} card={t.card} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/src/ui/Sheet.tsx`, `apps/app/src/ui/sheet.css`, `apps/app/src/game/stats.ts`, `apps/app/src/game/ScoreSheet.tsx`, `apps/app/src/game/RoundResultSheet.tsx`, `apps/app/src/game/GameOverSheet.tsx`
- Test: `apps/app/src/game/Sheets.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `who` (Task 7); engine `RULES`, types; fixtures (Task 7).
- Produces:
  - `Sheet({ title, onClose?, children, footer? })` — portal into `document.body`, `role="dialog"` named by `title`, slides up (`m.section`), "Close" button + Escape + backdrop tap when `onClose` is given; body has `data-scroll-clip` (its own scroll; e2e ignores clipped children).

  - `roundHeadline(summary, view): string`; `RoundResultSheet({ summary, view, note, onContinue })` — dialog "Round N", button "Continue".
  - `GameOverSheet({ view, isHost, onRematch, onHome })` — dialog "Game over", winner line, per-player stats, "Rematch" (host) or a waiting note, "Home".

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { RoundSummary } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { ScoreSheet, zapisRows } from './ScoreSheet';
import { RoundResultSheet, roundHeadline } from './RoundResultSheet';
import { GameOverSheet } from './GameOverSheet';
import { gameStats } from './stats';
import { SEATS, findState, viewOf } from '../test/fixtures';

const mid = viewOf(findState(s => s.rounds.filter(r => r.outcome !== 'thrown').length >= 2, SEATS, 600));
const over = viewOf(findState(s => s.phase === 'gameEnd', SEATS, 5000));

describe('zapisRows', () => {
  it('has one row per round with running totals and the bid in the bidder cell', () => {
    const rows = zapisRows(mid);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(mid.rounds.length);
    const last = rows.at(-1)!;
    for (const c of last.cells) expect(c.total).toBe(mid.scores[c.id].total);
    rows.forEach((row, i) => {
      const r = mid.rounds[i];
      const bidderCell = row.cells.find(c => c.id === r.bidder)!;
      expect(bidderCell.bid).toBe(r.outcome === 'thrown' ? `${r.bid}✕` : String(r.bid));
      expect(bidderCell.failed).toBe(r.outcome === 'failed');
    });
  });
});

describe('ScoreSheet', () => {
  it('is a closable dialog with every player', async () => {
    const onClose = vi.fn();
    render(<ScoreSheet view={mid} onClose={onClose} />);
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Score sheet' });
    for (const s of mid.seats) expect(within(dialog).getByText(s.name, { exact: false })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Close' }));
    expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});

describe('RoundResultSheet', () => {
  const base: RoundSummary = mid.rounds.find(r => r.outcome !== 'thrown')!;
  const name = base.bidder === 'me' ? 'You' : mid.seats.find(s => s.id === base.bidder)!.name;

  it('headlines made, failed, thrown and wykładana rounds', () => {
    expect(roundHeadline({ ...base, outcome: 'made', wykladana: false }, mid)).toBe(`${name} made ${base.bid}`);
    expect(roundHeadline({ ...base, outcome: 'failed', wykladana: false }, mid)).toBe(`${name} failed ${base.bid}`);
    expect(roundHeadline({ ...base, outcome: 'thrown', wykladana: false }, mid)).toBe(`${name} threw in at ${base.bid}`);
    expect(roundHeadline({ ...base, outcome: 'made', wykladana: true }, mid)).toBe(`${name} won every trick — wykładana!`);
  });

  it('lists every player and continues', async () => {
    const onContinue = vi.fn();
    render(<RoundResultSheet summary={base} view={mid} note="Coach says hi" onContinue={onContinue} />);
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: `Round ${base.number}` });
    expect(within(dialog).getAllByRole('row')).toHaveLength(base.players.length + 1);
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('Coach says hi')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue' }));
    expect(onContinue).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});

describe('game over', () => {
  it('gameStats sorts by total and counts bids', () => {
    const stats = gameStats(over);
    expect(stats.map(s => s.total)).toEqual([...stats.map(s => s.total)].sort((a, b) => b - a));
    for (const s of stats) {
      const bids = over.rounds.filter(r => r.bidder === s.id && r.outcome !== 'thrown').length;
      expect(s.bidsMade + s.bidsFailed).toBe(bids);
    }
  });

  it('shows the winner; only the host can rematch', async () => {
    const onRematch = vi.fn();
    const { unmount } = render(<GameOverSheet view={over} isHost onRematch={onRematch} onHome={() => {}} />);
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Game over' });
    const winner = over.winner === 'me' ? 'You win!' : `${over.seats.find(s => s.id === over.winner)!.name} wins!`;
    expect(within(dialog).getByText(`🏆 ${winner}`)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Rematch' }));
    expect(onRematch).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    unmount();
    render(<GameOverSheet view={over} isHost={false} onRematch={onRematch} onHome={() => {}} />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Rematch' })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByText('Waiting for the host to start a rematch…')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/ui/Sheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { m } from 'motion/react';
import './sheet.css';

export interface SheetProps {
  title: string;
  onClose?: () => void;
  children: ReactNode;
  footer?: ReactNode;
}

export function Sheet({ title, onClose, children, footer }: SheetProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!onClose) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  return createPortal(
    <div
      className="sheet-backdrop"
      onClick={e => {
        if (onClose && e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <m.section
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={title}
        className="sheet"
        initial={{ y: '100%' }}
        animate={{ y: 0 }}
        transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 360, damping: 34 }}
      >
        <header className="sheet__head">
          <h2 className="display">{title}</h2>
          {onClose && (
            <button type="button" className="iconbtn" aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose}>
              ✕
            </button>
          )}
        </header>
        <div className="sheet__body" data-scroll-clip>
          {children}
        </div>
        {footer && <footer className="sheet__foot">{footer}</footer>}
      </m.section>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/game/stats.ts`**

```ts
import type { PlayerId, PlayerView } from '@tysiac/engine';

export interface PlayerStats {
  id: PlayerId;
  name: string;
  avatar: string;
  total: number;
  bidsMade: number;
  bidsFailed: number;
  marriagePoints: number;
  bestRound: number;
}

export function gameStats(view: PlayerView): PlayerStats[] {
  return view.seats
    .map(s => {
      const mine = view.rounds.flatMap(r => r.players.filter(p => p.playerId === s.id));
      return {
        id: s.id,
        name: s.name,
        avatar: s.avatar,
        total: view.scores[s.id]?.total ?? 0,
        bidsMade: view.rounds.filter(r => r.bidder === s.id && r.outcome === 'made').length,
        bidsFailed: view.rounds.filter(r => r.bidder === s.id && r.outcome === 'failed').length,
        marriagePoints: mine.reduce((n, p) => n + (p.sittingOut ? 0 : p.marriagePoints), 0),
        bestRound: mine.reduce((best, p) => Math.max(best, p.scoreChange), 0),
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `src/game/ScoreSheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import { RULES, type PlayerId, type PlayerView, type RoundOutcome } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { Sheet } from '../ui/Sheet';

export interface ZapisCell {
  id: PlayerId;
  total: number;
  change: number;
  bid: string | null;
  failed: boolean;
  barrel: boolean;
  sittingOut: boolean;
}
export interface ZapisRow {
  number: number;
  outcome: RoundOutcome;
  cells: ZapisCell[];
}

/** Round-by-round running totals, like the paper score sheet (zapis). */
export function zapisRows(view: PlayerView): ZapisRow[] {
  return view.rounds.map(r => ({
    number: r.number,
    outcome: r.outcome,
    cells: view.seats.map(s => {
      const p = r.players.find(x => x.playerId === s.id);
      const total = p?.newTotalScore ?? 0;
      return {
        id: s.id,
        total,
        change: p?.scoreChange ?? 0,
        bid: r.bidder === s.id ? (r.outcome === 'thrown' ? `${r.bid}✕` : String(r.bid)) : null,
        failed: r.bidder === s.id && r.outcome === 'failed',
        barrel: total >= RULES.barrelAt && total < RULES.winAt,
        sittingOut: p?.sittingOut ?? false,
      };
    }),
  }));
}

export function ScoreSheet({ view, onClose }: { view: PlayerView; onClose(): void }) {
  const rows = zapisRows(view);
  return (
    <Sheet title="Score sheet" onClose={onClose}>
      <table className="zapis">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>#</th>
            {view.seats.map(s => (
              <th key={s.id}>
                {s.avatar} {s.name}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map(row => (
            <tr key={row.number}>
              <td>{row.number}</td>
              {row.cells.map(c => (
                <td key={c.id} className={`${c.failed ? 'zapis__failed' : ''} ${c.barrel ? 'zapis__barrel' : ''}`}>
                  {c.total}
                  {c.bid && <small>bid {c.bid}</small>}
                  {c.sittingOut && <small>dealt</small>}
                </td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      {rows.length === 0 && <p>No rounds yet.</p>}
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `src/game/RoundResultSheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import { RULES, type PlayerView, type RoundSummary } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { Sheet } from '../ui/Sheet';
import { who } from '../store/notes';

export function roundHeadline(summary: RoundSummary, view: PlayerView): string {
  const name = who(view, summary.bidder);
  if (summary.outcome === 'thrown') return `${name} threw in at ${summary.bid}`;
  if (summary.wykladana) return `${name} won every trick — wykładana!`;
  return summary.outcome === 'made' ? `${name} made ${summary.bid}` : `${name} failed ${summary.bid}`;
}

const signed = (n: number) => (n > 0 ? `+${n}` : String(n));

export interface RoundResultSheetProps {
  summary: RoundSummary;
  view: PlayerView;
  note: string | null;
  onContinue(): void;
}

export function RoundResultSheet({ summary, view, note, onContinue }: RoundResultSheetProps) {
  return (
    <Sheet
      title={`Round ${summary.number}`}
      footer={
        <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" onClick={onContinue}>
          Continue
        </button>
      }
    >
      <p className="result__headline display">{roundHeadline(summary, view)}</p>
      <table className="result">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Player</th>
            <th>Tricks</th>
            <th>Marriages</th>
            <th>Round</th>
            <th>Total</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {summary.players.map(p => (
            <tr key={p.playerId}>
              <td>{who(view, p.playerId)}</td>
              <td>{p.sittingOut ? '—' : p.trickPoints}</td>
              <td>{p.marriagePoints || ''}</td>
              <td className={p.scoreChange < 0 ? 'neg' : ''}>{signed(p.scoreChange)}</td>
              <td>
                {p.newTotalScore}
                {p.newTotalScore >= RULES.barrelAt && p.newTotalScore < RULES.winAt ? ' 🛢' : ''}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      {note && (
        <p className="coachnote">
          <b>Coach:</b> {note}
        </p>
      )}
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write `src/game/GameOverSheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import type { PlayerView } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { Sheet } from '../ui/Sheet';
import { gameStats } from './stats';

export interface GameOverSheetProps {
  view: PlayerView;
  isHost: boolean;
  onRematch(): void;
  onHome(): void;
}

export function GameOverSheet({ view, isHost, onRematch, onHome }: GameOverSheetProps) {
  const stats = gameStats(view);
  const winner = view.seats.find(s => s.id === view.winner);
  const headline = view.winner === view.me ? 'You win!' : `${winner?.name ?? 'Someone'} wins!`;
  return (
    <Sheet
      title="Game over"
      footer={
        <>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" onClick={onHome}>
            Home
          </button>
          {isHost && (
            <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" onClick={onRematch}>
              Rematch
            </button>
          )}
        </>
      }
    >
      <p className="result__headline display">🏆 {headline}</p>
      <table className="stats">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Player</th>
            <th>Score</th>
            <th>Made</th>
            <th>Failed</th>
            <th>Marr.</th>
            <th>Best</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {stats.map(s => (
            <tr key={s.id}>
              <td>
                {s.avatar} {s.name}
              </td>
              <td>{s.total}</td>
              <td>{s.bidsMade}</td>
              <td>{s.bidsFailed}</td>
              <td>{s.marriagePoints}</td>
              <td>{s.bestRound}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      {!isHost && <p className="coachnote">Waiting for the host to start a rematch…</p>}
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/ui/ProfileForm.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { AVATARS, NAME_MAX, cleanName } from '../store/prefs';

export interface ProfileFormProps {
  initialName: string;
  initialAvatar: string;
  submitLabel: string;
  onSubmit(name: string, avatar: string): void;
}

export function ProfileForm({ initialName, initialAvatar, submitLabel, onSubmit }: ProfileFormProps) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(initialName);
  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState(initialAvatar || AVATARS[0]);
  const clean = cleanName(name);
  return (
    <form
      className="profile"
      onSubmit={e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (clean) onSubmit(clean, avatar);
      }}
    >
      <label className="field">
        <span className="lbl">Your name</span>
        <input
          name="name"
          value={name}
          maxLength={NAME_MAX * 2}
          autoComplete="nickname"
          autoCapitalize="words"
          enterKeyHint="done"
          onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
        />
      </label>
      <fieldset className="avatars">
        <legend className="lbl">Avatar</legend>
        {AVATARS.map(a => (
          <button key={a} type="button" className="avatar avatar--pick" aria-label={`Avatar ${a}`} aria-pressed={a === avatar} onClick={() => setAvatar(a)}>
            {a}
          </button>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      <button type="submit" className="btn btn--pri" disabled={!clean}>
        {submitLabel}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

(`maxLength` is twice `NAME_MAX` so emoji — two UTF-16 units each — still fit; `cleanName` enforces 16 code points.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/screens/SettingsSheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import { Sheet } from '../ui/Sheet';
import { ProfileForm } from '../ui/ProfileForm';
import { usePrefs } from '../store/prefs';
import { THEMES } from '../theme/theme';

export interface SettingsSheetProps {
  /** Name/avatar editing (text input) — only outside the Game screen. */
  allowProfile: boolean;
  coachOn: boolean;
  onCoachChange?(on: boolean): void;
  onRules(): void;
  onClose(): void;
}

export function SettingsSheet({ allowProfile, coachOn, onCoachChange, onRules, onClose }: SettingsSheetProps) {
  const p = usePrefs();
  return (
    <Sheet title="Settings" onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="settings">
        <div className="lbl">Theme</div>
        <div className="themes" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Theme">
          {THEMES.map(t => (
            <button key={t.id} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={p.theme === t.id} className="theme-pick" onClick={() => p.setTheme(t.id)}>
              <span className="theme-pick__sw" aria-hidden="true">
                {t.swatch.map(c => (
                  <i key={c} style={{ background: c }} />
                ))}
              </span>
              {t.name}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <button type="button" role="switch" aria-checked={p.sound} className="switch" onClick={() => p.setSound(!p.sound)}>
          Sound
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          role="switch"
          aria-checked={coachOn}
          className="switch"
          onClick={() => {
            p.setCoach(!coachOn);
            onCoachChange?.(!coachOn);
          }}
        >
          Coach hints
        </button>
        <div className="lbl">Animation speed</div>
        <div className="seg" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Animation speed">
          {(['normal', 'fast'] as const).map(sp => (
            <button key={sp} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={p.speed === sp} onClick={() => p.setSpeed(sp)}>
              {sp === 'normal' ? 'Normal' : 'Fast'}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        {allowProfile && (
          <>
            <div className="lbl">Profile</div>
            <ProfileForm initialName={p.name} initialAvatar={p.avatar} submitLabel="Save" onSubmit={(n, a) => p.setProfile(n, a)} />
          </>
        )}
        <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" onClick={onRules}>
          Rules
        </button>
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/screens/RulesSheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import { RULES } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { Sheet } from '../ui/Sheet';

export function RulesSheet({ onClose }: { onClose(): void }) {
  const pts = RULES.cardPoints;
  const mv = RULES.marriageValues;
  const share = Math.floor(RULES.throwPool / 2);
  return (
    <Sheet title="Rules" onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="rules">
        <h3>Cards</h3>
        <p>
          24 cards: 9, 10, J, Q, K, A in ♣ ♦ ♥ ♠. Points: A {pts.A}, 10 {pts['10']}, K {pts.K}, Q {pts.Q}, J {pts.J}, 9 {pts['9']} —{' '}
          {RULES.pointsPerRound} per round. Strength: A &gt; 10 &gt; K &gt; Q &gt; J &gt; 9.
        </p>
        <h3>Marriages</h3>
        <p>
          K + Q of one suit: ♥ {mv.hearts}, ♦ {mv.diamonds}, ♣ {mv.clubs}, ♠ {mv.spades}. Lead the Q while holding its K to declare it — that suit
          becomes trump at once. Marriage points count only if you win at least one trick.
        </p>
        <h3>Dealing</h3>
        <p>
          {RULES.handSize} cards each and {RULES.talonSize} to the talon. With 4 players the dealer sits out and scores the talon's marriages plus{' '}
          {RULES.talonAcePoints} per ace in it. A hand under {RULES.redealBelow} points with no marriage means a redeal.
        </p>
        <h3>Bidding</h3>
        <p>
          The player left of the dealer starts at {RULES.autoBid}. On your turn: bid {RULES.bidStep} more, or pass. Your max is {RULES.maxBidBase} + your
          marriages. When two players have passed, the high bidder wins.
        </p>
        <h3>Talon, throw-in and hand-out</h3>
        <p>
          At {RULES.autoBid} only the bidder sees the talon; above that everyone does. The bidder may throw in: at {RULES.autoBid} nobody scores; above it the
          bidder loses the bid and the others get {share} each (0 on the barrel). Otherwise the bidder gives one card to each opponent.
        </p>
        <h3>Tricks</h3>
        <p>
          The bidder leads first. Follow suit if you can. Only the first player after the leader must also beat the lead card if possible. If you can't follow,
          play anything. A trump beats every other suit.
        </p>
        <h3>Scoring</h3>
        <p>
          Points round to 10 (a remainder of {RULES.roundUpFrom} or more rounds up). The bidder needs at least the bid in raw points, or loses the bid. On the
          barrel ({RULES.barrelAt}–{RULES.winAt - 1}) you only score as the bidder. First to {RULES.winAt} wins.
        </p>
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Sheet } from '../ui/Sheet';

export interface MenuSheetProps {
  paused: boolean;
  onPause(): void;
  onResume(): void;
  onSettings(): void;
  onRules(): void;
  onLeave(): void;
  onClose(): void;
}

export function MenuSheet({ paused, onPause, onResume, onSettings, onRules, onLeave, onClose }: MenuSheetProps) {
  return (
    <Sheet title="Menu" onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="menu">
        {paused ? (
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" onClick={onResume}>
            Resume game
          </button>
        ) : (
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" onClick={onPause}>
            Pause game
          </button>
        )}
        <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" onClick={onSettings}>
          Settings
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" onClick={onRules}>
          Rules
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" onClick={onLeave}>
          Leave to home
        </button>
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

### Task 13: Action zone — bidding, decision, hand-out, trick, waiting/away/paused panels and the coach line

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/src/game/panels.tsx`, `apps/app/src/game/CoachLine.tsx`, `apps/app/src/game/WhySheet.tsx`, `apps/app/src/game/ActionZone.tsx`, `apps/app/src/game/useNow.ts`, `apps/app/src/game/zone.css`
- Test: `apps/app/src/game/ActionZone.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: engine `RULES`, `SUIT_SYMBOL`, `cardLabel`, `marriagesInHand`, types (`Action`, `Advice`, `Card`, `PlayerId`, `PlayerView`); `who` (Task 7); `AWAY_BOT_AFTER_MS` (Task 4); `Sheet` (Task 11); `RoomInfo`, `RoomSeat`.
- Produces:
  - `zonePhase(view, idle): 'paused' | 'over' | 'wait' | Phase` — the `data-phase` value (the phase name only when it is the viewer's turn and nothing is animating).
  - `throwCost(view): string` — `'Throw in: nobody scores, new deal.'` at 100, else `'Throw in: you −N, others +60 each (0 on the barrel).'`.
  - `interface ActionZoneProps { view; room; idle; busy; selected: Card | null; given: Partial<Record<PlayerId, Card>>; hint: string | null; coach: boolean; advice: Advice | null; note: string | null; now: number; onAct(a: Action): void; onPlay(card: Card): void; onConfirmHandout(): void; onBotFor(p: PlayerId): void; onWhy(): void }`; `ActionZone(props)` — `<section class="zone" data-phase=…>` with reserved height (CSS), panels per phase, waiting line ("Bot Basia is bidding…"), away line ("NAME is away" → after 30 s button "Let a bot play for NAME"), paused panel ("Resume"), sit-out note, hint line, and the coach line (or a plain note when the coach is off).
  - `CoachLine({ advice, note, onWhy })` ("Coach: …" + "Why?"); `WhySheet({ advice, onClose })` (dialog "Why?"); `useNow(active: boolean, offset: number): number` (server-time clock ticking every second while `active`).

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { cardLabel, createGame, turnOf, type Action, type PlayerView } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { ActionZone, throwCost, type ActionZoneProps } from './ActionZone';
import { SEATS4, findState, roomInfo, viewOf } from '../test/fixtures';

function props(view: PlayerView, over: Partial<ActionZoneProps> = {}): ActionZoneProps {
  return {
    view, room: roomInfo(), idle: true, busy: false, selected: null, given: {}, hint: null, coach: false, advice: null, note: null, now: 0,
    onAct: vi.fn(), onPlay: vi.fn(), onConfirmHandout: vi.fn(), onBotFor: vi.fn(), onWhy: vi.fn(), ...over,
  };
}
const zone = () => document.querySelector('.zone') as HTMLElement;

const bidMine = viewOf(findState(s => s.phase === 'bidding' && turnOf(s) === 'me' && s.round!.bid.amount < 120));
const bidOther = viewOf(findState(s => s.phase === 'bidding' && turnOf(s) === 'b'));
const decision = viewOf(findState(s => s.phase === 'decision' && turnOf(s) === 'me'));
const handout = viewOf(findState(s => s.phase === 'handout' && turnOf(s) === 'me'));
const tricks = viewOf(findState(s => s.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(s) === 'me'));

describe('ActionZone: bidding', () => {
  it('shows the current bid, my max, Pass and Bid +10', async () => {
    const p = props(bidMine);
    render(<ActionZone {...p} />);
    const r = bidMine.round!;
    expect(zone().dataset.phase).toBe('bidding');
    expect(screen.getByText(String(r.bid.amount), { exact: false })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(String(r.maxBid))).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Pass' }));
    expect(p.onAct).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ type: 'pass' });
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `Bid ${r.bid.amount + 10}` }));
    expect(p.onAct).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ type: 'bid', amount: r.bid.amount + 10 });
  });

  it('hides Bid when the next bid is over my max', () => {
    const legal: Action[] = [{ type: 'pass' }, { type: 'pause' }];
    render(<ActionZone {...props({ ...bidMine, legal })} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Pass' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /^Bid / })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('waits while animations play or it is someone else', () => {
    const { unmount } = render(<ActionZone {...props(bidMine, { idle: false })} />);
    expect(zone().dataset.phase).toBe('wait');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Pass' })).toBeNull();
    unmount();
    render(<ActionZone {...props(bidOther)} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Bot Basia is bidding…')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('ActionZone: away and paused', () => {
  it('counts down, then offers a bot for an away player', async () => {
    const room = roomInfo();
    room.seats[1] = { ...room.seats[1]!, isBot: false, connected: false, awaySince: 100_000 };
    const { unmount } = render(<ActionZone {...props(bidOther, { room, now: 110_000 })} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Bot Basia is away')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('bot option in 20 s')).toBeInTheDocument();
    unmount();
    const p = props(bidOther, { room, now: 131_000 });
    render(<ActionZone {...p} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Let a bot play for Bot Basia' }));
    expect(p.onBotFor).toHaveBeenCalledWith('b');
  });

  it('paused: anyone can resume', async () => {
    const v: PlayerView = { ...bidOther, paused: { by: 'b', at: 0 }, legal: [{ type: 'resume' }] };
    const p = props(v);
    render(<ActionZone {...p} />);
    expect(zone().dataset.phase).toBe('paused');
    expect(screen.getByText('Bot Basia paused the game.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Resume' }));
    expect(p.onAct).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ type: 'resume' });
  });

  it('sitting-out dealer sees a sit-out note and no buttons', () => {
    const g = createGame({ seed: 's4', seats: SEATS4, maxPlayers: 4, firstDealer: 0 });
    const v = viewOf(g);
    expect(v.round!.sittingOut).toBe('me');
    render(<ActionZone {...props(v, { room: roomInfo({}, SEATS4) })} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('You deal and sit out this round.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Pass' })).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('ActionZone: decision', () => {
  it('Play / Throw in with the cost of throwing', async () => {
    const p = props(decision);
    render(<ActionZone {...p} />);
    expect(zone().dataset.phase).toBe('decision');
    expect(screen.getByText(throwCost(decision))).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Throw in' }));
    expect(p.onAct).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ type: 'throw' });
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Play' }));
    expect(p.onAct).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ type: 'play' });
  });

  it('throwCost at 100 and above', () => {
    const at = (amount: number): PlayerView => ({ ...decision, round: { ...decision.round!, bid: { ...decision.round!.bid, amount } } });
    expect(throwCost(at(100))).toBe('Throw in: nobody scores, new deal.');
    expect(throwCost(at(120))).toBe('Throw in: you −120, others +60 each (0 on the barrel).');
  });
});

describe('ActionZone: hand-out', () => {
  it('counts given cards and enables Confirm when both are chosen', async () => {
    const [x, y] = handout.round!.active.filter(p => p !== 'me');
    const [c1, c2] = handout.round!.hand;
    const { unmount } = render(<ActionZone {...props(handout, { given: { [x]: c1 } })} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Handing out · 1 of 2')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm hand-out' })).toBeDisabled();
    unmount();
    const p = props(handout, { given: { [x]: c1, [y]: c2 } });
    render(<ActionZone {...p} />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm hand-out' }));
    expect(p.onConfirmHandout).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});

describe('ActionZone: tricks', () => {
  it('Play is disabled until a card is raised, then names it', async () => {
    const { unmount } = render(<ActionZone {...props(tricks)} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Play' })).toBeDisabled();
    unmount();
    const card = tricks.round!.hand[0];
    const p = props(tricks, { selected: card, hint: 'You must follow ♦' });
    render(<ActionZone {...p} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('You must follow ♦')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `Play ${cardLabel(card)}` }));
    expect(p.onPlay).toHaveBeenCalledWith(card);
  });
});

describe('ActionZone: coach', () => {
  it('shows the advice headline with Why?, or the event note', async () => {
    const advice = { action: { type: 'pass' } as Action, headline: 'Pass — your hand is weak.', reasons: ['Only 21 points', 'No marriage'] };
    const p = props(bidMine, { coach: true, advice });
    const { unmount } = render(<ActionZone {...p} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Pass — your hand is weak.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Why?' }));
    expect(p.onWhy).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    unmount();
    render(<ActionZone {...props(bidOther, { coach: true, advice, note: 'Bot Zenek won the bid at 110.' })} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Pass — your hand is weak.')).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByText('Bot Zenek won the bid at 110.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('without the coach a public note still shows, without "Coach:"', () => {
    render(<ActionZone {...props(bidOther, { note: 'Bot Basia declared the ♥ marriage: +100. ♥ are trumps.' })} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Bot Basia declared the ♥ marriage: +100. ♥ are trumps.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Coach:')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```
