- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/game/useNow.ts`**

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

/** Server-time "now" (local clock + offset), ticking every second while `active`. */
export function useNow(active: boolean, offset: number): number {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now() + offset);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return;
    setNow(Date.now() + offset);
    const t = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now() + offset), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [active, offset]);
  return now;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/game/panels.tsx`**

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { RULES, SUIT_SYMBOL, cardLabel, marriagesInHand, type Action, type Card, type PlayerId, type PlayerView } from '@tysiac/engine';
import type { RoomInfo, RoomSeat } from '../net/protocol';
import { AWAY_BOT_AFTER_MS } from '../net/connection';
import { who } from '../store/notes';

const VERB: Partial<Record<PlayerView['phase'], string>> = {
  bidding: 'is bidding',
  decision: 'is deciding: play or throw in',
  handout: 'is handing out cards',
  tricks: 'is playing',
};

export function WaitLine({ view, room, now, onBotFor }: { view: PlayerView; room: RoomInfo | null; now: number; onBotFor(p: PlayerId): void }) {
  const p = view.turn;
  if (!p || p === view.me) return <p className="zone__wait">…</p>;
  const seat = room?.seats.find((s): s is RoomSeat => s?.id === p) ?? null;
  if (seat && !seat.isBot && !seat.connected && !seat.botFor) {
    const awayFor = now - (seat.awaySince ?? now);
    const left = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((AWAY_BOT_AFTER_MS - awayFor) / 1000));
    return (
      <div className="zone__wait">
        <span>{seat.name} is away</span>
        {left === 0 ? (
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" onClick={() => onBotFor(p)}>
            Let a bot play for {seat.name}
          </button>
        ) : (
          <span className="zone__muted">bot option in {left} s</span>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <p className="zone__wait">
      {who(view, p)} {VERB[view.phase] ?? 'is thinking'}…
    </p>
  );
}

interface PanelProps {
  view: PlayerView;
  myTurn: boolean;
  busy: boolean;
  wait: ReactNode;
}

export function BidPanel({ view, myTurn, busy, wait, onAct }: PanelProps & { onAct(a: Action): void }) {
  const r = view.round!;
  const bid = view.legal.find((a): a is Extract<Action, { type: 'bid' }> => a.type === 'bid');
  const marr = marriagesInHand(r.hand);
  const why = marr.length
    ? `(${RULES.maxBidBase} + ${marr.map(s => SUIT_SYMBOL[s]).join(' ')} marriage${marr.length > 1 ? 's' : ''})`
    : '(no marriage)';
  return (
    <>
      <div className="row">
        <div>
          <div className="lbl">Current bid</div>
          <div className="zone__big display">
            {r.bid.amount} <small>· {who(view, r.bid.high)}</small>
          </div>
        </div>
        {r.maxBid > 0 && (
          <div className="zone__max">
            Your max: <b>{r.maxBid}</b>
            <br />
            {why}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="zone__buttons">
        {myTurn ? (
          <>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" disabled={busy} onClick={() => onAct({ type: 'pass' })}>
              Pass
            </button>
            {bid && (
              <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" disabled={busy} onClick={() => onAct(bid)}>
                Bid {bid.amount}
              </button>
            )}
          </>
        ) : (
          wait
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

export function throwCost(view: PlayerView): string {
  const r = view.round!;
  const amount = r.bid.amount;
  if (amount <= RULES.autoBid) return 'Throw in: nobody scores, new deal.';
  const share = Math.floor(RULES.throwPool / Math.max(1, r.active.length - 1));
  return `Throw in: you −${amount}, others +${share} each (0 on the barrel).`;
}

export function DecisionPanel({ view, myTurn, busy, wait, onAct }: PanelProps & { onAct(a: Action): void }) {
  const r = view.round!;
  return (
    <>
      <div className="lbl">
        {who(view, r.bid.high)} won at {r.bid.amount}
      </div>
      {myTurn ? (
        <>
          <p className="zone__text">
            Play this hand or throw in? <span className="zone__muted">{throwCost(view)}</span>
          </p>
          <div className="zone__buttons">
            <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" disabled={busy} onClick={() => onAct({ type: 'throw' })}>
              Throw in
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" disabled={busy} onClick={() => onAct({ type: 'play' })}>
              Play
            </button>
          </div>
        </>
      ) : (
        <div className="zone__buttons">{wait}</div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

export function HandoutPanel(props: PanelProps & { given: Partial<Record<PlayerId, Card>>; selected: Card | null; onConfirm(): void }) {
  const { view, myTurn, busy, wait, given, selected, onConfirm } = props;
  const recipients = view.round!.active.filter(p => p !== view.me);
  const done = recipients.filter(p => given[p]).length;
  if (!myTurn) {
    return (
      <>
        <div className="lbl">Hand-out</div>
        <div className="zone__buttons">{wait}</div>
      </>
    );
  }
  const text =
    done === recipients.length
      ? 'Both cards chosen. Confirm, or tap ✕ to change.'
      : selected
        ? `Now tap who gets ${cardLabel(selected)}.`
        : 'Tap a card, then tap who gets it.';
  return (
    <>
      <div className="lbl">
        Handing out · {done} of {recipients.length}
      </div>
      <p className="zone__text">{text}</p>
      <div className="zone__buttons">
        <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" disabled={busy || done < recipients.length} onClick={onConfirm}>
          Confirm hand-out
        </button>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

export function TrickPanel({ view, myTurn, busy, wait, selected, onPlay }: PanelProps & { selected: Card | null; onPlay(card: Card): void }) {
  const r = view.round!;
  const trump = r.trump ? ` · trump ${SUIT_SYMBOL[r.trump]}` : '';
  const trickNo = Object.values(r.tricksTaken).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + 1;
  return (
    <>
      <div className="lbl">{myTurn ? `Your turn${trump}` : `Trick ${trickNo}${trump}`}</div>
      <div className="zone__buttons">
        {myTurn ? (
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" disabled={busy || !selected} onClick={() => selected && onPlay(selected)}>
            {selected ? `Play ${cardLabel(selected)}` : 'Play'}
          </button>
        ) : (
          wait
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

export function PausedPanel({ view, onAct }: { view: PlayerView; onAct(a: Action): void }) {
  const by = view.paused?.by;
  const text = by === view.me ? 'You paused the game.' : `${by ? who(view, by) : 'Someone'} paused the game.`;
  const canResume = view.legal.some(a => a.type === 'resume');
  return (
    <>
      <p className="zone__text">{text}</p>
      {canResume && (
        <div className="zone__buttons">
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" onClick={() => onAct({ type: 'resume' })}>
            Resume
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
            <b>Coach:</b> {text}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `src/game/ActionZone.tsx`**

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { Action, Advice, Card, PlayerId, PlayerView } from '@tysiac/engine';
import type { RoomInfo } from '../net/protocol';
import { BidPanel, DecisionPanel, HandoutPanel, PausedPanel, TrickPanel, WaitLine } from './panels';
import { CoachLine } from './CoachLine';
import './zone.css';

export { throwCost } from './panels';

export interface ActionZoneProps {
  view: PlayerView;
  room: RoomInfo | null;
  idle: boolean;
  busy: boolean;
  selected: Card | null;
  given: Partial<Record<PlayerId, Card>>;
  hint: string | null;
  coach: boolean;
  advice: Advice | null;
  note: string | null;
  now: number;
  onAct(a: Action): void;
  onPlay(card: Card): void;
  onConfirmHandout(): void;
  onBotFor(p: PlayerId): void;
  onWhy(): void;
}

export function zonePhase(view: PlayerView, idle: boolean): string {
  if (view.paused) return 'paused';
  if (view.phase === 'gameEnd') return 'over';
  if (!idle || view.turn !== view.me) return 'wait';
  return view.phase;
}

export function ActionZone(p: ActionZoneProps) {
  const { view } = p;
  const phase = zonePhase(view, p.idle);
  const myTurn = phase === view.phase;
  const wait = <WaitLine view={view} room={p.room} now={p.now} onBotFor={p.onBotFor} />;
  let body: ReactNode;
  if (view.paused) body = <PausedPanel view={view} onAct={p.onAct} />;
  else if (view.phase === 'gameEnd') body = <p className="zone__text">Game over</p>;
  else if (view.round?.sittingOut === view.me) {
    body = (
      <>
        <p className="zone__text">You deal and sit out this round.</p>
        {wait}
      </>
    );
  } else {
    switch (view.phase) {
      case 'bidding':
        body = <BidPanel view={view} myTurn={myTurn} busy={p.busy} wait={wait} onAct={p.onAct} />;
        break;
      case 'decision':
        body = <DecisionPanel view={view} myTurn={myTurn} busy={p.busy} wait={wait} onAct={p.onAct} />;
        break;
      case 'handout':
        body = <HandoutPanel view={view} myTurn={myTurn} busy={p.busy} wait={wait} given={p.given} selected={p.selected} onConfirm={p.onConfirmHandout} />;
        break;
      case 'tricks':
        body = <TrickPanel view={view} myTurn={myTurn} busy={p.busy} wait={wait} selected={p.selected} onPlay={p.onPlay} />;
        break;
    }
  }
  return (
    <section className="zone panel" data-phase={phase} aria-label="Actions">
      <div className="zone__main">{body}</div>
      {p.hint && (
        <p className="zone__hint" role="status">
          {p.hint}
        </p>
      )}
      {p.coach ? (
        <CoachLine advice={myTurn ? p.advice : null} note={p.note} onWhy={p.onWhy} />
      ) : (
        p.note && <p className="zone__note">{p.note}</p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```css
.zone {
  grid-area: zone;
  min-height: 0;
  height: 100%;
  padding: 10px 12px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.zone__main { flex: 1; min-height: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center; gap: 6px; }
.zone__big { font-size: 20px; font-weight: 700; line-height: 1.1; }
.zone__big small { font: 500 12px 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif; opacity: 0.7; }
.zone__max { text-align: right; font-size: 11px; opacity: 0.85; line-height: 1.35; }
.zone__max b { color: var(--accent); }
.zone__buttons { display: flex; gap: 8px; min-height: 44px; align-items: center; }
.zone__buttons .btn { flex: 1; }
.zone__buttons .btn--pri { flex: 1.4; }
.zone__text { margin: 0; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.35; }
.zone__muted { opacity: 0.7; font-size: 12px; }
.zone__wait { margin: 0; font-size: 13px; opacity: 0.9; min-height: 44px; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.zone__hint, .zone__note { margin: 0; font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.3; color: var(--accent-soft); }
.coachline {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 6px;
  padding-top: 6px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--chip-muted-bg);
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 1.35;
  min-height: calc(12px * 1.35 + 6px);
}
.coachline__text { flex: 1; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 2; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden; }
.coachline b { color: var(--accent-soft); }
.why { padding-left: 18px; line-height: 1.5; }
```

- Consumes: `useRoom`, `coachOn` (Task 8); `isIdle` (Task 7); `TopBar`, `Opponents`, `Table` (Task 10); `Hand` (Task 9); `ActionZone`, `WhySheet`, `useNow` (Task 13); `ScoreSheet`, `RoundResultSheet`, `GameOverSheet` (Task 11); `MenuSheet`, `SettingsSheet`, `RulesSheet` (Task 12); engine `advise`, `explainIllegal`, `cardEquals`, `cardKey`.
- Produces:
  - `selection.ts`: `useSelection` zustand store `{ selected: string | null; given: Record<PlayerId, Card>; hint: string | null; select(key); give(p, card); ungive(p); setHint(h); reset() }` (reset whenever the round or phase changes).
  - `motionFeatures.ts`: `export default domMax` (dynamically imported by `LazyMotion` in Task 16).
  - `screens/Game.tsx`: `export default function Game()` — renders `<main class="game" data-phase=… data-seq=…>` (`data-seq` = latest `view.seq`, used by e2e to wait for the server) with TopBar, Opponents, Table, ActionZone, Hand, and the sheets (round result → "Continue"; game over → "Rematch"/"Home"; score sheet; menu → pause/resume/settings/rules/leave; settings without profile editing; "Why?"). Tap rules: tricks → legal card toggles raise, illegal shows `explainIllegal` reason; hand-out → tap card, tap opponent; Play/Confirm commit.

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { cardEquals, cardLabel, explainIllegal, turnOf, type Action, type Card, type GameState } from '@tysiac/engine';
import Game from './Game';
import { useRoom } from '../store/room';
import { useSelection } from '../game/selection';
import { FakeConnection } from '../test/fakes';
import { findState, stateMsg, viewOf, welcomeMsg } from '../test/fixtures';

function join(s: GameState): FakeConnection {
  const conn = new FakeConnection();
  useRoom.getState().attach(conn);
  conn.push(welcomeMsg());
  conn.push(stateMsg(s));
  return conn;
}
const legalCards = (s: GameState): Card[] => viewOf(s).legal.flatMap(a => (a.type === 'card' ? [a.card] : []));
const hand = () => screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Your hand' });

beforeEach(() => {
  useRoom.getState().detach();
  useSelection.getState().reset();
});

describe('Game screen: tricks', () => {
  it('tapping a card only raises it; Play commits and the card moves to the table at once', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const s0 = findState(s => s.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(s) === 'me');
    const conn = join(s0);
    render(<Game />);
    const label = cardLabel(legalCards(s0)[0]);
    await user.click(within(hand()).getByRole('button', { name: label }));
    expect(within(hand()).getByRole('button', { name: label })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
    expect(conn.acts()).toHaveLength(0);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `Play ${label}` }));
    expect(conn.acts()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(within(hand()).queryByRole('button', { name: label })).toBeNull();
    expect(within(screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Table' })).getByRole('img', { name: label })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('double-clicking Play sends one act', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const s0 = findState(s => s.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(s) === 'me');
    const conn = join(s0);
    render(<Game />);
    const label = cardLabel(legalCards(s0)[0]);
    await user.click(within(hand()).getByRole('button', { name: label }));
    await user.dblClick(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `Play ${label}` }));
    expect(conn.acts()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('tapping an illegal card explains why and does not raise it', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const s0 = findState(s => s.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(s) === 'me' && legalCards(s).length < s.round!.hands.me.length);
    const conn = join(s0);
    render(<Game />);
    const illegal = viewOf(s0).round!.hand.find(c => !legalCards(s0).some(l => cardEquals(l, c)))!;
    const button = within(hand()).getByRole('button', { name: cardLabel(illegal) });
    expect(button).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    await user.click(button);
    expect(screen.getByText(explainIllegal(viewOf(s0), illegal)!)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(button).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    expect(conn.acts()).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('a rejected card comes back to the hand', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const s0 = findState(s => s.phase === 'tricks' && turnOf(s) === 'me');
    const conn = join(s0);
    render(<Game />);
    const label = cardLabel(legalCards(s0)[0]);
    await user.click(within(hand()).getByRole('button', { name: label }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `Play ${label}` }));
    conn.push({ t: 'reject', id: conn.acts()[0].id, code: 'illegalCard', message: 'Nope' });
    expect(await within(hand()).findByRole('button', { name: label })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Nope')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('Game screen: other phases', () => {
  it('bidding: Pass sends a pass', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const conn = join(findState(s => s.phase === 'bidding' && turnOf(s) === 'me'));
    render(<Game />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Pass' }));
    expect(conn.acts()[0].action).toEqual({ type: 'pass' });
  });

  it('hand-out: tap a card, tap an opponent, take back, then confirm both', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const conn = join(findState(s => s.phase === 'handout' && turnOf(s) === 'me'));
    render(<Game />);
    const first = within(hand()).getAllByRole('button')[0].getAttribute('aria-label')!;
    await user.click(within(hand()).getByRole('button', { name: first }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Give to Bot Basia' }));
    expect(within(hand()).queryByRole('button', { name: first })).toBeNull();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `Take back ${first}` }));
    expect(within(hand()).getByRole('button', { name: first })).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(within(hand()).getByRole('button', { name: first }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Give to Bot Basia' }));
    const second = within(hand()).getAllByRole('button')[0].getAttribute('aria-label')!;
    await user.click(within(hand()).getByRole('button', { name: second }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Give to Bot Zenek' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm hand-out' }));
    const action = conn.acts()[0].action as Extract<Action, { type: 'handout' }>;
    expect(cardLabel(action.to.b)).toBe(first);
    expect(cardLabel(action.to.z)).toBe(second);
  });

  it('menu opens the score sheet and settings without a name field', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    join(findState(s => s.phase === 'bidding'));
    render(<Game />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Score sheet' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Score sheet' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Close' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Menu' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Settings' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Settings' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Your name')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`src/game/selection.ts`:

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import type { Card, PlayerId } from '@tysiac/engine';

export interface SelectionState {
  /** cardKey of the raised card. */
  selected: string | null;
  /** Hand-out assignments (recipient → card). */
  given: Record<PlayerId, Card>;
  hint: string | null;
  select(key: string | null): void;
  give(p: PlayerId, card: Card): void;
  ungive(p: PlayerId): void;
  setHint(hint: string | null): void;
  reset(): void;
}

export const useSelection = create<SelectionState>()(set => ({
  selected: null,
  given: {},
  hint: null,
  select: key => set({ selected: key }),
  give: (p, card) => set(s => ({ given: { ...s.given, [p]: card }, selected: null })),
  ungive: p =>
    set(s => {
      const given = { ...s.given };
      delete given[p];
      return { given };
    }),
  setHint: hint => set({ hint }),
  reset: () => set({ selected: null, given: {}, hint: null }),
}));
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/screens/Game.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useLocation } from 'wouter';
import { useShallow } from 'zustand/react/shallow';
import { advise, cardEquals, cardKey, explainIllegal, type Card, type PlayerId } from '@tysiac/engine';
import { coachOn, useRoom } from '../store/room';
import { isIdle } from '../store/client';
import { useSelection } from '../game/selection';
import { useNow } from '../game/useNow';
import { TopBar } from '../game/TopBar';
import { Opponents } from '../game/Opponents';
import { Table } from '../game/Table';
import { Hand } from '../game/Hand';
import { ActionZone } from '../game/ActionZone';
import { WhySheet } from '../game/WhySheet';
import { ScoreSheet } from '../game/ScoreSheet';
import { RoundResultSheet } from '../game/RoundResultSheet';
import { GameOverSheet } from '../game/GameOverSheet';
import { MenuSheet } from './MenuSheet';
import { SettingsSheet } from './SettingsSheet';
import { RulesSheet } from './RulesSheet';
import './game.css';

type SheetName = 'menu' | 'score' | 'settings' | 'rules' | 'why';

export default function Game() {
  const s = useRoom(
    useShallow(st => ({
      me: st.me, room: st.room, view: st.view, shown: st.shown, queue: st.queue, holding: st.holding, sweepTo: st.sweepTo,
      pending: st.pending, inflight: st.inflight, shake: st.shake, toast: st.toast, roundResult: st.roundResult, note: st.note,
      clockOffset: st.clockOffset,
    })),
  );
  const sel = useSelection();
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState<SheetName | null>(null);
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const shown = s.shown;
  const idle = isIdle(s);
  const coach = coachOn(s);
  const anyAway = !!s.room?.seats.some(x => x && !x.isBot && !x.connected && !x.botFor);
  const now = useNow(anyAway, s.clockOffset);
  const phaseKey = shown ? `${shown.round?.number ?? 0}:${shown.phase}` : '';
  const advice = useMemo(() => (coach && idle && shown && shown.turn === shown.me && !shown.paused ? advise(shown) : null), [coach, idle, shown]);

  useEffect(() => {
    useSelection.getState().reset();
  }, [phaseKey]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!s.shake) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => useRoom.getState().clearShake(), 600);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [s.shake]);

  if (!shown) {
    return (
      <main className="game game--loading">
        <p>Dealing…</p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const room = useRoom.getState();
  const r = shown.round;
  const myTurn = idle && shown.turn === shown.me && !shown.paused;
  const givenCards = Object.values(sel.given);
  const hand = r ? r.hand.filter(c => !(s.pending && cardEquals(c, s.pending.card)) && !givenCards.some(g => cardEquals(g, c))) : [];
  const legalCards = shown.legal.flatMap(a => (a.type === 'card' ? [a.card] : []));
  const isLegal = (c: Card) => legalCards.some(l => cardEquals(l, c));
  const isPlayable = (c: Card) => shown.phase !== 'tricks' || !myTurn || isLegal(c);
  const selectedCard = hand.find(c => cardKey(c) === sel.selected) ?? null;
  const recommended = advice?.action.type === 'card' ? cardKey(advice.action.card) : null;
  const isHost = s.room?.hostId === s.me;

  const onCardTap = (card: Card) => {
    room.clearToast();
    const key = cardKey(card);
    if (shown.phase === 'tricks') {
      if (!myTurn) {
        sel.setHint(explainIllegal(shown, card) ?? 'One moment…');
        return;
      }
      if (!isLegal(card)) {
        sel.select(null);
        sel.setHint(explainIllegal(shown, card) ?? "You can't play that card now");
        return;
      }
      sel.setHint(null);
      sel.select(sel.selected === key ? null : key);
      return;
    }
    if (shown.phase === 'handout' && myTurn) {
      sel.setHint(null);
      sel.select(sel.selected === key ? null : key);
    }
  };
  const onPlay = (card: Card) => {
    room.playCard(card);
    sel.reset();
  };
  const onConfirmHandout = () => {
    room.act({ type: 'handout', to: { ...sel.given } });
    sel.reset();
  };
  const onTarget = (p: PlayerId) => {
    if (selectedCard) sel.give(p, selectedCard);
  };
  const leave = () => {
    room.detach();
    navigate('/');
  };
  const close = () => setSheet(null);

  return (
    <>
      <main className="game" data-phase={shown.phase} data-seq={s.view?.seq ?? 0}>
        <TopBar view={shown} onMenu={() => setSheet('menu')} onScores={() => setSheet('score')} />
        <Opponents
          view={shown}
          room={s.room}
          given={sel.given}
          targetable={myTurn && shown.phase === 'handout' && selectedCard !== null}
          onTarget={onTarget}
          onUngive={sel.ungive}
        />
        <Table view={shown} pending={s.pending?.card ?? null} sweepTo={s.sweepTo} />
        <ActionZone
          view={shown}
          room={s.room}
          idle={idle}
          busy={s.inflight !== null || s.pending !== null}
          selected={selectedCard}
          given={sel.given}
          hint={sel.hint ?? s.toast}
          coach={coach}
          advice={advice}
          note={s.note}
          now={now}
          onAct={a => room.act(a)}
          onPlay={onPlay}
          onConfirmHandout={onConfirmHandout}
          onBotFor={p => room.send({ t: 'botFor', playerId: p })}
          onWhy={() => setSheet('why')}
        />
        <Hand
          cards={hand}
          selected={sel.selected}
          isPlayable={isPlayable}
          recommended={recommended}
          declared={r?.marriages[shown.me] ?? []}
          shake={s.shake}
          onTap={onCardTap}
        />
      </main>
      {s.roundResult && (
        <RoundResultSheet summary={s.roundResult} view={shown} note={coach ? s.note : null} onContinue={() => room.dismissResult()} />
      )}
      {!s.roundResult && idle && shown.phase === 'gameEnd' && (
        <GameOverSheet view={shown} isHost={isHost} onRematch={() => room.send({ t: 'rematch' })} onHome={leave} />
      )}
      {sheet === 'score' && <ScoreSheet view={shown} onClose={close} />}
      {sheet === 'menu' && (
        <MenuSheet
          paused={!!shown.paused}
          onPause={() => {
            room.act({ type: 'pause' });
            close();
          }}
          onResume={() => {
            room.act({ type: 'resume' });
            close();
          }}
          onSettings={() => setSheet('settings')}
          onRules={() => setSheet('rules')}
          onLeave={leave}
          onClose={close}
        />
      )}
      {sheet === 'settings' && (
        <SettingsSheet
          allowProfile={false}
          coachOn={coach}
          onCoachChange={on => room.send({ t: 'setCoach', coach: on })}
          onRules={() => setSheet('rules')}
          onClose={close}
        />
      )}
      {sheet === 'rules' && <RulesSheet onClose={close} />}
      {sheet === 'why' && advice && <WhySheet advice={advice} onClose={close} />}
    </>
  );
}
```

### Task 15: Lobby — round table, clockwise seats, swap by tap or drag, first dealer, bots, share, start

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/src/screens/Lobby.tsx`, `apps/app/src/screens/lobby.css`
- Test: `apps/app/src/screens/Lobby.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useRoom` (Task 8); `LobbyOp`, `RoomInfo`, `RoomSeat` (protocol); `FakeConnection`, `roomInfo` (tests).
- Produces: `seatPosition(k: number, n: number): { left: string; top: string }` (k = clockwise steps from the viewer, viewer at the bottom); `export default function Lobby()` — room code, round table with seat buttons "Seat N: NAME" / "Seat N: empty" (`aria-pressed` when picked, "D" badge on the first dealer, draggable with `drag` + `dragSnapToOrigin`), "Tap two seats to swap…" hint, picked-seat actions "Make first dealer" (host) and "Remove bot" (host, bot seat), "Share link" (`navigator.share`, else clipboard → "Link copied"), "Add bot" (host, disabled when full), "Start game" (host, disabled until full) or "Waiting for the host to start…", "Leave" (sends `leave`, detaches, goes home).

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import Lobby, { seatPosition } from './Lobby';
import { useRoom } from '../store/room';
import { FakeConnection } from '../test/fakes';
import { roomInfo } from '../test/fixtures';
import type { ClientMessage, RoomInfo } from '../net/protocol';

function show(room: RoomInfo, me = 'me'): FakeConnection {
  const conn = new FakeConnection();
  useRoom.getState().attach(conn);
  conn.push({ t: 'welcome', you: me, room, serverTime: 0 });
  render(<Lobby />);
  return conn;
}
const lobbyOps = (conn: FakeConnection) => conn.sent.filter((m): m is Extract<ClientMessage, { t: 'lobby' }> => m.t === 'lobby').map(m => m.op);
const partial = (): RoomInfo => {
  const r = roomInfo({ status: 'lobby' });
  r.seats[2] = null;
  return r;
};

beforeEach(() => useRoom.getState().detach());
afterEach(() => {
  Reflect.deleteProperty(navigator, 'share');
});

describe('Lobby', () => {
  it('shows the code and every seat, empty ones too', () => {
    show(partial());
    expect(screen.getByText('ABCDEF')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat 1: Filip' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat 2: Bot Basia' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat 3: empty' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('tapping two seats swaps them', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const conn = show(partial());
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat 1: Filip' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat 1: Filip' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat 3: empty' }));
    expect(lobbyOps(conn)).toEqual([{ op: 'swapSeats', a: 0, b: 2 }]);
  });

  it('the host makes a picked seat the first dealer or removes a bot', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const conn = show(partial());
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat 2: Bot Basia' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Make first dealer' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat 2: Bot Basia' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove bot' }));
    expect(lobbyOps(conn)).toEqual([{ op: 'setFirstDealer', seat: 1 }, { op: 'removeBot', playerId: 'b' }]);
  });

  it('the host adds bots and starts once every seat is filled', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const conn = show(partial());
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start game' })).toBeDisabled();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add bot' }));
    expect(lobbyOps(conn)).toEqual([{ op: 'addBot' }]);
    conn.push({ t: 'room', room: roomInfo({ status: 'lobby' }) });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Add bot' })).toBeDisabled();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start game' }));
    expect(lobbyOps(conn).at(-1)).toEqual({ op: 'start' });
  });

  it('guests wait for the host', () => {
    show(roomInfo({ status: 'lobby', hostId: 'b' }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Add bot' })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Start game' })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByText('Waiting for the host to start…')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shares with the share sheet, or copies the link', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const share = vi.fn(async () => {});
    Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'share', { value: share, configurable: true });
    show(partial());
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Share link' }));
    expect(share).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ url: `${window.location.origin}/r/ABCDEF` }));
    Reflect.deleteProperty(navigator, 'share');
    const write = vi.spyOn(navigator.clipboard, 'writeText');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Share link' }));
    expect(write).toHaveBeenCalledWith(`${window.location.origin}/r/ABCDEF`);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Link copied')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('places the viewer at the bottom and goes clockwise', () => {
    expect(seatPosition(0, 3)).toEqual({ left: '50%', top: '88%' });
    const left = seatPosition(1, 3);
    expect(parseFloat(left.left)).toBeLessThan(50);
    const right = seatPosition(2, 3);
    expect(parseFloat(right.left)).toBeGreaterThan(50);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/screens/Lobby.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useLocation } from 'wouter';
import { m } from 'motion/react';
import type { LobbyOp, RoomSeat } from '../net/protocol';
import { useRoom } from '../store/room';
import './lobby.css';

/** Seat k steps clockwise from the viewer (viewer at the bottom), as % of the table box. */
export function seatPosition(k: number, n: number): { left: string; top: string } {
  const angle = ((90 + (k * 360) / n) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const round = (x: number) => Math.round(x * 10) / 10;
  return { left: `${round(50 + 42 * Math.cos(angle))}%`, top: `${round(50 + 38 * Math.sin(angle))}%` };
}

export default function Lobby() {
  const room = useRoom(s => s.room);
  const me = useRoom(s => s.me);
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [note, setNote] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  if (!room) return null;

  const send = useRoom.getState().send;
  const op = (o: LobbyOp) => send({ t: 'lobby', op: o });
  const isHost = room.hostId === me;
  const n = room.seats.length;
  const myIndex = Math.max(0, room.seats.findIndex(s => s?.id === me));
  const full = room.seats.every(Boolean);
  const pickedSeat = picked === null ? null : room.seats[picked];

  const tapSeat = (i: number) => {
    if (picked === null) return setPicked(i);
    if (picked === i) return setPicked(null);
    op({ op: 'swapSeats', a: picked, b: i });
    setPicked(null);
  };
  const dropOn = (from: number, x: number, y: number) => {
    if (typeof document.elementsFromPoint !== 'function') return;
    const target = document
      .elementsFromPoint(x, y)
      .map(el => el.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-seat-index]'))
      .find(el => el && Number(el.dataset.seatIndex) !== from);
    if (target) op({ op: 'swapSeats', a: from, b: Number(target.dataset.seatIndex) });
  };
  const share = async () => {
    const url = `${window.location.origin}/r/${room.code}`;
    try {
      if (typeof navigator.share === 'function') {
        await navigator.share({ title: 'Tysiąc', text: `Join my Tysiąc game (${room.code})`, url });
        return;
      }
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      setNote('Link copied');
    } catch {
      setNote(url);
    }
  };
  const leave = () => {
    send({ t: 'leave' });
    useRoom.getState().detach();
    navigate('/');
  };

  return (
    <main className="screen lobby">
      <header className="lobby__head">
        <button type="button" className="iconbtn" aria-label="Leave" onClick={leave}>
          ←
        </button>
        <div className="lobby__code">
          <small>Room code</small>
          <b className="display">{room.code}</b>
        </div>
      </header>
      <div className="lobby__table">
        <div className="lobby__felt">
          <span className="lobby__arrow">
            <b>↻</b>clockwise
          </span>
        </div>
        {room.seats.map((seat, i) => (
          <SeatButton
            key={seat?.id ?? `empty-${i}`}
            index={i}
            seat={seat}
            pos={seatPosition((i - myIndex + n) % n, n)}
            isMe={seat?.id === me}
            dealer={room.firstDealer === i}
            picked={picked === i}
            onTap={() => tapSeat(i)}
            onDrop={(x, y) => dropOn(i, x, y)}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
      <p className="lobby__hint">
        {picked === null
          ? 'Tap two seats to swap them (or drag) so the order matches where you sit. Play goes clockwise.'
          : 'Now tap another seat to swap.'}
      </p>
      {picked !== null && isHost && (
        <div className="row lobby__picked">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn btn--sec"
            onClick={() => {
              op({ op: 'setFirstDealer', seat: picked });
              setPicked(null);
            }}
          >
            Make first dealer
          </button>
          {pickedSeat?.isBot && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="btn btn--sec"
              onClick={() => {
                op({ op: 'removeBot', playerId: pickedSeat.id });
                setPicked(null);
              }}
            >
              Remove bot
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="lobby__actions">
        <div className="row">
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" onClick={() => void share()}>
            Share link
          </button>
          {isHost && (
            <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" disabled={full} onClick={() => op({ op: 'addBot' })}>
              Add bot
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        {note && (
          <p className="lobby__note" role="status">
            {note}
          </p>
        )}
        {isHost ? (
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" disabled={!full} onClick={() => op({ op: 'start' })}>
            Start game
          </button>
        ) : (
          <p className="lobby__note">Waiting for the host to start…</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

interface SeatButtonProps {
  index: number;
  seat: RoomSeat | null;
  pos: { left: string; top: string };
  isMe: boolean;
  dealer: boolean;
  picked: boolean;
  onTap(): void;
  onDrop(x: number, y: number): void;
}

function SeatButton({ index, seat, pos, isMe, dealer, picked, onTap, onDrop }: SeatButtonProps) {
  return (
    <m.button
      type="button"
      className={`seat ${picked ? 'seat--picked' : ''} ${seat ? '' : 'seat--empty'}`}
      style={pos}
      data-seat-index={index}
      aria-label={`Seat ${index + 1}: ${seat ? seat.name : 'empty'}`}
      aria-pressed={picked}
      drag={!!seat}
      dragSnapToOrigin
      dragMomentum={false}
      whileDrag={{ scale: 1.12, rotate: -4, zIndex: 5 }}
      onDragEnd={(_, info) => onDrop(info.point.x - window.scrollX, info.point.y - window.scrollY)}
      onClick={onTap}
    >
      <span className="avatar">
        {seat?.avatar ?? '+'}
        {dealer && <span className="avatar__dealer">D</span>}
      </span>
      <span>{seat ? (isMe ? 'You' : seat.name) : 'Empty'}</span>
      {seat && !seat.isBot && !seat.connected && <span className="badge">away</span>}
    </m.button>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the fake service** — append to `apps/app/src/test/fakes.ts`:

```ts
import type { CreateRoomRequest, CreateRoomResponse, RoomExistsResponse } from '../net/protocol';
import type { HelloMessage, RoomService } from '../net/connection';

export class FakeService implements RoomService {
  readonly kind = 'local' as const;
  created: CreateRoomRequest[] = [];
  conns: FakeConnection[] = [];
  hellos: (() => HelloMessage)[] = [];
  exists: Record<string, RoomExistsResponse> = { K7QX2M: { exists: true, status: 'lobby', maxPlayers: 3, seatsTaken: 1 } };

  async createRoom(req: CreateRoomRequest): Promise<CreateRoomResponse> {
    this.created.push(req);
    return { code: 'K7QX2M' };
  }
  async roomExists(code: string): Promise<RoomExistsResponse> {
    return this.exists[code] ?? { exists: false };
  }
  connect(_code: string, hello: () => HelloMessage): FakeConnection {
    const conn = new FakeConnection();
    this.conns.push(conn);
    this.hellos.push(hello);
    return conn;
  }
}
```

```tsx
describe('Home', () => {
```

```tsx
import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import RoomRoute from './RoomRoute';
import { setServiceForTests } from '../net/service';
import { usePrefs } from '../store/prefs';
import { useRoom } from '../store/room';
import { FakeService } from '../test/fakes';
import { roomInfo } from '../test/fixtures';
import type { ClientMessage } from '../net/protocol';

let svc: FakeService;
const renderRoute = (code = 'K7QX2M') =>
  render(
    <Suspense fallback={null}>
      <RoomRoute code={code} />
    </Suspense>,
  );
const lobbyOps = (msgs: ClientMessage[]) => msgs.flatMap(m => (m.t === 'lobby' ? [m.op] : []));

beforeEach(() => {
  svc = new FakeService();
  setServiceForTests(svc);
  useRoom.getState().detach();
  usePrefs.setState({ name: 'Filip', avatar: '🦊', lastRoom: null, gamesStarted: 0, coach: null, countedRoom: null });
});
afterEach(() => {
  useRoom.getState().detach();
  setServiceForTests(null);
});

describe('RoomRoute', () => {
  it('asks for a name before joining', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    usePrefs.setState({ name: '' });
    renderRoute();
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Join game K7QX2M' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(svc.conns).toHaveLength(0);
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Your name'), 'Kuba');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Join' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(svc.conns).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(svc.hellos[0]()).toMatchObject({ t: 'hello', v: 1, name: 'Kuba', avatar: '🦊', coach: true });
  });

  it('rejects a malformed code without connecting', () => {
    renderRoute('nope');
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Bad room code' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(svc.conns).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('practice rooms add two bots and start', async () => {
    usePrefs.setState({ lastRoom: { code: 'K7QX2M', practice: true, at: Date.now() } });
    renderRoute();
    await waitFor(() => expect(svc.conns).toHaveLength(1));
    const conn = svc.conns[0];
    const lobby = roomInfo({ code: 'K7QX2M', status: 'lobby' });
    lobby.seats[1] = null;
    lobby.seats[2] = null;
    conn.push({ t: 'welcome', you: 'me', room: lobby, serverTime: 0 });
    await waitFor(() => expect(lobbyOps(conn.sent)).toEqual([{ op: 'addBot' }, { op: 'addBot' }]));
    const half = roomInfo({ code: 'K7QX2M', status: 'lobby' });
    half.seats[2] = null;
    conn.push({ t: 'room', room: half });
    conn.push({ t: 'room', room: roomInfo({ code: 'K7QX2M', status: 'lobby' }) });
    await waitFor(() => expect(lobbyOps(conn.sent)).toEqual([{ op: 'addBot' }, { op: 'addBot' }, { op: 'start' }]));
    expect(svc.hellos[0]().coach).toBe(true);
  });

  it('shows the lobby for a lobby room', async () => {
    renderRoute();
    await waitFor(() => expect(svc.conns).toHaveLength(1));
    svc.conns[0].push({ t: 'welcome', you: 'me', room: roomInfo({ code: 'K7QX2M', status: 'lobby' }), serverTime: 0 });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Room code')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('fatal join errors stop reconnecting and explain', async () => {
    renderRoute();
    await waitFor(() => expect(svc.conns).toHaveLength(1));
    const conn = svc.conns[0];
    conn.push({ t: 'error', code: 'roomFull', message: 'This room is full' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: "Can't join" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('This room is full.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Home' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(conn.status).toBe('closed');
  });

  it('shows a reconnecting banner', async () => {
    renderRoute();
    await waitFor(() => expect(svc.conns).toHaveLength(1));
    svc.conns[0].push({ t: 'welcome', you: 'me', room: roomInfo({ code: 'K7QX2M', status: 'lobby' }), serverTime: 0 });
    svc.conns[0].setStatus('reconnecting');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Reconnecting…')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation } from 'wouter';
import { getService } from '../net/service';
import { normalizeCode } from '../net/connection';
import { getIdentity } from '../net/identity';
import { coachEnabled, usePrefs } from '../store/prefs';
import { ProfileForm } from '../ui/ProfileForm';
import { SettingsSheet } from './SettingsSheet';
import { RulesSheet } from './RulesSheet';
import './home.css';

const RESUME_WINDOW_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

function useResume(): string | null {
  const last = usePrefs(s => s.lastRoom);
  const [code, setCode] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!last || Date.now() - last.at > RESUME_WINDOW_MS) return;
    let alive = true;
    getService()
      .then(svc => svc.roomExists(last.code))
      .then(r => {
        if (alive && r.exists && r.status !== 'finished') setCode(last.code);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [last]);
  return code;
}

export default function Home() {
  const p = usePrefs();
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState<'settings' | 'rules' | null>(null);
  const resume = useResume();

  if (!p.name) {
    return (
      <main className="screen home">
        <h1 className="display home__brand">Tysiąc</h1>
        <p>Welcome! Pick a name and an avatar — friends will see them at the table.</p>
        <ProfileForm initialName="" initialAvatar={p.avatar} submitLabel="Continue" onSubmit={(n, a) => p.setProfile(n, a)} />
      </main>
    );
  }

  const create = async (maxPlayers: 3 | 4, practice: boolean) => {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const svc = await getService();
      const me = getIdentity();
      const res = await svc.createRoom({ maxPlayers, playerId: me.playerId, name: p.name, avatar: p.avatar });
      p.setLastRoom({ code: res.code, practice, at: Date.now() });
      navigate(`/r/${res.code}`);
    } catch {
      setError("Couldn't create a room. Check your connection and try again.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const join = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const c = normalizeCode(code);
    if (!c) {
      setError('A room code has 6 letters or digits.');
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const r = await (await getService()).roomExists(c);
      if (r.exists) navigate(`/r/${c}`);
      else setError(`No room with code ${c}.`);
    } catch {
      setError("Couldn't reach the server. Try again.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="screen home">
      <h1 className="display home__brand">Tysiąc</h1>
      <p className="home__hello">
        Hi {p.avatar} {p.name}
      </p>
      {resume && (
        <div className="panel home__resume">
          <span>Game {resume} is still on.</span>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" onClick={() => navigate(`/r/${resume}`)}>
            Resume
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      <section>
        <div className="lbl">New game</div>
        <div className="row">
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void create(3, false)}>
            3 players
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void create(4, false)}>
            4 players
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>
      <form className="home__join" onSubmit={e => void join(e)}>
        <label className="field">
          <span className="lbl">Join with code</span>
          <input
            value={code}
            onChange={e => setCode(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
            autoCapitalize="characters"
            autoComplete="off"
            spellCheck={false}
            maxLength={6}
            placeholder="K7QX2M"
            enterKeyHint="go"
          />
        </label>
        <button type="submit" className="btn btn--sec" disabled={busy}>
          Join
        </button>
      </form>
      <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void create(3, true)}>
        Practice vs bots
      </button>
      <div className="row">
        <Link href="/learn" className="btn btn--sec">
          Learn to play
        </Link>
        <Link href="/history" className="btn btn--sec">
          History
        </Link>
      </div>
      <button type="button" className="btn btn--sec" onClick={() => setSheet('settings')}>
        Settings
      </button>
      {error && (
        <p className="home__error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      {sheet === 'settings' && (
        <SettingsSheet allowProfile coachOn={coachEnabled(p, false)} onRules={() => setSheet('rules')} onClose={() => setSheet(null)} />
      )}
      {sheet === 'rules' && <RulesSheet onClose={() => setSheet(null)} />}
    </main>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `src/screens/RoomRoute.tsx`**

```tsx
import { lazy, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useLocation } from 'wouter';
import { PROTOCOL_VERSION } from '../net/protocol';
import { FATAL_ERRORS, normalizeCode } from '../net/connection';
import { getService } from '../net/service';
import { getIdentity } from '../net/identity';
import { coachEnabled, usePrefs } from '../store/prefs';
import { useRoom } from '../store/room';
import { ProfileForm } from '../ui/ProfileForm';
import { Placeholder } from './Placeholder';
import './home.css';

const Lobby = lazy(() => import('./Lobby'));
const Game = lazy(() => import('./Game'));

const FATAL_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {
  roomFull: 'This room is full.',
  gameInProgress: 'This game has already started without you.',
  badSecret: 'This seat belongs to another device.',
  versionMismatch: 'The app was updated — please reload.',
  notInRoom: 'That room does not exist (or has expired).',
};

/** Practice rooms: the host fills empty seats with bots and starts as soon as the table is full. */
export function usePracticeAutostart(enabled: boolean): void {
  const room = useRoom(s => s.room);
  const me = useRoom(s => s.me);
  const asked = useRef(0);
  const started = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled || !room || room.status !== 'lobby' || room.hostId !== me) return;
    const send = useRoom.getState().send;
    const missing = room.seats.filter(s => s === null).length;
    if (missing > 0) {
      if (asked.current < room.maxPlayers - 1) {
        for (let i = 0; i < missing; i++) send({ t: 'lobby', op: { op: 'addBot' } });
        asked.current += missing;
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!started.current) {
      started.current = true;
      send({ t: 'lobby', op: { op: 'start' } });
    }
  }, [enabled, room, me]);
}

export default function RoomRoute({ code }: { code: string }) {
  const norm = normalizeCode(code);
  const name = usePrefs(s => s.name);
  const avatar = usePrefs(s => s.avatar);
  const setProfile = usePrefs(s => s.setProfile);
  const practice = usePrefs(s => !!norm && s.lastRoom !== null && s.lastRoom.code === norm && s.lastRoom.practice);
  const room = useRoom(s => s.room);
  const view = useRoom(s => s.view);
  const status = useRoom(s => s.status);
  const error = useRoom(s => s.error);
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!norm || !name) return;
    let alive = true;
    void getService().then(svc => {
      if (!alive) return;
      const conn = svc.connect(norm, () => {
        const p = usePrefs.getState();
        const me = getIdentity();
        return { t: 'hello', v: PROTOCOL_VERSION, playerId: me.playerId, secret: me.secret, name: p.name, avatar: p.avatar, coach: coachEnabled(p, practice) };
      });
      useRoom.getState().attach(conn);
      usePrefs.getState().setLastRoom({ code: norm, practice, at: Date.now() });
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      useRoom.getState().detach();
    };
  }, [norm, name, practice]);

  usePracticeAutostart(practice);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (norm && room?.status === 'playing') usePrefs.getState().noteGameStarted(norm);
  }, [norm, room?.status]);

  if (!norm) return <Placeholder title="Bad room code" text="Room codes have 6 letters or digits." back />;
  if (!name) {
    return (
      <main className="screen">
        <h1 className="display">Join game {norm}</h1>
        <ProfileForm initialName="" initialAvatar={avatar} submitLabel="Join" onSubmit={setProfile} />
      </main>
    );
  }
  if (error && FATAL_ERRORS.has(error.code)) {
    return (
      <main className="screen">
        <h1 className="display">Can't join</h1>
        <p>{FATAL_TEXT[error.code] ?? error.message}</p>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn--pri" onClick={() => navigate('/')}>
          Home
        </button>
      </main>
    );
  }
  return (
    <>
      {status === 'reconnecting' && (
        <div className="netbanner" role="status">
          Reconnecting…
        </div>
      )}
      {!room ? (
        <Placeholder title={norm} text="Connecting…" />
      ) : room.status === 'lobby' ? (
        <Lobby />
      ) : view ? (
        <Game />
      ) : (
        <Placeholder title={norm} text="Dealing…" />
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Suspense, lazy } from 'react';
import { LazyMotion, MotionConfig } from 'motion/react';
import { Route, Switch } from 'wouter';
import Home from './screens/Home';
import { Placeholder } from './screens/Placeholder';

const RoomRoute = lazy(() => import('./screens/RoomRoute'));
const loadFeatures = () => import('./game/motionFeatures').then(m => m.default);

export function App() {
  return (
    <LazyMotion features={loadFeatures} strict>
      <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">
        <Suspense fallback={<Placeholder title="Tysiąc" text="Loading…" />}>
          <Switch>
            <Route path="/" component={Home} />
            <Route path="/r/:code">{params => <RoomRoute code={params.code} />}</Route>
            <Route path="/learn">
              <Placeholder title="Learn to play" text="The interactive lesson is coming soon. Until then, try Practice vs bots — the coach is on." back />
            </Route>
            <Route path="/history">
              <Placeholder title="History" text="Your finished games will show up here soon." back />
            </Route>
            <Route>
              <Placeholder title="Not found" text="That page does not exist." back />
            </Route>
          </Switch>
        </Suspense>
      </MotionConfig>
    </LazyMotion>
  );
}
```

### Task 17: Sounds — Kenney "Casino Audio" samples with a WebAudio fallback

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/scripts/fetch-sounds.sh`, `apps/app/src/audio/sounds.ts`, `apps/app/public/sounds/` (`card.m4a`, `shuffle.m4a`, `sweep.m4a`, `turn.m4a`, `LICENSE-kenney-casino-audio.txt`)
- Modify: `apps/app/src/screens/RoomRoute.tsx` (install sounds once)
- Test: `apps/app/src/audio/sounds.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SoundName` (Task 7); `setSoundSink` (Task 8); `usePrefs` (Task 3).
- Produces: `SOUND_FILES: Record<SoundName, string>` (`/sounds/<name>.m4a`); `type AudioContextLike`; `class SoundPlayer { constructor(enabled: () => boolean, makeCtx?: () => AudioContextLike | null, fetcher?: (url: string) => Promise<Response>); unlock(): void; preload(): Promise<void>; play(name: SoundName): void }` — decoded samples when available, otherwise a short synthesized blip (so a failed download never breaks sound); `installSounds(): SoundPlayer` (idempotent; unlocks audio on the first `pointerdown`/`keydown`, registers itself as the room store's sound sink). Lives in the lazy room chunk, not the initial bundle.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the download script** — `apps/app/scripts/fetch-sounds.sh`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Downloads Kenney "Casino Audio" (CC0, https://kenney.nl/assets/casino-audio) and converts four samples
# to AAC (.m4a — plays in iOS Safari). Run from anywhere; writes apps/app/public/sounds.
set -euo pipefail
APP="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
OUT="$APP/public/sounds"
FALLBACK_URL="https://kenney.nl/media/pages/assets/casino-audio/2472606a04-1721639069/kenney_casino-audio.zip"
URL="$(curl -fsSL https://kenney.nl/assets/casino-audio | grep -oE 'https://kenney.nl/media/pages/assets/casino-audio/[^"'"'"' ]+\.zip' | head -1 || true)"
URL="${URL:-$FALLBACK_URL}"
if ! command -v ffmpeg >/dev/null; then
  echo "ffmpeg not found — skipping samples; the app will use synthesized sounds." >&2
  exit 0
fi
TMP="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf "$TMP"' EXIT
curl -fsSL "$URL" -o "$TMP/kenney.zip"
mkdir "$TMP/x"
unzip -q "$TMP/kenney.zip" -d "$TMP/x"
mkdir -p "$OUT"
convert() { ffmpeg -loglevel error -y -i "$TMP/x/Audio/$1" -ac 1 -c:a aac -b:a 64k "$OUT/$2.m4a"; }
convert card-place-1.ogg card
convert card-shuffle.ogg shuffle
convert card-slide-3.ogg sweep
convert chip-lay-1.ogg turn
cp "$TMP/x/License.txt" "$OUT/LICENSE-kenney-casino-audio.txt"
ls -l "$OUT"
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { SOUND_FILES, SoundPlayer, type AudioContextLike } from './sounds';

function fakeCtx() {
  const started: string[] = [];
  const raw = {
    state: 'suspended' as AudioContextState,
    currentTime: 0,
    destination: {} as AudioDestinationNode,
    resume: vi.fn(async () => {
      raw.state = 'running';
    }),
    decodeAudioData: vi.fn(async () => ({ duration: 0.2 }) as AudioBuffer),
    createBufferSource: vi.fn(() => ({ buffer: null as AudioBuffer | null, connect: vi.fn(), start: () => started.push('sample') })),
    createGain: vi.fn(() => ({ gain: { value: 1, setValueAtTime: vi.fn(), exponentialRampToValueAtTime: vi.fn() }, connect: vi.fn() })),
    createOscillator: vi.fn(() => ({ type: 'sine', frequency: { value: 0 }, connect: vi.fn(), start: () => started.push('synth'), stop: vi.fn() })),
  };
  return { ctx: raw as unknown as AudioContextLike, raw, started };
}

describe('SoundPlayer', () => {
  it('plays the decoded sample once preloaded', async () => {
    const { ctx, started } = fakeCtx();
    const fetcher = vi.fn(async () => new Response(new ArrayBuffer(8)));
    const p = new SoundPlayer(() => true, () => ctx, fetcher);
    await p.preload();
    expect(fetcher).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(Object.keys(SOUND_FILES).length);
    expect(fetcher).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/sounds/card.m4a');
    p.play('card');
    expect(started).toEqual(['sample']);
  });

  it('falls back to a synthesized sound when a file is missing', async () => {
    const { ctx, started } = fakeCtx();
    const p = new SoundPlayer(() => true, () => ctx, async () => new Response('', { status: 404 }));
    await p.preload();
    p.play('turn');
    expect(started.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(started.every(s => s === 'synth')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('is silent when sound is off', async () => {
    const { ctx, started } = fakeCtx();
    const p = new SoundPlayer(() => false, () => ctx, async () => new Response(new ArrayBuffer(8)));
    await p.preload();
    p.play('card');
    expect(started).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('unlock resumes a suspended context', () => {
    const { ctx, raw } = fakeCtx();
    const p = new SoundPlayer(() => true, () => ctx, async () => new Response(new ArrayBuffer(8)));
    p.unlock();
    expect(raw.resume).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('does nothing without Web Audio', () => {
    const p = new SoundPlayer(() => true, () => null, async () => new Response(new ArrayBuffer(8)));
    expect(() => p.play('card')).not.toThrow();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/audio/sounds.ts`**

```ts
import type { SoundName } from '../store/client';
import { setSoundSink } from '../store/room';
import { usePrefs } from '../store/prefs';

export const SOUND_FILES: Record<SoundName, string> = {
  card: '/sounds/card.m4a',
  shuffle: '/sounds/shuffle.m4a',
  sweep: '/sounds/sweep.m4a',
  turn: '/sounds/turn.m4a',
};

export type AudioContextLike = Pick<
  AudioContext,
  'state' | 'resume' | 'currentTime' | 'destination' | 'createBufferSource' | 'createGain' | 'createOscillator' | 'decodeAudioData'
>;

interface Synth {
  freqs: number[];
  dur: number;
  type: OscillatorType;
  vol: number;
}

const SYNTH: Record<SoundName, Synth> = {
  card: { freqs: [180], dur: 0.06, type: 'triangle', vol: 0.18 },
  shuffle: { freqs: [140, 190, 160], dur: 0.12, type: 'sawtooth', vol: 0.05 },
  sweep: { freqs: [320, 240], dur: 0.14, type: 'triangle', vol: 0.08 },
  turn: { freqs: [660, 880], dur: 0.18, type: 'sine', vol: 0.1 },
};

function defaultContext(): AudioContextLike | null {
  const w = window as unknown as { AudioContext?: typeof AudioContext; webkitAudioContext?: typeof AudioContext };
  const C = w.AudioContext ?? w.webkitAudioContext;
  return C ? new C() : null;
}

export class SoundPlayer {
  private ctx: AudioContextLike | null | undefined;
  private readonly buffers = new Map<SoundName, AudioBuffer | 'missing'>();
  private loading: Promise<void> | null = null;

  constructor(
    private readonly enabled: () => boolean,
    private readonly makeCtx: () => AudioContextLike | null = defaultContext,
    private readonly fetcher: (url: string) => Promise<Response> = url => fetch(url),
  ) {}

  /** Call from a user gesture: iOS only starts audio after one. */
  unlock(): void {
    const ctx = this.context();
    if (!ctx) return;
    if (ctx.state === 'suspended') void ctx.resume();
    void this.preload();
  }

  preload(): Promise<void> {
    const ctx = this.context();
    if (!ctx) return Promise.resolve();
    this.loading ??= Promise.all(
      (Object.keys(SOUND_FILES) as SoundName[]).map(async name => {
        try {
          const res = await this.fetcher(SOUND_FILES[name]);
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
          this.buffers.set(name, await ctx.decodeAudioData(await res.arrayBuffer()));
        } catch {
          this.buffers.set(name, 'missing');
        }
      }),
    ).then(() => undefined);
    return this.loading;
  }

  play(name: SoundName): void {
    if (!this.enabled()) return;
    const ctx = this.context();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const buffer = this.buffers.get(name);
    if (buffer && buffer !== 'missing') {
      const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      gain.gain.value = 0.7;
      src.buffer = buffer;
      src.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      src.start();
      return;
    }
    this.synth(ctx, SYNTH[name]);
  }

  private synth(ctx: AudioContextLike, s: Synth): void {
    s.freqs.forEach((f, i) => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      const t = ctx.currentTime + i * s.dur * 0.6;
      osc.type = s.type;
      osc.frequency.value = f;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(s.vol, t);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + s.dur);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start(t);
      osc.stop(t + s.dur);
    });
  }

  private context(): AudioContextLike | null {
    if (this.ctx === undefined) {
      try {
        this.ctx = this.makeCtx();
      } catch {
        this.ctx = null;
      }
    }
    return this.ctx;
  }
}

let installed: SoundPlayer | null = null;

export function installSounds(): SoundPlayer {
  if (installed) return installed;
  const player = new SoundPlayer(() => usePrefs.getState().sound);
  const unlock = () => player.unlock();
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown', unlock, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('keydown', unlock);
  setSoundSink(name => player.play(name));
  installed = player;
  return player;
}
```

### Task 18: PWA (manifest, icons, service worker) and the 120 KB initial-JS budget

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/public/icon.svg`, `apps/app/pwa-assets.config.ts`, `apps/app/scripts/check-budget.mjs`, `apps/app/scripts/check-pwa.mjs`, generated `apps/app/public/{pwa-64x64.png,pwa-192x192.png,pwa-512x512.png,maskable-icon-512x512.png,apple-touch-icon-180x180.png,favicon.ico}`
- Modify: `apps/app/vite.config.ts`, `apps/app/index.html`, `apps/app/src/main.tsx`, `apps/app/tsconfig.json`, `apps/app/package.json` (`build` script)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the built app.
- Produces: `dist/manifest.webmanifest` (standalone, theme colours, icons incl. maskable), `dist/sw.js` precaching the app shell, fonts (`.woff2`) and sounds (`.m4a`) with SPA navigation fallback (except `/api/` and `/ws/`), apple-touch-icon + `apple-mobile-web-app-capable` meta; `pnpm build` fails when initial JS > 120 KB gzip or a PWA requirement is missing.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the checks (they fail now)**

`apps/app/scripts/check-pwa.mjs`:

```js
// Fails the build when the PWA shell is incomplete.
import { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const dist = fileURLToPath(new URL('../dist/', import.meta.url));
const fail = msg => {
  console.error(`PWA check failed: ${msg}`);
  process.exit(1);
};
const manifestPath = join(dist, 'manifest.webmanifest');
if (!existsSync(manifestPath)) fail('dist/manifest.webmanifest is missing');
const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, 'utf8'));
if (manifest.display !== 'standalone') fail('manifest.display must be "standalone"');
if (!manifest.icons?.some(i => i.sizes === '512x512' && i.purpose === 'maskable')) fail('a maskable 512×512 icon is required');
if (!existsSync(join(dist, 'sw.js'))) fail('dist/sw.js is missing');
const sw = readFileSync(join(dist, 'sw.js'), 'utf8');
for (const needle of ['index.html', '.woff2']) if (!sw.includes(needle)) fail(`the service worker does not precache ${needle}`);
const html = readFileSync(join(dist, 'index.html'), 'utf8');
for (const needle of ['apple-touch-icon', 'apple-mobile-web-app-capable', 'manifest.webmanifest', 'viewport-fit=cover']) {
  if (!html.includes(needle)) fail(`index.html lacks ${needle}`);
}
if (!existsSync(join(dist, 'apple-touch-icon-180x180.png'))) fail('apple-touch-icon-180x180.png is missing');
const soundsDir = join(dist, 'sounds');
const sounds = existsSync(soundsDir) ? readdirSync(soundsDir).filter(f => f.endsWith('.m4a')) : [];
if (sounds.length > 0 && !sw.includes('sounds/')) fail('sounds are not precached');
console.log(`PWA check OK (${sounds.length} sounds precached)`);
```

`apps/app/scripts/check-budget.mjs`:

```js
// Initial JS = the entry script + every chunk it modulepreloads. Must stay <= 120 KB gzip (spec §7).
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { gzipSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const LIMIT = 120 * 1024;
const dist = fileURLToPath(new URL('../dist/', import.meta.url));
const html = readFileSync(join(dist, 'index.html'), 'utf8');
const files = new Set([
  ...[...html.matchAll(/<script[^>]+src="\/([^"]+\.js)"/g)].map(m => m[1]),
  ...[...html.matchAll(/<link[^>]+rel="modulepreload"[^>]+href="\/([^"]+\.js)"/g)].map(m => m[1]),
]);
let total = 0;
for (const f of files) {
  const size = gzipSync(readFileSync(join(dist, f))).length;
  total += size;
  console.log(`${f}: ${(size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB gzip`);
}
console.log(`initial JS: ${(total / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB gzip (limit ${LIMIT / 1024} KB)`);
if (files.size === 0) {
  console.error('No entry script found in dist/index.html');
  process.exit(1);
}
if (total > LIMIT) {
  console.error('Initial JS budget exceeded');
  process.exit(1);
}
```

In `apps/app/package.json` set:

```json
    "build": "tsc --noEmit && vite build && node scripts/check-budget.mjs && node scripts/check-pwa.mjs",
```

### Task 19: Playwright WebKit e2e — full games vs bots on five viewports, layout and keyboard checks

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/app/playwright.config.ts`, `apps/app/e2e/layout.ts`, `apps/app/e2e/game.spec.ts`, `apps/app/e2e/lobby.spec.ts`
- Modify: `.gitignore` (root: add `apps/app/test-results/` and `apps/app/playwright-report/`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the built app served by `vite preview` on port 4173 in local fast mode (`/?local=1&fast=1`); accessible names and `data-phase` / `data-seq` attributes from earlier tasks.
- Produces: `layoutProblems(page, { allowScroll? }): Promise<string[]>`; `expectFits(page, label, opts?)` (polls until no element is outside the viewport, the document does not scroll, and `scrollY === 0`; `allowScroll` relaxes the vertical checks for Home/Lobby); e2e projects `se` 375×667, `iphone15` 393×852, `promax` 440×956, `landscape` 852×393, `desktop` 1280×800.

- [ ] **Step 1: Install WebKit for Playwright**

Run: `cd /Users/filip/Code/tysiac-game/apps/app && pnpm exec playwright install webkit`

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `apps/app/playwright.config.ts`**

```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

const VIEWPORTS = [
  { name: 'se', width: 375, height: 667, mobile: true },
  { name: 'iphone15', width: 393, height: 852, mobile: true },
  { name: 'promax', width: 440, height: 956, mobile: true },
  { name: 'landscape', width: 852, height: 393, mobile: true },
  { name: 'desktop', width: 1280, height: 800, mobile: false },
];

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: 'e2e',
  timeout: 10 * 60_000,
  expect: { timeout: 10_000 },
  fullyParallel: true,
  retries: 0,
  reporter: 'list',
  use: {
    baseURL: 'http://localhost:4173',
    serviceWorkers: 'block',
    actionTimeout: 10_000,
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
  },
  webServer: {
    command: 'pnpm build && pnpm preview',
    url: 'http://localhost:4173',
    reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,
    timeout: 240_000,
  },
  projects: VIEWPORTS.map(v => ({
    name: v.name,
    use: {
      ...devices['Desktop Safari'],
      browserName: 'webkit' as const,
      viewport: { width: v.width, height: v.height },
      isMobile: v.mobile,
      hasTouch: v.mobile,
      deviceScaleFactor: 2,
    },
  })),
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `apps/app/e2e/layout.ts`**

```ts
import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

export interface FitOptions {
  /** Home and Lobby scroll normally: only check sideways overflow. */
  allowScroll?: boolean;
}

/** Elements outside the viewport, and document scrolling, as human-readable problems. */
export async function layoutProblems(page: Page, opts: FitOptions = {}): Promise<string[]> {
  return page.evaluate(({ allowScroll }) => {
    const out: string[] = [];
    const vw = window.innerWidth;
    const vh = window.innerHeight;
    const doc = document.documentElement;
    if (doc.scrollWidth > vw + 1) out.push(`page scrolls sideways (${doc.scrollWidth} > ${vw})`);
    if (!allowScroll) {
      if (doc.scrollHeight > vh + 1) out.push(`page scrolls (${doc.scrollHeight} > ${vh})`);
      if (window.scrollY !== 0) out.push(`scrollY is ${window.scrollY}`);
    }
    for (const el of Array.from(document.body.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('*'))) {
      if (el.parentElement?.closest('[data-scroll-clip]')) continue;
      const style = getComputedStyle(el);
      if (style.display === 'none' || style.visibility === 'hidden' || Number(style.opacity) === 0) continue;
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.width === 0 || r.height === 0) continue;
      const sideways = r.left < -1 || r.right > vw + 1;
      const vertical = !allowScroll && (r.top < -1 || r.bottom > vh + 1);
      if (sideways || vertical) {
        out.push(`<${el.tagName.toLowerCase()} class="${String(el.className)}"> at ${Math.round(r.left)},${Math.round(r.top)} size ${Math.round(r.width)}×${Math.round(r.height)}`);
      }
    }
    return out.slice(0, 8);
  }, { allowScroll: !!opts.allowScroll });
}

export async function expectFits(page: Page, label: string, opts: FitOptions = {}): Promise<void> {
  await expect.poll(() => layoutProblems(page, opts), { message: `layout problems at "${label}"`, timeout: 5000 }).toEqual([]);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `apps/app/e2e/game.spec.ts`**

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { expectFits } from './layout';

async function firstLaunch(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  await page.goto('/?local=1&fast=1');
  const name = page.getByLabel('Your name');
  await name.focus();
  await name.fill('Tester');
  await name.blur();
  await page.waitForTimeout(200);
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollY)).toBe(0);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Avatar 🐻' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Practice vs bots' })).toBeVisible();
}

/** Runs a move and waits until the server's next state arrives (data-seq changes). */
async function move(page: Page, run: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {
  const game = page.locator('.game');
  const before = (await game.getAttribute('data-seq')) ?? '';
  await run();
  await expect(game).not.toHaveAttribute('data-seq', before);
}

async function handOut(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  const hand = page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Your hand' });
  for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
    await hand.getByRole('button').first().click();
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Give to / }).first().click();
  }
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm hand-out' }).click();
}

async function playLegalCard(page: Page, checkTapDoesNotPlay: boolean): Promise<void> {
  const hand = page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Your hand' });
  const card = hand.locator('button:not([aria-disabled="true"])').first();
  const label = (await card.getAttribute('aria-label'))!;
  await card.click();
  if (checkTapDoesNotPlay) {
    await page.waitForTimeout(300);
    await expect(hand.getByRole('button', { name: label })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  }
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: `Play ${label}` }).click();
}

test('focusing and blurring the name input leaves the page at the top', async ({ page }) => {
  await firstLaunch(page);
  await expectFits(page, 'home', { allowScroll: true });
});

test('plays a full practice game against bots with nothing off-screen', async ({ page }) => {
  await firstLaunch(page);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Practice vs bots' }).click();
  const zone = page.locator('.zone');
  await expect(zone).toBeVisible({ timeout: 20_000 });
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const check = async (key: string) => {
    if (seen.has(key)) return;
    seen.add(key);
    await expectFits(page, key);
  };
  let tapChecked = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < 6000; i++) {
    if (await page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Game over' }).isVisible()) break;
    const cont = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue' });
    if (await cont.isVisible()) {
      await check('round result');
      await cont.click();
      continue;
    }
    const phase = await zone.getAttribute('data-phase');
    if (phase === 'bidding') {
      await check('bidding');
      const bid = page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Bid (110|120)$/ });
      await move(page, async () => {
        if (await bid.isVisible()) await bid.click();
        else await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Pass' }).click();
      });
    } else if (phase === 'decision') {
      await check('decision');
      await move(page, () => page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Play', exact: true }).click());
    } else if (phase === 'handout') {
      await check('handout');
      await move(page, () => handOut(page));
    } else if (phase === 'tricks') {
      await check('tricks');
      await move(page, () => playLegalCard(page, !tapChecked));
      tapChecked = true;
    } else {
      if (phase === 'wait') await check('waiting');
      await page.waitForTimeout(40);
    }
  }
  await expect(page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Game over' })).toBeVisible();
  await check('game over');
  for (const key of ['bidding', 'tricks', 'waiting', 'round result', 'game over']) expect(seen.has(key), `saw ${key}`).toBe(true);
});

test('the Wycinanki theme also fits', async ({ page }) => {
  await firstLaunch(page);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Settings' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('radio', { name: /Wycinanki/ }).click();
  await expect(page.locator('html')).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', 'wycinanki');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Close' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Practice vs bots' }).click();
  await expect(page.locator('.zone')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 20_000 });
  await expectFits(page, 'wycinanki game');
});
```

```ts
  await expect(page.locator('.game')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 20_000 });
  await expect(page.getByText('Bot Zenek deals · bidding')).toBeVisible();
  await expectFits(page, 'first bidding');
});
```

## Self-review notes (resolved ambiguities)

1. **Protocol package timing:** chosen option = *precondition*. M3 runs after `packages/protocol` exists; app code imports it only through `src/net/protocol.ts` (type re-exports + a local `PROTOCOL_VERSION` checked by a test) so zod never enters the bundle. If the package is missing, Task 1 stops with BLOCKED rather than creating it.
2. **`domAnimation` vs `layoutId`:** the spec asks for `LazyMotion + domAnimation` *and* shared `layoutId` card travel; `layoutId` and drag need `domMax`. The app uses `LazyMotion` with `domMax` loaded by dynamic import (`game/motionFeatures.ts`), which keeps the initial bundle small — the intent of the spec.
3. **Round end has no engine phase** (M1 dropped `roundEnd`): the round-result sheet is driven by the `roundScored`/`threw` events in the playback queue, which pauses until "Continue"; the game-over sheet appears when the shown phase is `gameEnd`.
4. **Animation model:** the server's latest `view` and a lagging `shown` view; queued events are applied to `shown` one by one (card flight 280 ms, trick hold 1000 ms, sweep 380 ms, ×0.5 on "Fast", flights skipped with reduced motion). Actions are offered only when idle (`isIdle`). A state with no events (reconnect/resync) replaces everything at once.
5. **Coach visibility:** the coach flag lives on the room seat (`hello.coach` / `setCoach`); the client shows `advise` and `explainEvent` only when the viewer's seat has coach on. Public notes (marriage → trump, wykładana, bidding won) show for everyone without the "Coach:" label.
6. **First dealer vs tap-to-swap in the lobby:** tapping a seat selects it; a second seat tap swaps; with a seat selected the host gets "Make first dealer" (and "Remove bot" for bots). Drag-and-drop also swaps.
7. **Opponent cards entering the table:** opponents' cards have no hand element to share a `layoutId` with, so they fly in from their side of the screen (variants keyed by seat spot) and exit toward the trick winner; the viewer's own cards use `layoutId` from the hand.
8. **Sounds:** Kenney files are `.ogg`; they are converted to AAC `.m4a` for iOS Safari by `scripts/fetch-sounds.sh` (ffmpeg). Mapping: card = `card-place-1`, shuffle = `card-shuffle`, sweep = `card-slide-3`, turn = `chip-lay-1`. Without ffmpeg/network the synthesized fallback is used.
9. **Rules page:** a sheet (from Settings or the in-game Menu) rather than a separate route; the spec's route list (`/`, `/r/:code`, `/history`, `/learn`) is unchanged.
10. **Settings inside the game** omit name/avatar editing so the Game screen never shows a text input.
11. **Router:** `wouter` (≈2 KB) instead of React Router to protect the 120 KB budget.
12. **TypeScript 5.9** (not 7.0) to match the engine's compiler major version in the workspace.
